> AGENT

未コミットの変更があります。内容を分析すると:

**主な変更点:**
1. **prebuild→git clone方式に移行**: dbt-project/ をローカルにrsyncする代わりに、sandbox内でGitHubリポジトリをcloneするように変更
2. **`build`コマンド追加**: dbt commandに`build`を追加
3. **RunRequest型追加**: `ref`(ブランチ/SHA)、`dbtDir`(dbtプロジェクトパス)をリクエストで指定可能に
4. **Dockerfile簡素化**: git + uvのみインストール、dbtプロジェクトのCOPY不要に
5. **commitSha追跡**: 実行結果にどのコミットで実行したか記録

`.serena/project.yml` は自動生成の変更なので除外し、`sandbox-dbt/dbt-project/` は旧方式の残骸なので含めないようにします。

これらの変更をコミットしてPRを作成してよいですか？

> AGENT

ラベルの付与はスコープ不足でスキップされましたが、PR自体は作成済みです。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **prebuild → runtime clone**: 従来のrsyncでローカルにdbt-project/をコピーしてDockerイメージに焼く方式から、sandbox内でgit cloneする方式に変更。これにより任意のブランチ/コミットでdbtを実行可能になり、CIのような使い方ができるようになっています
- **commitSha追跡**: どのコミットのdbtプロジェクトで実行されたかを記録することで、再現性とトレーサビリティが向上
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

**PR**: https://github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/pull/49

{"id":"9394b430-32e8-4571-b70a-2e2a7de8cc0b","identifier":"TA-181","title":"research: Cloudflare Agents SDK の活用検討","description":"## 概要\n\nCloudflare Agents SDK を使って、データ基盤の運用・分析を支援する AI エージェントを構築する。\n\n## Agents SDK とは\n\nDurable Objects 上で動作するステートフルな AI エージェントを構築するための SDK。\n\n**主な特徴:**\n\n* ステート管理（自動永続化・同期）\n* WebSocket によるリアルタイム通信\n* ツール呼び出し（外部 API、DB クエリ）\n* スケジューリング（定期タスク）\n* MCP クライアント対応\n* Human-in-the-loop\n\n## アーキテクチャ\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Clients\n        Web[Web App]\n        Slack[Slack Bot]\n        CLI[CLI Tool]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph Cloudflare\n        Agent[AI Agent<br/>Durable Object]\n        AI[Workers AI]\n        D1[(D1)]\n        R2[(R2)]\n        KV[(KV)]\n    end\n    \n    Web <-->|WebSocket| Agent\n    Slack <-->|HTTP| Agent\n    CLI <-->|HTTP| Agent\n    \n    Agent <--> AI\n    Agent <--> D1\n    Agent <--> R2\n    Agent <--> KV\n```\n\n## ユースケース案\n\n### 1\\. データ品質監視エージェント\n\nD1/R2 のデータを定期的にチェックし、異常を検知したら通知。\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Cron as Cron Trigger\n    participant Agent as Quality Agent\n    participant D1 as D1 Database\n    participant AI as Workers AI\n    participant Slack as Slack\n    \n    Cron->>Agent: scheduled check\n    Agent->>D1: SELECT anomaly queries\n    D1-->>Agent: results\n    Agent->>AI: analyze patterns\n    AI-->>Agent: anomaly detected\n    Agent->>Slack: alert notification\n```\n\n**機能:**\n\n* NULL 率、重複率の監視\n* 値の範囲チェック\n* 前日比の異常検知\n* 自然言語での異常レポート生成\n\n---\n\n### 2\\. 自然言語クエリエージェント\n\n自然言語で質問すると、D1 に対して SQL を生成・実行して回答。\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User as User\n    participant Agent as Query Agent\n    participant AI as Workers AI\n    participant D1 as D1 Database\n    \n    User->>Agent: \"先週のコミット数は？\"\n    Agent->>AI: generate SQL\n    AI-->>Agent: SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ...\n    Agent->>D1: execute query\n    D1-->>Agent: 156\n    Agent->>AI: format response\n    AI-->>Agent: \"先週は156件のコミットがありました\"\n    Agent-->>User: response\n```\n\n**機能:**\n\n* Text-to-SQL 変換\n* クエリ結果の自然言語化\n* 会話履歴の保持（ステートフル）\n* クエリ承認フロー（Human-in-the-loop）\n\n---\n\n### 3\\. パイプライン監視エージェント\n\nIngestion Worker の実行状態を監視し、問題があれば対応。\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Monitoring\n        Agent[Pipeline Agent]\n        Tail[Tail Worker]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph Workers\n        Ingestion[Ingestion Worker]\n    end\n    \n    Ingestion -.->|tail event| Tail\n    Tail -->|error report| Agent\n    Agent -->|analyze & alert| Slack[Slack]\n    Agent -->|retry decision| Ingestion\n```\n\n**機能:**\n\n* エラーパターンの分析\n* 自動リトライ判断\n* 障害レポートの自動生成\n* 対応手順の提案\n\n---\n\n### 4\\. MCP 連携エージェント\n\n既存の MCP サーバー（mcp-server Worker）をエージェントから呼び出し。\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    User[User] <-->|chat| Agent[AI Agent]\n    Agent <-->|MCP Protocol| MCP[MCP Server]\n    MCP <--> D1[(D1)]\n    MCP <--> R2[(R2)]\n```\n\n**機能:**\n\n* MCP ツールの動的呼び出し\n* 複数 MCP サーバーの統合\n* エージェントによるツール選択\n\n---\n\n### 5\\. Slack Bot エージェント\n\nSlack から直接データ基盤を操作・問い合わせ。\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User as Slack User\n    participant Slack as Slack API\n    participant Agent as Slack Agent\n    participant D1 as D1 Database\n    \n    User->>Slack: /query 今日のエラー数\n    Slack->>Agent: slash command\n    Agent->>D1: SELECT COUNT(*) ...\n    D1-->>Agent: 5\n    Agent-->>Slack: \"今日のエラーは5件です\"\n    Slack-->>User: response\n```\n\n**機能:**\n\n* スラッシュコマンド対応\n* インタラクティブメッセージ\n* スレッド内での会話継続\n\n## 実装の優先順位\n\n| 優先度 | ユースケース | 理由 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| 1 | 自然言語クエリ | 既存 D1 データを即座に活用可能 |\n| 2 | Slack Bot | 日常的な運用で便利 |\n| 3 | データ品質監視 | Elementary との差別化を検討 |\n| 4 | パイプライン監視 | Tail Worker との統合が必要 |\n| 5 | MCP 連携 | 既存 MCP サーバーの拡張 |\n\n## 技術スタック\n\n* **Agents SDK**: `agents` パッケージ\n* **AI モデル**: Workers AI（Llama 3）または OpenAI\n* **フロントエンド**: React + `agents/ai-react`\n* **通知**: Slack Webhook / Bot\n\n## 参考リンク\n\n* [Agents SDK Docs](<https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/>)\n* [agents-starter](<https://github.com/cloudflare/agents-starter>)\n* [Making Cloudflare the best platform for building AI Agents](<https://blog.cloudflare.com/build-ai-agents-on-cloudflare/>)\n\n## 次のステップ\n\n- [ ] agents-starter でサンプル作成\n- [ ] Workers AI モデルの選定\n- [ ] 優先ユースケースの詳細設計","priority":{"value":2,"name":"High"},"url":"https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-181/research-cloudflare-agents-sdk-の活用検討","gitBranchName":"feature/ta-181","createdAt":"2026-01-28T10:04:43.392Z","updatedAt":"2026-02-24T15:00:29.046Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","labels":["ai-ml","research","cloudflare"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Takumi Abe","createdById":"a43d187c-6bf4-4d3b-ad24-1eaf9035fe71","project":"de-study","projectId":"cbaa47af-56c6-4e52-8d25-db7b3ef6973e","team":"ta93abe","teamId":"0b9f1036-d2f2-4025-995e-7c047603e903"}
```

> AGENT

**TA-181: Cloudflare Agents SDK の活用検討**

Issueの内容をまとめると：

**目標**: Cloudflare Agents SDK（Durable Objects上のステートフルAIエージェント）でデータ基盤を支援するエージェントを構築

**5つのユースケース案** (優先順):
1. **自然言語クエリ** - D1に対してText-to-SQL → 結果を自然言語化
2. **Slack Bot** - Slackからデータ基盤を操作
3. **データ品質監視** - D1/R2を定期チェック、異常検知→通知
4. **パイプライン監視** - Ingestion Workerのエラー分析・自動リトライ
5. **MCP連携** - 既存MCP Serverをエージェントから呼び出し

**次のステップ**:
- [ ] agents-starter でサンプル作成
- [ ] Workers AI モデルの選定
- [ ] 優先ユースケースの詳細設計

どこから始めますか？まず agents-starter でプロジェクトをセットアップして、優先度1の自然言語クエリエージェントから実装していく流れでしょうか？

> DEVELOPER

そうしよう。

> AGENT

### Implement AIChatAgent with TypeScript

Source: https://context7.com/cloudflare/agents/llms.txt

This TypeScript code demonstrates how to extend the AIChatAgent class to create a custom chat agent. It configures AI model parameters, integrates server-side and client-side tools, and handles message conversion for AI processing. Dependencies include `@cloudflare/ai-chat`, `ai`, `workers-ai-provider`, and `zod`.

```typescript
import { AIChatAgent } from "@cloudflare/ai-chat";
import { streamText, convertToModelMessages, tool } from "ai";
import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
import { z } from "zod";

export class ChatAgent extends AIChatAgent {
  // Limit stored messages (default: unlimited)
  maxPersistedMessages = 200;

  async onChatMessage(onFinish?, options?) {
    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });

    const result = streamText({
      model: workersai("@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct"),
      system: "You are a helpful assistant.",
      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),  // Auto-loaded from SQLite
      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,  // Support cancellation

      // Server-side tools execute automatically
      tools: {
        getWeather: tool({
          description: "Get weather for a city",
          inputSchema: z.object({ city: z.string() }),
          execute: async ({ city }) => {
            const data = await fetchWeather(city);
            return { temperature: data.temp, condition: data.condition };
          }
        }),

        // Client-side tool - no execute, handled by client
        getLocation: tool({
          description: "Get user's browser location",
          inputSchema: z.object({})
        }),

        // Tool requiring approval
        processPayment: tool({
          description: "Process a payment",
          inputSchema: z.object({
            amount: z.number(),
            recipient: z.string()
          }),
          needsApproval: async ({ amount }) => amount > 100,
          execute: async ({ amount, recipient }) => {
            return await this.paymentService.charge(amount, recipient);
          }
        })
      }
    });

    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
  }
}
```

### Configure Cloudflare Worker with Agent Bindings (JSONC)

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/configuration.md

This JSONC configuration sets up a Cloudflare Worker for an agents project. It defines the worker's name, main entry point, compatibility settings, static assets, Durable Object bindings for agents (like 'MyAgent' and 'ChatAgent'), and enables SQLite storage via migrations. It also includes an optional AI binding for Workers AI integration.

```jsonc
{
  "$schema": "node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json",
  "name": "my-agent-app",
  "main": "src/server.ts",
  "compatibility_date": "2025-01-01",
  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"],

  "assets": {
    "directory": "public",
    "binding": "ASSETS"
  },

  "durable_objects": {
    "bindings": [
      {
        "name": "MyAgent",
        "class_name": "MyAgent"
      },
      {
        "name": "ChatAgent",
        "class_name": "ChatAgent"
      }
    ]
  },

  "migrations": [
    {
      "tag": "v1",
      "new_sqlite_classes": ["MyAgent", "ChatAgent"]
    }
  ],

  "ai": {
    "binding": "AI"
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Configure Wrangler for Cloudflare Agents

Source: https://context7.com/cloudflare/agents/llms.txt

This JSON configuration sets up a Cloudflare Worker project for agents. It defines Durable Object bindings for agent classes, specifies SQLite migrations for state management, and includes optional bindings for AI services, workflows, KV namespaces, and R2 buckets. Ensure 'name', 'main', and 'compatibility_date' are set appropriately for your project.

```jsonc
{
  "name": "my-agents-app",
  "main": "src/index.ts",
  "compatibility_date": "2025-01-01",
  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"],

  // Durable Object bindings - one per agent class
  "durable_objects": {
    "bindings": [
      { "name": "GameAgent", "class_name": "GameAgent" },
      { "name": "ChatAgent", "class_name": "ChatAgent" },
      { "name": "EmailAgent", "class_name": "EmailAgent" }
    ]
  },

  // SQLite migrations - required for state persistence
  "migrations": [
    {
      "tag": "v1",
      "new_sqlite_classes": ["GameAgent", "ChatAgent", "EmailAgent"]
    }
  ],

  // Optional: AI binding for Workers AI
  "ai": { "binding": "AI" },

  // Optional: Workflows
  "workflows": [
    {
      "name": "processing-workflow",
      "binding": "PROCESSING_WORKFLOW",
      "class_name": "ProcessingWorkflow"
    }
  ],

  // Optional: KV for OAuth provider
  "kv_namespaces": [
    { "binding": "OAUTH_KV", "id": "your-kv-id" }
  ],

  // Optional: R2 for file storage
  "r2_buckets": [
    { "binding": "BUCKET", "bucket_name": "your-bucket" }
  ]
}
```

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/guides/anthropic-patterns/README.md

These bash commands outline the initial setup steps for the project, including installing npm dependencies and creating a `.dev.vars` file for API keys. This is a standard procedure for Node.js projects to manage environment-specific configurations.

# Install dependencies
npm install

# Create environment variables file
# Add your API keys to this file
cp .dev.vars.example .dev.vars
# Then edit .dev.vars to include your keys:
# OPENAI_API_KEY=your_key_here
# AI_GATEWAY_TOKEN=your_key_here

  },
  "durable_objects_namespaces": [
    {
      "binding": "AGENT_DO"
    }
  ]
}

### Define Client and Server Tools in AIChatAgent (TypeScript)

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/client-tools-continuation.md

This snippet demonstrates how to define both client-side and server-side tools within an `AIChatAgent`. Client tools lack an `execute` function and are handled on the client, while server tools have an `execute` function that runs automatically on the server. It uses Zod for schema validation and `workers-ai-provider` for model interaction.

```typescript
import {
  AIChatAgent,
  streamText,
  tool,
  convertToModelMessages,
  stepCountIs
} from "@cloudflare/ai-chat";
import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
import { z } from "zod";

// Assume fetchWeather is defined elsewhere
// async function fetchWeather(city: string): Promise<string> { /* ... */ }

export class MyAgent extends AIChatAgent {
  async onChatMessage() {
    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });

    const result = streamText({
      model: workersai("@cf/zai-org/glm-4.7-flash"),
      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
      tools: {
        // Client-side tool: no execute function
        getUserLocation: tool({
          description: "Get the user's location from their browser",
          inputSchema: z.object({})
        }),

        // Server-side tool: has execute, runs automatically
        getWeather: tool({
          description: "Get weather for a city",
          inputSchema: z.object({ city: z.string() }),
          execute: async ({ city }) => fetchWeather(city)
        })
      },
      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5) // Allow multi-step so the LLM can respond after tool results
    });

    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Using OpenAI Provider with Cloudflare Agents (TypeScript)

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/chat-agents.md

Illustrates the integration of the OpenAI provider with Cloudflare Agents. This snippet demonstrates initializing the `@ai-sdk/openai` package and configuring `streamText` to use an OpenAI chat model.

```typescript
import { createOpenAI } from "@ai-sdk/openai";

const openai = createOpenAI({ apiKey: this.env.OPENAI_API_KEY });
const result = streamText({
  model: openai.chat("gpt-4o"),
  messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages)
});
```

### routeAgentRequest() Usage

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/routing.md

The main entry point for agent routing. Handles both HTTP requests and WebSocket upgrades.

```APIDOC
## POST /agents/{agent-name}/{instance-name}

### Description
Routes incoming requests to the appropriate agent instance based on the URL structure.

### Method
POST (or other HTTP methods, including WebSocket upgrades)

### Endpoint
`/agents/{agent-name}/{instance-name}`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **agent-name** (string) - Required - The name of the agent class (automatically converted to kebab-case).
- **instance-name** (string) - Required - The unique identifier for the agent instance.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None (for routing purposes; agent logic may define its own body)

### Request Example
```typescript
import { routeAgentRequest } from "agents";

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);

    if (agentResponse) {
      return agentResponse;
    }

    return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  }
};
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200 or other)
- The `Response` object returned by the matched agent instance, or `undefined` if no agent matches.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Hello from agent!"
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### Define Server-Side Tools with Approval in TypeScript

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/guides/human-in-the-loop/README.md

Defines tools for an AIChatAgent, specifying which tools require user approval before execution (`needsApproval: true`) and which can be executed client-side (no `execute` function). This example uses `zod` for schema validation and `openai` for model integration.

```typescript
import { AIChatAgent } from "@cloudflare/ai-chat";
import { streamText, convertToModelMessages, tool } from "ai";
import { z } from "zod";

export class HumanInTheLoop extends AIChatAgent {
  async onChatMessage() {
    const result = streamText({
      model: openai("gpt-4o"),
      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
      tools: {
        // Requires approval -- needsApproval: true
        getWeather: tool({
          description: "Get weather for a city",
          inputSchema: z.object({ city: z.string() }),
          needsApproval: true,
          execute: async ({ city }) => `The weather in ${city} is sunny.`
        }),

        // Client-side tool -- no execute function
        getLocalTime: tool({
          description: "Get local time for a location",
          inputSchema: z.object({ location: z.string() })
        }),

        // Automatic -- no approval, runs server-side
        getNews: tool({
          description: "Get news for a location",
          inputSchema: z.object({ location: z.string() }),
          execute: async ({ location }) => `${location} news: all good!`
        })
      },
      maxSteps: 5
    });

    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Server-Side Tool Definition with tool() in v6

Source: https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/migration-to-ai-sdk-v6.md

Shows the recommended server-side tool definition pattern in v6 using the `tool()` function for Zod type safety. Includes examples of `needsApproval` and omitting `execute` for client-handled tools.

```typescript
// Server: all tools defined here
const tools = {
  getWeather: tool({
    description: "Get weather",
    inputSchema: z.object({ city: z.string() }),
    execute: async ({ city }) => fetchWeather(city)
  }),
  getLocation: tool({
    description: "Get user location",
    inputSchema: z.object({})
    // No execute -- client handles via onToolCall
  }),
  processPayment: tool({
    description: "Process payment",
    inputSchema: z.object({ amount: z.number() }),
    needsApproval: async ({ amount }) => amount > 100,
    execute: async ({ amount }) => charge(amount)
  })
};

// Client: handle tools via callbacks
useAgentChat({
  agent,
  onToolCall: async ({ toolCall, addToolOutput }) => {
    if (toolCall.toolName === "getLocation") {
      const pos = await getPosition();
      addToolOutput({
        toolCallId: toolCall.toolCallId,
        output: { lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude }
      });
    }
  }
});
```
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Cloudflare Agents SDK** は Durable Objects 上で動作し、SQLite で状態を自動永続化する。`AIChatAgent` クラスを継承して `onChatMessage` を実装するのが基本パターン
- **AI SDK v6** (`ai` パッケージ) の `streamText` / `tool` を使い、Workers AI・OpenAI・Anthropic をプロバイダーとして差し替え可能
- `routeAgentRequest` でHTTP/WebSocket リクエストをエージェントインスタンスにルーティング
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

では、プロジェクトの骨格を作ります。まず自然言語クエリエージェント（D1に対してText-to-SQL）を実装しますが、**AIプロバイダーの選択**について確認させてください。

Issueには Workers AI (Llama 3) または OpenAI と書いてありますが、選択肢として：

1. **Workers AI** (`@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct`) - 無料枠あり、Cloudflare内完結、ただしSQL生成の精度は劣る
2. **OpenAI** (`gpt-4o`) - SQL生成の精度が高い、API Key必要
3. **Anthropic Claude** (`claude-sonnet-4-20250514`) - 同様に高精度、API Key必要

どれを使いますか？複数対応も可能ですが、最初のデフォルトをどれにするかで `.dev.vars` の設定が変わります。

> DEVELOPER

1

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/index.ts",
  "content": "import { routeAgentRequest } from \"agents\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nexport { DataAgent } from \"./agent\";\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n\n    if (url.pathname === \"/health\") {\n      return new Response(\"OK\");\n    }\n\n    // /agents/data-agent/:instanceId にルーティング\n    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);\n    if (agentResponse) {\n      return agentResponse;\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n"
}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `routeAgentRequest` は URL パス `/agents/{agent-name}/{instance-id}` を自動解析し、対応する Durable Object インスタンスにルーティングする。`agent-name` はクラス名をkebab-caseに変換したもの（`DataAgent` → `data-agent`）
- エージェントクラスは `export` で公開する必要がある。Wrangler が Durable Object として認識するため
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

ここからがエージェント本体です。D1のスキーマ情報をコンテキストとしてLLMに渡し、Text-to-SQLを行う部分ですが、**SQLの実行戦略**について意見を聞かせてください。

D1に対して生成されたSQLを実行する際、2つのアプローチがあります：

1. **自動実行**: LLMがSQLを生成→即座に実行→結果を返す（シンプルだが誤ったDELETE等のリスク）
2. **承認フロー（Human-in-the-loop）**: SELECT以外は承認を求める（安全だが手間）

エージェントの `tools` で `needsApproval` を使えば、SELECT以外のクエリに承認を要求できます。どちらにしますか？

> DEVELOPER

そうして

     1→-- ============================================
     2→-- Core Infrastructure Tables
     3→-- ============================================
     4→
     5→-- データソース設定
     6→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_sources (
     7→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
     8→    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
     9→    source_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'api', 'export', 'manual'
    10→    api_endpoint TEXT,
    11→    schedule_cron TEXT,
    12→    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    13→    config_json TEXT,
    14→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    15→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    16→);
    17→
    18→-- パイプライン実行履歴
    19→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pipeline_runs (
    20→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    21→    data_source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    22→    run_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'full', 'incremental'
    23→    status TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'pending', 'running', 'success', 'failed'
    24→    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    25→    completed_at TEXT,
    26→    records_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    27→    records_failed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    28→    error_message TEXT,
    29→    r2_output_path TEXT,
    30→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    31→    FOREIGN KEY (data_source_id) REFERENCES data_sources(id)
    32→);
    33→
    34→-- エラーログ
    35→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS error_logs (
    36→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    37→    pipeline_run_id TEXT,
    38→    error_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    39→    error_message TEXT NOT NULL,
    40→    stack_trace TEXT,
    41→    context_json TEXT,
    42→    occurred_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    43→    FOREIGN KEY (pipeline_run_id) REFERENCES pipeline_runs(id)
    44→);
    45→
    46→-- 同期状態（増分同期用）
    47→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_state (
    48→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    49→    data_source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    50→    last_sync_at TEXT,
    51→    last_cursor TEXT,
    52→    metadata_json TEXT,
    53→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    54→    FOREIGN KEY (data_source_id) REFERENCES data_sources(id)
    55→);
    56→
    57→-- Core Infrastructure Indexes
    58→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pipeline_runs_status ON pipeline_runs(status);
    59→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pipeline_runs_source ON pipeline_runs(data_source_id, started_at DESC);
    60→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_error_logs_occurred ON error_logs(occurred_at DESC);
    61→
    62→-- ============================================
    63→-- GitHub Tables
    64→-- ============================================
    65→
    66→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS github_users (
    67→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    68→    login TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    69→    name TEXT,
    70→    avatar_url TEXT,
    71→    synced_at TEXT NOT NULL
    72→);
    73→
    74→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS github_repos (
    75→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    76→    owner_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    77→    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    78→    full_name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    79→    description TEXT,
    80→    language TEXT,
    81→    stars INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    82→    forks INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    83→    is_private INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    84→    default_branch TEXT,
    85→    synced_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    86→    FOREIGN KEY (owner_id) REFERENCES github_users(id)
    87→);
    88→
    89→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS github_commits (
    90→    sha TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    91→    repo_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    92→    message TEXT,
    93→    author_name TEXT,
    94→    author_email TEXT,
    95→    author_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    96→    additions INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    97→    deletions INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    98→    files_changed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    99→    synced_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   100→    FOREIGN KEY (repo_id) REFERENCES github_repos(id)
   101→);
   102→
   103→-- GitHub Indexes
   104→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_github_commits_repo ON github_commits(repo_id);
   105→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_github_commits_date ON github_commits(author_date DESC);
   106→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_github_repos_owner ON github_repos(owner_id);
   107→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_github_repos_language ON github_repos(language);
   108→
   109→-- ============================================
   110→-- Views
   111→-- ============================================
   112→
   113→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_daily_commits AS
   114→SELECT
   115→    date(author_date) AS commit_date,
   116→    count(*) AS commit_count,
   117→    sum(additions) AS total_additions,
   118→    sum(deletions) AS total_deletions,
   119→    count(DISTINCT repo_id) AS repos_touched
   120→FROM github_commits
   121→GROUP BY date(author_date)
   122→ORDER BY commit_date DESC;
   123→
   124→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_repo_stats AS
   125→SELECT
   126→    r.full_name,
   127→    r.language,
   128→    count(c.sha) AS total_commits,
   129→    sum(c.additions) AS total_additions,
   130→    sum(c.deletions) AS total_deletions,
   131→    max(c.author_date) AS last_commit_date
   132→FROM github_repos r
   133→LEFT JOIN github_commits c ON r.id = c.repo_id
   134→GROUP BY r.id
   135→ORDER BY total_commits DESC;
   136→
   137→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_weekly_activity AS
   138→SELECT
   139→    strftime('%Y-W%W', author_date) AS week,
   140→    count(*) AS commits,
   141→    count(DISTINCT repo_id) AS active_repos,
   142→    sum(additions + deletions) AS lines_changed
   143→FROM github_commits
   144→GROUP BY strftime('%Y-W%W', author_date)
   145→ORDER BY week DESC;
   146→
   147→-- ============================================
   148→-- Initial Data
   149→-- ============================================
   150→
   151→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron)
   152→VALUES ('github', 'GitHub', 'api', 'https://api.github.com', '0 0 * * *');
   153→

     1→-- ============================================
     2→-- OAuth2 Token Storage
     3→-- ============================================
     4→
     5→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oauth_tokens (
     6→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
     7→    access_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     8→    refresh_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     9→    token_type TEXT DEFAULT 'Bearer',
    10→    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    11→    scope TEXT,
    12→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    13→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    14→);
    15→
    16→-- ============================================
    17→-- Oura Ring Tables
    18→-- ============================================
    19→
    20→-- Daily Sleep
    21→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_sleep (
    22→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    23→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    24→    score INTEGER,
    25→    timestamp TEXT,
    26→    -- contributors
    27→    deep_sleep INTEGER,
    28→    efficiency INTEGER,
    29→    latency INTEGER,
    30→    rem_sleep INTEGER,
    31→    restfulness INTEGER,
    32→    timing INTEGER,
    33→    total_sleep INTEGER,
    34→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    35→);
    36→
    37→-- Daily Activity
    38→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_activity (
    39→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    40→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    41→    score INTEGER,
    42→    active_calories INTEGER,
    43→    total_calories INTEGER,
    44→    steps INTEGER,
    45→    equivalent_walking_distance REAL,
    46→    high_activity_time INTEGER,
    47→    medium_activity_time INTEGER,
    48→    low_activity_time INTEGER,
    49→    sedentary_time INTEGER,
    50→    resting_time INTEGER,
    51→    met_average REAL,
    52→    -- contributors
    53→    meet_daily_targets INTEGER,
    54→    move_every_hour INTEGER,
    55→    recovery_time INTEGER,
    56→    stay_active INTEGER,
    57→    training_frequency INTEGER,
    58→    training_volume INTEGER,
    59→    timestamp TEXT,
    60→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    61→);
    62→
    63→-- Daily Readiness
    64→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_readiness (
    65→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    66→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    67→    score INTEGER,
    68→    temperature_deviation REAL,
    69→    temperature_trend_deviation REAL,
    70→    timestamp TEXT,
    71→    -- contributors
    72→    activity_balance INTEGER,
    73→    body_temperature INTEGER,
    74→    hrv_balance INTEGER,
    75→    previous_day_activity INTEGER,
    76→    previous_night INTEGER,
    77→    recovery_index INTEGER,
    78→    resting_heart_rate INTEGER,
    79→    sleep_balance INTEGER,
    80→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    81→);
    82→
    83→-- Heart Rate (high volume: ~288 records/day at 5min intervals)
    84→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_heart_rate (
    85→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    86→    bpm INTEGER NOT NULL,
    87→    source TEXT,
    88→    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
    89→    day TEXT NOT NULL,
    90→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    91→);
    92→
    93→-- ============================================
    94→-- Oura Indexes
    95→-- ============================================
    96→
    97→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_sleep_day ON oura_daily_sleep(day DESC);
    98→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_activity_day ON oura_daily_activity(day DESC);
    99→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_readiness_day ON oura_daily_readiness(day DESC);
   100→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_day ON oura_heart_rate(day DESC);
   101→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_timestamp ON oura_heart_rate(timestamp DESC);
   102→
   103→-- ============================================
   104→-- Oura Views
   105→-- ============================================
   106→
   107→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_oura_daily_summary AS
   108→SELECT
   109→    s.day,
   110→    s.score AS sleep_score,
   111→    a.score AS activity_score,
   112→    r.score AS readiness_score,
   113→    a.steps,
   114→    a.total_calories,
   115→    a.active_calories,
   116→    r.temperature_deviation,
   117→    s.deep_sleep AS sleep_deep_sleep,
   118→    s.efficiency AS sleep_efficiency,
   119→    s.total_sleep AS sleep_total_sleep
   120→FROM oura_daily_sleep s
   121→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_activity a ON s.day = a.day
   122→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_readiness r ON s.day = r.day
   123→ORDER BY s.day DESC;
   124→

     1→-- Linear Issues
     2→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS linear_issues (
     3→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
     4→    identifier TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
     5→    title TEXT NOT NULL,
     6→    description_length INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
     7→    priority INTEGER,
     8→    estimate REAL,
     9→    state_name TEXT,
    10→    state_type TEXT,
    11→    label_names TEXT,
    12→    project_name TEXT,
    13→    cycle_number INTEGER,
    14→    assignee_name TEXT,
    15→    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    16→    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    17→    started_at TEXT,
    18→    completed_at TEXT,
    19→    canceled_at TEXT,
    20→    due_date TEXT,
    21→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    22→);
    23→
    24→-- Linear Projects
    25→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS linear_projects (
    26→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    27→    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    28→    state TEXT,
    29→    progress REAL,
    30→    start_date TEXT,
    31→    target_date TEXT,
    32→    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    33→    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    34→    completed_at TEXT,
    35→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    36→);
    37→
    38→-- Linear Labels
    39→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS linear_labels (
    40→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    41→    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    42→    color TEXT,
    43→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    44→);
    45→
    46→-- Indexes
    47→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_linear_issues_state ON linear_issues(state_type);
    48→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_linear_issues_project ON linear_issues(project_name);
    49→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_linear_issues_completed ON linear_issues(completed_at DESC);
    50→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_linear_issues_updated ON linear_issues(updated_at DESC);
    51→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_linear_issues_identifier ON linear_issues(identifier);
    52→
    53→-- Views
    54→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_linear_weekly_completion AS
    55→SELECT
    56→    strftime('%Y-W%W', completed_at) AS week,
    57→    count(*) AS completed_count,
    58→    avg(julianday(completed_at) - julianday(created_at)) AS avg_lead_time_days
    59→FROM linear_issues
    60→WHERE completed_at IS NOT NULL
    61→GROUP BY strftime('%Y-W%W', completed_at)
    62→ORDER BY week DESC;
    63→
    64→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_linear_label_summary AS
    65→SELECT
    66→    j.value AS label_name,
    67→    count(*) AS issue_count,
    68→    sum(CASE WHEN state_type = 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS completed_count
    69→FROM linear_issues, json_each(linear_issues.label_names) AS j
    70→GROUP BY j.value
    71→ORDER BY issue_count DESC;
    72→
    73→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_linear_project_progress AS
    74→SELECT
    75→    project_name,
    76→    count(*) AS total_issues,
    77→    sum(CASE WHEN state_type = 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS completed,
    78→    sum(CASE WHEN state_type = 'canceled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS canceled,
    79→    sum(CASE WHEN state_type IN ('triage', 'backlog', 'unstarted') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pending,
    80→    sum(CASE WHEN state_type = 'started' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS in_progress,
    81→    round(100.0 * sum(CASE WHEN state_type = 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) / count(*), 1) AS completion_rate
    82→FROM linear_issues
    83→WHERE project_name IS NOT NULL
    84→GROUP BY project_name;
    85→
    86→-- Initial Data
    87→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron)
    88→VALUES ('linear', 'Linear', 'api', 'https://api.linear.app/graphql', '0 */6 * * *');
    89→
    90→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)
    91→VALUES ('linear', 'linear', NULL);
    92→

     1→-- =============================================
     2→-- Withings Health Data Tables
     3→-- =============================================
     4→
     5→-- Body composition measurements (weight, fat, muscle, etc.)
     6→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS withings_measures (
     7→  grpid     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
     8→  date      TEXT NOT NULL,
     9→  timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
    10→  weight        REAL,
    11→  fat_ratio     REAL,
    12→  fat_mass      REAL,
    13→  fat_free_mass REAL,
    14→  muscle_mass   REAL,
    15→  bone_mass     REAL,
    16→  hydration     REAL,
    17→  heart_pulse   INTEGER,
    18→  synced_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    19→);
    20→
    21→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_withings_measures_date ON withings_measures(date DESC);
    22→
    23→-- Sleep summary
    24→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS withings_sleep (
    25→  id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    26→  date      TEXT NOT NULL,
    27→  startdate INTEGER NOT NULL,
    28→  enddate   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    29→  total_sleep_duration  INTEGER,
    30→  deep_sleep_duration   INTEGER,
    31→  light_sleep_duration  INTEGER,
    32→  rem_sleep_duration    INTEGER,
    33→  wakeup_duration       INTEGER,
    34→  sleep_score           INTEGER,
    35→  sleep_efficiency      REAL,
    36→  sleep_latency         INTEGER,
    37→  hr_average  REAL,
    38→  hr_min      INTEGER,
    39→  hr_max      INTEGER,
    40→  rr_average  REAL,
    41→  rr_min      REAL,
    42→  rr_max      REAL,
    43→  snoring               INTEGER,
    44→  snoring_episode_count INTEGER,
    45→  synced_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    46→);
    47→
    48→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_withings_sleep_date ON withings_sleep(date DESC);
    49→
    50→-- Daily activity
    51→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS withings_activity (
    52→  date      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    53→  steps     INTEGER,
    54→  distance  REAL,
    55→  elevation REAL,
    56→  soft      INTEGER,
    57→  moderate  INTEGER,
    58→  intense   INTEGER,
    59→  active    INTEGER,
    60→  calories      REAL,
    61→  total_calories REAL,
    62→  hr_average  REAL,
    63→  hr_min      INTEGER,
    64→  hr_max      INTEGER,
    65→  synced_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    66→);
    67→
    68→-- Daily summary view
    69→-- Uses subqueries to pick the latest measure per date (avoids cartesian product)
    70→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_withings_daily_summary AS
    71→SELECT
    72→  d.date,
    73→  m.weight,
    74→  m.fat_ratio,
    75→  m.fat_mass,
    76→  m.muscle_mass,
    77→  m.bone_mass,
    78→  s.total_sleep_duration,
    79→  s.deep_sleep_duration,
    80→  s.light_sleep_duration,
    81→  s.rem_sleep_duration,
    82→  s.sleep_score,
    83→  s.sleep_efficiency,
    84→  a.steps,
    85→  a.distance,
    86→  a.calories,
    87→  a.total_calories,
    88→  a.active
    89→FROM (
    90→  SELECT date FROM withings_measures
    91→  UNION
    92→  SELECT date FROM withings_sleep
    93→  UNION
    94→  SELECT date FROM withings_activity
    95→) d
    96→LEFT JOIN (
    97→  SELECT m1.*
    98→  FROM withings_measures m1
    99→  INNER JOIN (
   100→    SELECT date, MAX(grpid) AS max_grpid FROM withings_measures GROUP BY date
   101→  ) m2 ON m1.grpid = m2.max_grpid
   102→) m ON d.date = m.date
   103→LEFT JOIN (
   104→  SELECT s1.*
   105→  FROM withings_sleep s1
   106→  INNER JOIN (
   107→    SELECT date, MAX(id) AS max_id FROM withings_sleep GROUP BY date
   108→  ) s2 ON s1.id = s2.max_id
   109→) s ON d.date = s.date
   110→LEFT JOIN withings_activity a ON d.date = a.date
   111→ORDER BY d.date DESC;
   112→
   113→-- =============================================
   114→-- Initial Data
   115→-- =============================================
   116→
   117→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron)
   118→VALUES ('withings', 'Withings', 'api', 'https://wbsapi.withings.net', '0 */12 * * *');
   119→
   120→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)
   121→VALUES ('withings', 'withings', NULL);
   122→

  "content": "export const D1_SCHEMA = `\n-- データソースは GitHub, Oura Ring, Linear, Withings の4つ\n\n-- ============ Core Infrastructure ============\n-- data_sources: データソース設定 (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron, is_active)\n-- pipeline_runs: パイプライン実行履歴 (id, data_source_id, run_type, status, started_at, completed_at, records_processed, records_failed, error_message)\n-- error_logs: エラーログ (id, pipeline_run_id, error_type, error_message, occurred_at)\n-- sync_state: 同期状態 (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at, last_cursor)\n\n-- ============ GitHub ============\n-- github_users: ユーザー (id, login, name, avatar_url, synced_at)\n-- github_repos: リポジトリ (id, owner_id, name, full_name, description, language, stars, forks, is_private, synced_at)\n-- github_commits: コミット (sha, repo_id, message, author_name, author_email, author_date, additions, deletions, files_changed, synced_at)\n-- v_daily_commits: 日別コミット集計 (commit_date, commit_count, total_additions, total_deletions, repos_touched)\n-- v_repo_stats: リポジトリ統計 (full_name, language, total_commits, total_additions, total_deletions, last_commit_date)\n-- v_weekly_activity: 週別アクティビティ (week, commits, active_repos, lines_changed)\n\n-- ============ Oura Ring ============\n-- oura_daily_sleep: 日別睡眠データ (id, day, score, deep_sleep, efficiency, latency, rem_sleep, restfulness, timing, total_sleep)\n-- oura_daily_activity: 日別活動データ (id, day, score, active_calories, total_calories, steps, high_activity_time, medium_activity_time, low_activity_time, sedentary_time)\n-- oura_daily_readiness: 日別コンディション (id, day, score, temperature_deviation, hrv_balance, resting_heart_rate, sleep_balance)\n-- oura_heart_rate: 心拍数 (id, bpm, source, timestamp, day)\n-- v_oura_daily_summary: Oura日次サマリー (day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories, temperature_deviation)\n\n-- ============ Linear ============\n-- linear_issues: Issue (id, identifier, title, priority, state_name, state_type, label_names[JSON], project_name, assignee_name, created_at, completed_at)\n-- linear_projects: プロジェクト (id, name, state, progress, start_date, target_date)\n-- v_linear_weekly_completion: 週別完了数 (week, completed_count, avg_lead_time_days)\n-- v_linear_project_progress: プロジェクト進捗 (project_name, total_issues, completed, in_progress, completion_rate)\n\n-- ============ Withings ============\n-- withings_measures: 体組成 (grpid, date, weight, fat_ratio, fat_mass, muscle_mass, bone_mass, heart_pulse)\n-- withings_sleep: 睡眠 (id, date, total_sleep_duration, deep_sleep_duration, rem_sleep_duration, sleep_score, sleep_efficiency, hr_average)\n-- withings_activity: 活動 (date, steps, distance, calories, total_calories, hr_average)\n-- v_withings_daily_summary: Withings日次サマリー (date, weight, fat_ratio, muscle_mass, sleep_score, steps, calories)\n\n-- 注意: 日付カラムは TEXT 型 (ISO 8601 形式)。SQLite の date() / strftime() 関数を使うこと。\n`;\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/agent.ts",
  "content": "import { AIChatAgent } from \"@cloudflare/ai-chat\";\nimport { streamText, convertToModelMessages, tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\nimport { D1_SCHEMA } from \"./schema\";\n\nconst SYSTEM_PROMPT = `あなたはデータ基盤のアシスタントです。\nユーザーの質問に対して、D1データベース (SQLite) に対するSQLを生成・実行して回答します。\n\n## 利用可能なデータソース\n- GitHub: コミット、リポジトリ、ユーザー情報\n- Oura Ring: 睡眠、活動、コンディションデータ\n- Linear: Issue、プロジェクト管理データ\n- Withings: 体組成、睡眠、活動データ\n\n## スキーマ\n${D1_SCHEMA}\n\n## ルール\n- SELECT クエリのみ自動実行されます。データの変更は承認が必要です。\n- 日本語で回答してください。\n- 集計ビュー (v_ プレフィックス) が使える場合はそちらを優先してください。\n- 大量データの取得は LIMIT を付けてください。\n- 結果を簡潔にまとめて回答してください。\n`;\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    onFinish?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });\n\n    const result = streamText({\n      model: workersai(\"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\"),\n      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),\n      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,\n      maxSteps: 5,\n      tools: {\n        queryD1: tool({\n          description:\n            \"D1データベースに対してSQLクエリを実行する。SELECTクエリは自動実行、それ以外は承認が必要。\",\n          inputSchema: z.object({\n            sql: z.string().describe(\"実行するSQLクエリ\"),\n            params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe(\"バインドパラメータ\"),\n          }),\n          needsApproval: async ({ sql }) => {\n            const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();\n            return !normalized.startsWith(\"SELECT\");\n          },\n          execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {\n            try {\n              const stmt = this.env.DB.prepare(sql);\n              const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n              const result = await bound.all();\n\n              return {\n                success: true,\n                rowCount: result.results.length,\n                results: result.results.slice(0, 50),\n                meta: result.meta,\n              };\n            } catch (error) {\n              return {\n                success: false,\n                error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n              };\n            }\n          },\n        }),\n        listTables: tool({\n          description: \"D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する\",\n          inputSchema: z.object({}),\n          execute: async () => {\n            const result = await this.env.DB.prepare(\n              \"SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name\"\n            ).all();\n            return result.results;\n          },\n        }),\n      },\n    });\n\n    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();\n  }\n}\n"
}

import { t as ResumableStream } from "./resumable-stream-DTLAk9XC.js";
import {
  JSONSchema7,
  StreamTextOnFinishCallback,
  Tool,
  ToolSet,
  UIMessage
} from "ai";
import { Agent, AgentContext } from "agents";

//#region src/index.d.ts
/**
 * Schema for a client-defined tool sent from the browser.
 * These tools are executed on the client, not the server.
 *
 * **For most apps**, define tools on the server with `tool()` from `"ai"` —
 * you get full Zod type safety, server-side execution, and simpler code.
 * Use `onToolCall` in `useAgentChat` for tools that need client-side execution.
 *
 * **For SDKs and platforms** where the tool surface is determined dynamically
 * by the embedding application at runtime, client tool schemas let the
 * client register tools the server does not know about at deploy time.
 *
 * Note: Uses `parameters` (JSONSchema7) rather than AI SDK's `inputSchema`
 * because this is the wire format. Zod schemas cannot be serialized.
 */
type ClientToolSchema = {
  /** Unique name for the tool */ name: string /** Human-readable description of what the tool does */;
  description?: Tool["description"] /** JSON Schema defining the tool's input parameters */;
  parameters?: JSONSchema7;
import { MessageType } from "./types.js";
import { autoTransformMessages } from "./ai-chat-v5-migration.js";
import { t as applyChunkToParts } from "./message-builder-uSvexDl2.js";
import { jsonSchema, tool } from "ai";
import { Agent, __DO_NOT_USE_WILL_BREAK__agentContext } from "agents";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

//#region src/resumable-stream.ts
/**
* ResumableStream: Standalone class for buffering, persisting, and replaying
* stream chunks in SQLite. Extracted from AIChatAgent to separate concerns.
*
* Handles:
* - Chunk buffering (batched writes to SQLite for performance)
* - Stream lifecycle (start, complete, error)
* - Chunk replay for reconnecting clients
* - Stale stream cleanup
* - Active stream restoration after agent restart
*/
/** Number of chunks to buffer before flushing to SQLite */
const CHUNK_BUFFER_SIZE = 10;
/** Maximum buffer size to prevent memory issues on rapid reconnections */
const CHUNK_BUFFER_MAX_SIZE = 100;
/** Maximum age for a "streaming" stream before considering it stale (ms) - 5 minutes */
const STREAM_STALE_THRESHOLD_MS = 300 * 1e3;
/** Default cleanup interval for old streams (ms) - every 10 minutes */
const CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS = 600 * 1e3;
/** Default age threshold for cleaning up completed streams (ms) - 24 hours */
const CLEANUP_AGE_THRESHOLD_MS = 1440 * 60 * 1e3;
/** Shared encoder for UTF-8 byte length measurement */

  "content": "import { AIChatAgent } from \"@cloudflare/ai-chat\";\nimport { streamText, convertToCoreMessages, tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\nimport { D1_SCHEMA } from \"./schema\";\n\nconst SYSTEM_PROMPT = `あなたは Oura Ring データのアシスタントです。\nユーザーの質問に対して、D1データベース (SQLite) に対するSQLを生成・実行して回答します。\n\n## 利用可能なデータ\nOura Ring から取得した健康データ（睡眠、活動、コンディション、心拍数）\n\n## スキーマ\n${D1_SCHEMA}\n\n## ルール\n- SELECT クエリのみ自動実行されます。データの変更は承認が必要です。\n- 日本語で回答してください。\n- v_oura_daily_summary ビューが使える場合はそちらを優先してください。\n- 大量データの取得は LIMIT を付けてください。\n- 結果を簡潔にまとめて回答してください。\n`;\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });\n\n    const result = streamText({\n      model: workersai(\"@cf/meta/llama-3.1-70b-instruct\"),\n      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n      messages: convertToCoreMessages(this.messages),\n      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,\n      maxSteps: 5,\n      tools: {\n        queryD1: tool({\n          description:\n            \"D1データベースに対してSQLクエリを実行する。SELECTクエリは自動実行、それ以外は承認が必要。\",\n          parameters: z.object({\n            sql: z.string().describe(\"実行するSQLクエリ\"),\n            params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe(\"バインドパラメータ\"),\n          }),\n          needsApproval: async ({ sql }) => {\n            const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();\n            return !normalized.startsWith(\"SELECT\");\n          },\n          execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {\n            try {\n              const stmt = this.env.DB.prepare(sql);\n              const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n              const result = await bound.all();\n\n              return {\n                success: true,\n                rowCount: result.results.length,\n                results: result.results.slice(0, 50),\n                meta: result.meta,\n              };\n            } catch (error) {\n              return {\n                success: false,\n                error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n              };\n            }\n          },\n        }),\n        listTables: tool({\n          description: \"D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する\",\n          parameters: z.object({}),\n          execute: async () => {\n            const result = await this.env.DB.prepare(\n              \"SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name\"\n            ).all();\n            return result.results;\n          },\n        }),\n      },\n    });\n\n    return result.toDataStreamResponse();\n  }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

型は `@cloudflare/workers-types` の `AiModels` から派生しています。モデル名は型チェック時に厳密にチェックされるので、`as any` でキャストするか、正しい型名を使う必要があります。`needsApproval` も `ai` v4.3 にはないので、SELECT判定はexecute内で行うように変更します。

string): boolean {\n  const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();\n  return normalized.startsWith(\"SELECT\") || normalized.startsWith(\"WITH\");\n}\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai =  * @param options.inputSchema - A function that returns the JSON Schema for a tool.
 * @param options.error - The error that occurred while parsing the tool call.
 */
type ToolCallRepairFunction<TOOLS extends ToolSet> = (options: {
    system: string | SystemModelMessage | Array<SystemModelMessage> | undefined;
    messages: ModelMessage[];
    toolCall: LanguageModelV3ToolCall;
    tools: TOOLS;
    inputSchema: (options: {
        toolName: string;
    }) => PromiseLike<JSONSchema7>;
    error: NoSuchToolError | InvalidToolInputError;
}) => Promise<LanguageModelV3ToolCall | null>;

/**
 * Include settings for generateText (requestBody and responseBody).
 */
type GenerateTextIncludeSettings = {
    requestBody?: boolean;
    responseBody?: boolean;
};
```

function createToolsFromClientSchemas(clientTools) {
	if (!clientTools || clientTools.length === 0) return {};
	const seenNames = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	for (const t of clientTools) {
		if (seenNames.has(t.name)) console.warn(`[createToolsFromClientSchemas] Duplicate tool name "${t.name}" found. Later definitions will override earlier ones.`);
		seenNames.add(t.name);
	}
	return Object.fromEntries(clientTools.map((t) => [t.name, tool({
		description: t.description ?? "",
		inputSchema: jsonSchema(t.parameters ?? { type: "object" })
	})]));
}

> AGENT

起動成功です。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **AI SDK v6 の破壊的変更**: `convertToCoreMessages` → `convertToModelMessages`（async化）、`maxSteps` → `stopWhen: stepCountIs(N)`、`parameters` → `inputSchema` に戻った
- **Workers AI のモデルID型**: `workers-ai-provider` の型定義が `@cloudflare/workers-types` から `AiModels` を参照しており、最新モデル名は型に含まれていることもある。v6+では解消
- **Durable Objects + SQLite**: Agents SDK はDO内のSQLiteに会話履歴を自動永続化。`this.messages` でアクセスでき、再接続時にも状態が保持される
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

プロジェクト構造のまとめです：

```
agents/
├── biome.json
├── package.json
├── tsconfig.json
├── wrangler.jsonc
└── src/
    ├── index.ts      # エントリポイント (routeAgentRequest)
    ├── agent.ts       # DataAgent (AIChatAgent) - Text-to-SQL
    ├── schema.ts      # D1スキーマ定義 (Ouraのみ)
    └── types.ts       # Env型
```

**実装済み:**
- `DataAgent` - Oura Ring データに対する自然言語クエリエージェント
- `queryD1` ツール - SELECTのみ自動実行、それ以外は拒否
- `listTables` ツール - テーブル一覧取得
- Workers AI (`llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast`) でSQL生成

### Generate Text with AI SDK in TypeScript Agent

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/using-ai-models

Shows how to use the AI SDK for text generation within a TypeScript Agent. This example utilizes the `generateText` function to interact with an AI model and return the generated text.

```typescript
import { Agent } from "agents";
import { generateText } from "ai";
import { openai } from "@ai-sdk/openai";


export class MyAgent extends Agent<Env> {
  async onRequest(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    const { text } = await generateText({
      model: openai("o3-mini"),
      prompt: "Build me an AI agent on Cloudflare Workers",
    });


    return Response.json({ modelResponse: text });
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Generate Text with AI SDK in JavaScript and TypeScript

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/llms-full

Demonstrates how to generate text using the AI SDK, leveraging MCP tools. It fetches all available MCP tools and uses them with a specified model and prompt. This is useful for AI-powered agents that need to interact with external services or data.

```javascript
import { generateText } from "ai";


export class MyAgent extends Agent {
  async onRequest(request) {
    // Get all MCP tools as AI SDK compatible tools
    const tools = this.mcp.getAITools();


    const result = await generateText({
      model: openai("gpt-4"),
      prompt: "What's the weather in San Francisco?",
      tools,
    });


    return new Response(result.text);
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { generateText } from "ai";


export class MyAgent extends Agent<Env, never> {
  async onRequest(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    // Get all MCP tools as AI SDK compatible tools
    const tools = this.mcp.getAITools();


    const result = await generateText({
      model: openai("gpt-4"),
      prompt: "What's the weather in San Francisco?",
      tools,
    });


    return new Response(result.text);
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Use Workers AI Models with Streaming in JavaScript Agent

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/using-ai-models

Demonstrates how to use a Workers AI model within an Agent and enable streaming responses for improved performance. This avoids buffering and delays, especially for complex models. Requires an 'AI' binding in Wrangler configuration.

```javascript
import { Agent } from "agents";


export class MyAgent extends Agent {
  async onRequest(request) {
    const response = await env.AI.run(
      "@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-32b",
      {
        prompt: "Build me a Cloudflare Worker that returns JSON.",
        stream: true, // Stream a response and don't block the client!
      },
    );


    // Return the stream
    return new Response(answer, {
      headers: { "content-type": "text/event-stream" },
    });
  }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Use Workers AI Models with Streaming in TypeScript Agent

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/using-ai-models

Shows how to integrate Workers AI models with streaming enabled in a TypeScript Agent. This approach enhances responsiveness by streaming data directly. Ensure an 'AI' binding is configured in your Wrangler setup.

```typescript
import { Agent } from "agents";


interface Env {
  AI: Ai;
}


export class MyAgent extends Agent<Env> {
  async onRequest(request: Request) {
    const response = await env.AI.run(
      "@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-32b",
      {
        prompt: "Build me a Cloudflare Worker that returns JSON.",
        stream: true, // Stream a response and don't block the client!
      },
    );


    // Return the stream
    return new Response(answer, {
      headers: { "content-type": "text/event-stream" },
    });
  }
}
```

### Use Workers AI with Llama 3 Model

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/guides/slack-agent

This TypeScript code demonstrates how to replace the OpenAI integration with Cloudflare's Workers AI. It initializes the `Ai` service and uses the `run` method with the '@cf/meta/llama-3-8b-instruct' model to generate AI replies based on conversation history.

```typescript
import { Ai } from "@cloudflare/ai";


export class MyAgent extends SlackAgent {
  async generateAIReply(conversation: SlackMsg[]) {
    const ai = new Ai(this.ctx.env.AI);
    const response = await ai.run("@cf/meta/llama-3-8b-instruct", {
      messages: normalizeForLLM(conversation, await this.ensureAppUserId()),
    });
    return response.response;
  }
}
```
```

### Choose the Right Text Generation Model

Source: https://context7_llms

Navigate the diverse range of text generation models available through Workers AI. This notebook provides a 'speed dating' approach to help you understand and select the most suitable model for your needs.

```APIDOC
## Choose the Right Text Generation Model

### Description
This guide helps you select the optimal text generation model from the wide variety offered by Cloudflare Workers AI. It uses a comparative approach to understand model capabilities and performance.

### Method
GET (for retrieving model information)
POST (for model inference)

### Endpoint
`/v1/models` (for listing available models)
`/v1/run/{model_name}` (for model inference)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **model_name** (string) - Required - The name of the text generation model to use.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body (for POST /v1/run/{model_name})
- **prompt** (string) - Required - The input text prompt for the model.
- **max_tokens** (integer) - Optional - The maximum number of tokens to generate.
- **temperature** (float) - Optional - Controls randomness; higher values mean more diverse output.

### Request Example (List Models)
```bash
curl https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/{account_id}/ai/v1/models
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200) (List Models)
- **result** (array) - A list of available AI models.
  - **name** (string) - The unique identifier for the model.
  - **description** (string) - A brief description of the model's capabilities.
  - **context_length** (integer) - The maximum number of tokens the model can process.

#### Success Response (200) (Model Inference)
- **response** (object) - The generated text.
  - **content** (string) - The generated text output.

#### Response Example (Model Inference)
```json
{
  "response": {
    "content": "Cloudflare Workers AI offers a variety of powerful text generation models optimized for performance and scalability."
  }
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### Workers AI OpenAI Compatible Endpoints

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/ai-gateway/usage/providers/workersai/index

Utilize Workers AI with OpenAI compatible endpoints for seamless integration with existing OpenAI codebase. Supports text generation and text embedding models.

```APIDOC
## POST /v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/chat/completions

### Description
Provides text generation capabilities using Workers AI models through an OpenAI compatible endpoint.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/chat/completions`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **account_id** (string) - Required - Your Cloudflare account ID.
- **gateway_id** (string) - Required - The name of your AI Gateway.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **model** (string) - Required - The Workers AI model ID (e.g., `@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct`).
- **messages** (array) - Required - An array of message objects representing the conversation history.
  - **role** (string) - Required - The role of the message sender (e.g., 'user', 'system', 'assistant').
  - **content** (string) - Required - The content of the message.

### Request Example
```bash
curl https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/chat/completions \
 --header 'Authorization: Bearer {cf_api_token}' \
 --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
 --data '{ "model": "@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct", "messages": [ { "role": "user", "content": "What is Cloudflare?" } ] }'
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **choices** (array) - An array of completion choices.
  - **message** (object)
    - **role** (string) - The role of the sender of the message.
    - **content** (string) - The content of the message.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "choices": [
    {
      "message": {
        "role": "assistant",
        "content": "Cloudflare is a web infrastructure and security company..."
      }
    }
  ]
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/embeddings

### Description
Generates text embeddings using Workers AI embedding models through an OpenAI compatible endpoint.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/embeddings`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **account_id** (string) - Required - Your Cloudflare account ID.
- **gateway_id** (string) - Required - The name of your AI Gateway.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **model** (string) - Required - The Workers AI embedding model ID (e.g., `@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5`).
- **text** (string) - Required - The input text to generate embeddings for.

### Request Example
```bash
curl https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/embeddings \
  --header 'Authorization: Bearer {cf_api_token}' \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --data '{ "model": "@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5", "text": "Cloudflare docs are amazing!" }'
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **data** (array) - An array of embedding objects.
  - **embedding** (array) - The generated embedding vector.
  - **index** (integer) - The index of the embedding.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "data": [
    {
      "embedding": [ 0.00123, -0.00456, ... ],
      "index": 0
    }
  ]
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### Bash: Call Workers AI chat completions endpoint

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/index

This bash script illustrates how to make a POST request to the Cloudflare Workers AI chat completions endpoint using cURL. It includes setting the Authorization header with an API token, specifying the Content-Type as JSON, and providing the request body with the desired model and messages for text generation.

```bash
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/{account_id}/ai/v1/chat/completions \
  --header "Authorization: Bearer {api_token}" \
  --header "Content-Type: application/json" \
  --data ' 
    {
      "model": "@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
      "messages": [
        {
          "role": "user",
          "content": "how to build a wooden spoon in 3 short steps? give as short as answer as possible"
        }
      ]
    }
'
```

--------------------------------

### Call Workers AI Chat Completions via AI Gateway (Bash)

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/ai-gateway/usage/providers/workersai/index

Example using curl to interact with the OpenAI-compatible chat completions endpoint of Workers AI via AI Gateway. Requires an API token and specifies JSON content with messages.

```bash
curl https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/{account_id}/{gateway_id}/workers-ai/v1/chat/completions \
 --header 'Authorization: Bearer {cf_api_token}' \
 --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
 --data '{
      "model": "@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
      "messages": [
        {
          "role": "user",
          "content": "What is Cloudflare?"
        }
      ]
    }
'

```

### Hugging Face Chat UI > Supported models

Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers-ai/configuration/hugging-face-chat-ui/index

The Hugging Face Chat UI template supports any text generation models available through Workers AI that are prefixed with the `@hf` parameter. This allows for flexibility in choosing the models you want to use within the chat interface. You can find a list of supported models on the Cloudflare Workers AI documentation site, enabling you to select the best model for your specific use case.

---
title: Function calling
description: Function calling enables people to take Large Language Models (LLMs) and use the model response to execute functions or interact with external APIs. The developer usually defines a set of functions and the required input schema for each function, which we call tools. The model then intelligently understands when it needs to do a tool call, and it returns a JSON output which the user needs to feed to another function or API.
image: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dev-products-preview.png

1. ### [Workers AI Markdown Conversion: New endpoint to list supported formats](/changelog/post/2025-10-23-new-markdown-conversion-endpoint/)  
[ Workers AI ](/workers-ai/)  
Developers can now programmatically retrieve a list of all file formats supported by the [Markdown Conversion utility](/workers-ai/features/markdown-conversion/) in Workers AI.  
You can use the [env.AI](/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/) binding:  
TypeScript  
```  
await env.AI.toMarkdown().supported()  
```  
Or call the REST API:  
Terminal window  
```  
curl https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/{ACCOUNT_ID}/ai/tomarkdown/supported \  
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer {API_TOKEN}'  
```  
Both return a list of file formats that users can convert into Markdown:  
```  
[  
  {  
    "extension": ".pdf",  
    "mimeType": "application/pdf",  
  },  
  {  
    "extension": ".jpeg",  
    "mimeType": "image/jpeg",  
  },  
  ...  
]  
```  
Learn more about our [Markdown Conversion utility](/workers-ai/features/markdown-conversion/).

# Using AI Models

Agents can call AI models from any provider. [Workers AI](/workers-ai/) is built in and requires no API keys. You can also use [OpenAI ↗](https://platform.openai.com/docs/quickstart?language=javascript), [Anthropic ↗](https://docs.anthropic.com/en/api/client-sdks#typescript), [Google Gemini ↗](https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/openai), or any service that exposes an OpenAI-compatible API.

Mar 20, 2025
1. ### [Markdown conversion in Workers AI](/changelog/post/2025-03-20-markdown-conversion/)  
[ Workers AI ](/workers-ai/)  
Document conversion plays an important role when designing and developing AI applications and agents. Workers AI now provides the `toMarkdown` utility method that developers can use to for quick, easy, and convenient conversion and summary of documents in multiple formats to Markdown language.  
You can call this new tool using a binding by calling `env.AI.toMarkdown()` or the using the [REST API](/api/resources/ai/) endpoint.  
In this example, we fetch a PDF document and an image from R2 and feed them both to `env.AI.toMarkdown()`. The result is a list of converted documents. Workers AI models are used automatically to detect and summarize the image.

## Workers AI テキスト生成モデル

### 推奨モデル（現行）

| モデル | サイズ | 特徴 |
|---|---|---|
| `@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast` | 70B | **現在使用中**。高精度、tool calling対応 |
| `@cf/meta/llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct` | 17B (MoE) | **Llama 4**。最新、高速 |
| `@cf/openai/gpt-oss-120b` | 120B | **GPT-OSS**。大規模、高精度 |
| `@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct` | 8B | 軽量、高速、低コスト |
| `@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-32b` | 32B | 推論特化（CoT） |
| `@cf/google/gemma-3-12b-it` | 12B | Google Gemma 3 |

### Tool calling（function calling）対応について

ドキュメントによると、tool callingは**Beta機能**で、対応モデルは限られています。AI SDK経由で `streamText` + `tools` を使う場合、モデル側のtool calling対応が必要です。

**Text-to-SQL用途**なら、現在の `llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast` が最も安定した選択肢です。**Llama 4 Scout** (`llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct`) も試す価値があります（MoEで高速かつ高精度）。

Copy page

# Pricing

Note

Workers AI has updated pricing to be more granular, with per-model unit-based pricing presented, but still billing in neurons in the back end.

Workers AI is included in both the [Free and Paid Workers plans](/workers/platform/pricing/) and is priced at **$0.011 per 1,000 Neurons**.

Our free allocation allows anyone to use a total of **10,000 Neurons per day at no charge**. To use more than 10,000 Neurons per day, you need to sign up for the [Workers Paid plan](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers). On Workers Paid, you will be charged at $0.011 / 1,000 Neurons for any usage above the free allocation of 10,000 Neurons per day.

You can monitor your Neuron usage in the [Cloudflare Workers AI dashboard ↗](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/ai/workers-ai).

All limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC. If you exceed any one of the above limits, further operations will fail with an error.

| Free  allocation | Pricing                |                               |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Workers Free     | 10,000 Neurons per day | N/A - Upgrade to Workers Paid |
| Workers Paid     | 10,000 Neurons per day | $0.011 / 1,000 Neurons        |

## What are Neurons?

Neurons are our way of measuring AI outputs across different models, representing the GPU compute needed to perform your request. Our serverless model allows you to pay only for what you use without having to worry about renting, managing, or scaling GPUs.

Note

The Price in Tokens column is equivalent to the Price in Neur


## Audio model pricing

| Model                             | Price in Tokens                    | Price in Neurons                         |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |

---
title: Workers AI updated pricing
description: Granular pricing in units and neurons
image: https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog-preview.png
---

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

# Changelog

New updates and improvements at Cloudflare.

[ Subscribe to RSS ](/changelog/rss/index.xml) [ View RSS feeds ](/fundamentals/new-features/available-rss-feeds/) 

![hero image](/_astro/hero.CVYJHPAd_ZEA2nF.svg) 

[ ← Back to all posts ](/changelog/) 

## Workers AI updated pricing

Feb 20, 2025 

[ Workers AI ](/workers-ai/) 

We've updated the Workers AI [pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) to include the latest models and how model usage maps to Neurons.

* Each model's core input format(s) (tokens, audio seconds, images, etc) now include mappings to Neurons, making it easier to understand how your included Neuron volume is consumed and how you are charged at scale
* Per-model pricing, instead of the previous bucket approach, allows us to be more flexible on how models are charged based on their size, performance and capabilities. As we optimize each model, we can then pass on savings for that model.
* You will still only pay for what you consume: Workers AI inference is serverless, and not billed by the hour.

Going forward, models will be launched with their associated Neuron costs, and we'll be updating the Workers AI dashboard and API to reflect consumption in both raw units and Neurons. Visit the [Workers AI pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) page to learn more about Workers AI pricing.
</text>
</result>

[ Workers AI ](/workers-ai/)  
We've updated the Workers AI [pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) to include the latest models and how model usage maps to Neurons.  
   * Each model's core input format(s) (tokens, audio seconds, images, etc) now include mappings to Neurons, making it easier to understand how your included Neuron volume is consumed and how you are charged at scale  
   * Per-model pricing, instead of the previous bucket approach, allows us to be more flexible on how models are charged based on their size, performance and capabilities. As we optimize each model, we can then pass on savings for that model.

Going forward, models will be launched with their associated Neuron costs, and we'll be updating the Workers AI dashboard and API to reflect consumption in both raw units and Neurons. Visit the [Workers AI pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) page to learn more about Workers AI pricing.

title: Pricing
description: Workers plans and pricing information.
image: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dev-products-preview.png

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

  
## Was this helpful?

  
[ Edit](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/edit/production/src/content/docs/workers/platform/pricing.mdx) [ Issue](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/issues/new/choose) 

Copy page

# Pricing

By default, users have access to the Workers Free plan. The Workers Free plan includes limited usage of Workers, Pages Functions, Workers KV and Hyperdrive. Read more about the [Free plan limits](/workers/platform/limits/#worker-limits).

The Workers Paid plan includes Workers, Pages Functions, Workers KV, Hyperdrive, and Durable Objects usage for a minimum charge of $5 USD per month for an account. The plan includes increased initial usage allotments, with clear charges for usage that exceeds the base plan. There are no additional charges for data transfer (egress) or throughput (bandwidth).

All included usage is on a monthly basis.

Pages Functions billing

All [Pages Functions](/pages/functions/) are billed as Workers. All pricing and inclusions in this document apply to Pages Functions. Refer to [Functions Pricing](/pages/functions/pricing/) for more information on Pages Functions pricing.

## Workers

Users on the Workers Paid plan have access to the Standard usage model. Workers Enterprise accounts are billed based on the usage model specified in their contract. To switch to the Standard usage model, contact your Account Manager.


</text>

> AGENT

はい、かかります。比較するとこうです：

| モデル | 料金 | Function Calling |
|---|---|---|
| `llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast` | 有料（Neuron課金） | Yes |
| **`llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct`** | **$0.27/M input, $0.85/M output** | **Yes** |
| `llama-3.1-8b-instruct` | 有料（より安い） | 不明 |

ただし、**全モデル共通で1日10,000 Neurons が無料枠**です（Free/Paid両方）。無料枠を超えた分が $0.011/1,000 Neurons で課金されます。

開発・テスト用途なら無料枠内に収まる可能性が高いです。現在の `llama-3.3-70b` のままでいいですか？それとも Llama 4 Scout に変更しますか？

> DEVELOPER

今のままでいいや。

> AGENT

了解です。`llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast` のままでいきます。コミットしますか？

> DEVELOPER

どうやってテストできる？

> AGENT

テスト方法は3つあります：

### 1. `/health` エンドポイント（curl）
```bash
curl http://localhost:8787/health
```
→ これは動くがエージェント本体ではない

### 2. 簡易HTML（WebSocket）
`AIChatAgent` は WebSocket ベースなので、ブラウザで接続する簡易UIを `public/index.html` に置く方法

### 3. React クライアント（`useAgentChat`）
Agents SDK 公式の React hook を使った本格的なチャットUI

---

手軽にテストするなら **2. 簡易HTML** が一番早いです。`public/index.html` に最小限のチャットUIを作れば、`wrangler dev` でそのまま配信されます。

どれにしますか？

> DEVELOPER

slack で使えるようにして。

> AGENT

Slack Bot として使えるようにします。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Slack Events API** はHTTP POST で来るので、WebSocket ベースの `AIChatAgent` とは別にハンドリングが必要
- Slack向けには `streamText` ではなく `generateText`（非ストリーミング）を使い、完了した回答を Slack API で投稿する
- ツール定義とシステムプロンプトをAgentとSlackで共有する設計にする
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

まずツール定義を共通化して、Slackハンドラーを追加します。

> TOOL

tool_use Write

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/tools.ts",
  "content": "import { tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { D1_SCHEMA } from \"./schema\";\n\nexport const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `あなたは Oura Ring データのアシスタントです。\nユーザーの質問に対して、D1データベース (SQLite) に対するSQLを生成・実行して回答します。\n\n## 利用可能なデータ\nOura Ring から取得した健康データ（睡眠、活動、コンディション、心拍数）\n\n## スキーマ\n${D1_SCHEMA}\n\n## ルール\n- SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE は拒否してください。\n- 日本語で回答してください。\n- v_oura_daily_summary ビューが使える場合はそちらを優先してください。\n- 大量データの取得は LIMIT を付けてください。\n- 結果を簡潔にまとめて回答してください。\n`;\n\nfunction isSafeQuery(sql: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();\n  return normalized.startsWith(\"SELECT\") || normalized.startsWith(\"WITH\");\n}\n\nexport function createTools(db: D1Database) {\n  return {\n    queryD1: tool({\n      description: \"D1データベースに対してSELECTクエリを実行する。読み取り専用。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        sql: z.string().describe(\"実行するSELECTクエリ\"),\n        params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe(\"バインドパラメータ\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {\n        if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {\n          return { success: false, error: \"SELECT/WITH クエリのみ実行可能です。\" };\n        }\n        try {\n          const stmt = db.prepare(sql);\n          const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n          const queryResult = await bound.all();\n\n          return {\n            success: true,\n            rowCount: queryResult.results.length,\n            results: queryResult.results.slice(0, 50),\n          };\n        } catch (error) {\n          return {\n            success: false,\n            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n          };\n        }\n      },\n    }),\n    listTables: tool({\n      description: \"D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({}),\n      execute: async () => {\n        const queryResult = await db\n          .prepare(\n            \"SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name\"\n          )\n          .all();\n        return queryResult.results;\n      },\n    }),\n  };\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "import { AIChatAgent } from \"@cloudflare/ai-chat\";\nimport { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { SYSTEM_PROMPT, createTools } from \"./tools\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });\n\n    const result = streamText({\n      model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),\n      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,\n      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),\n      tools: createTools(this.env.DB),\n    });\n\n    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();\n  }\n}\n"
}
