```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/slack.ts",
  "content": "import { generateText, stepCountIs } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { SYSTEM_PROMPT, createTools } from \"./tools\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\ntype SlackEvent =\n  | { type: \"url_verification\"; challenge: string }\n  | {\n      type: \"event_callback\";\n      event: {\n        type: string;\n        text?: string;\n        user?: string;\n        channel?: string;\n        bot_id?: string;\n        ts?: string;\n        thread_ts?: string;\n      };\n    };\n\nasync function verifySlackSignature(\n  request: Request,\n  body: string,\n  signingSecret: string\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const timestamp = request.headers.get(\"x-slack-request-timestamp\");\n  const signature = request.headers.get(\"x-slack-signature\");\n  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;\n\n  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes\n  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;\n\n  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n    \"raw\",\n    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),\n    { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    false,\n    [\"sign\"]\n  );\n  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));\n  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;\n}\n\nasync function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {\n  await fetch(\"https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      channel,\n      text,\n      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),\n    }),\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n  const body = await request.text();\n\n  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {\n    return new Response(\"Invalid signature\", { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);\n\n  // Slack URL verification challenge\n  if (event.type === \"url_verification\") {\n    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"text/plain\" } });\n  }\n\n  if (event.type !== \"event_callback\") {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  const { event: slackEvent } = event;\n\n  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops\n  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== \"app_mention\") {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, \"\").trim();\n  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  // Respond asynchronously — Slack expects 200 within 3 seconds\n  const ctx = { waitUntil: (p: Promise<unknown>) => p };\n  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;\n\n  // Use waitUntil pattern via global ExecutionContext\n  const promise = (async () => {\n    try {\n      const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });\n      const { text } = await generateText({\n        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n        system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n        prompt: userText,\n        stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),\n        tools: createTools(env.DB),\n      });\n\n      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, slackEvent.channel!, text, threadTs);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"Slack handler error:\", error);\n      await postSlackMessage(\n        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,\n        slackEvent.channel!,\n        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,\n        threadTs\n      );\n    }\n  })();\n\n  // Return immediately, process in background\n  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);\n\n  return new Response(\"OK\");\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/index.ts",
  "content": "import { routeAgentRequest } from \"agents\";\nimport { handleSlackEvent } from \"./slack\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nexport { DataAgent } from \"./agent\";\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n\n    if (url.pathname === \"/health\") {\n      return new Response(\"OK\");\n    }\n\n    // Slack Events API\n    if (url.pathname === \"/slack/events\" && request.method === \"POST\") {\n      // Pass waitUntil so Slack handler can process AI response in background\n      globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL = (p: Promise<unknown>) => ctx.waitUntil(p);\n      return handleSlackEvent(request, env);\n    }\n\n    // Agent WebSocket routing\n    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);\n    if (agentResponse) {\n      return agentResponse;\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n"
}

> AGENT

正常に起動しました。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Slack は3秒以内にHTTP 200を返す必要がある**。AI推論は数秒以上かかるため、`ctx.waitUntil()` でバックグラウンド処理し、即座に200を返す設計
- **`app_mention` イベント**を使うことで、`@bot名` でメンションされた時だけ反応。`message` イベントだと全メッセージに反応してしまう
- **署名検証**: `x-slack-signature` ヘッダーで HMAC-SHA256 検証を行い、リクエストが本当にSlackから来たものか確認
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## Slack App セットアップ手順

デプロイ後に以下の設定が必要です：

1. **[Slack API](https://api.slack.com/apps) で App を作成**
2. **OAuth & Permissions** → Bot Token Scopes に追加:
   - `app_mentions:read`
   - `chat:write`
3. **Event Subscriptions** を有効化:
   - Request URL: `https://data-agent.<your-subdomain>.workers.dev/slack/events`
   - Subscribe to: `app_mention`
4. **Bot をワークスペースにインストール** → Bot Token を取得
5. **シークレットを設定**:
   ```bash
   cd agents
   wrangler secret put SLACK_BOT_TOKEN      # xoxb-... 
   wrangler secret put SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET  # Basic Information > Signing Secret
   ```

from a manifest と from scratch どちらで作り始めればいい？

> AGENT

**From a manifest** がおすすめです。

`manifest.json` を用意すれば、OAuth スコープ・イベント購読・Bot名などを一括で設定できます。手動で画面ポチポチする必要がなくなります。

> AGENT

入ってますね。ただし Slack CLI (`slack v3`) は **Slack の次世代プラットフォーム（Deno ベース）** 向けで、外部URL（Cloudflare Workers）にイベントを送る従来型のアプリとは仕組みが違います。

**Slack CLI でできること:**
- `slack manifest` でマニフェストを生成・管理
- `slack app create` でアプリ作成

**ただし注意:**
- Slack CLI の次世代アプリは Slack 側でホスティングされる前提
- 外部URL（Workers）にイベントを飛ばす「HTTP API型」アプリは **Web UI or manifest.json** で作るのが確実

---

選択肢としては：

1. **`slack app create --manifest` でマニフェストから作成** → これなら CLI から一発で作れる
2. **Web UI で From a manifest** → 確実

> AGENT

マニフェストを作りました。手順は以下です：

### 1. Slack CLI にログイン
```bash
slack login
```

### 2. アプリ作成
```bash
slack app create --manifest agents/slack-manifest.json --org-workspace
```

または **Slack CLI が外部URL型アプリに対応していない場合**は、Web UI で：

1. https://api.slack.com/apps → **Create New App** → **From a manifest**
2. ワークスペースを選択
3. `agents/slack-manifest.json` の中身を貼り付け
4. **Create** → **Install to Workspace**

### 3. デプロイ＆シークレット設定
```bash
cd agents
wrangler deploy --minify
wrangler secret put SLACK_BOT_TOKEN        # OAuth > Bot User OAuth Token (xoxb-...)
wrangler secret put SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET   # Basic Information > Signing Secret
```

> **注意**: `request_url` の `ta93abe` はあなたの Workers サブドメインに合わせてください。デプロイ後、Slack がURL検証（`url_verification`）チャレンジを送信し、自動で通過します。

> AGENT

Slack CLI v3 はマニフェストからの直接作成に対応していません。**Web UI** で作りましょう。

### 手順

1. **https://api.slack.com/apps** を開く
2. **Create New App** → **From a manifest**
3. ワークスペースを選択
4. **JSON** タブで以下を貼り付け：

```json
{
  "_metadata": {
    "major_version": 2,
    "minor_version": 1
  },
  "display_information": {
    "name": "Data Agent",
    "description": "Oura Ring データに対する自然言語クエリ Bot",
    "background_color": "#f38020"
  },
  "features": {
    "bot_user": {
      "display_name": "Data Agent",
      "always_online": true
    }
  },
  "oauth_config": {
    "scopes": {
      "bot": ["app_mentions:read", "chat:write"]
    }
  },
  "settings": {
    "event_subscriptions": {
      "request_url": "https://data-agent.ta93abe.workers.dev/slack/events",
      "bot_events": ["app_mention"]
    },
    "org_deploy_enabled": false,
    "socket_mode_enabled": false,
    "token_rotation_enabled": false
  }
}
```

5. **Create** → **Install to Workspace** → **Allow**
6. 2つのシークレットを取得：
   - **OAuth & Permissions** → `Bot User OAuth Token` (xoxb-...)
   - **Basic Information** → `Signing Secret`

取得できたら教えてください。`wrangler secret put` で設定します。

> AGENT

スクリーンショットの **App Credentials** セクションにある項目のうち、必要なのは以下の2つです：

### 1. Signing Secret（このページにある）
「**Signing Secret**」の横にある「**Show**」ボタンをクリックして表示される値をコピーしてください。

### 2. Bot User OAuth Token（別のページ）
左サイドバーの「**OAuth & Permissions**」をクリック → 「**Bot User OAuth Token**」（`xoxb-` で始まる値）をコピーしてください。

---

両方をコピーしたら、以下のコマンドで Workers に設定します：

```bash
cd agents
wrangler secret put SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET
# → Signing Secret の値を貼り付け

wrangler secret put SLACK_BOT_TOKEN
# → Bot User OAuth Token (xoxb-...) を貼り付け
```

設定が完了したら教えてください。Slack でテストしましょう。

     8→  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
     9→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    10→
    11→    if (url.pathname === "/health") {
    12→      return new Response("OK");
    13→    }
    14→
    15→    // Slack Events API
    16→    if (url.pathname === "/slack/events" && request.method === "POST") {
    17→      // Pass waitUntil so Slack handler can process AI response in background
    18→      globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL = (p: Promise<unknown>) => ctx.waitUntil(p);
    19→      return handleSlackEvent(request, env);
    20→    }
    21→
    22→    // Agent WebSocket routing

    26→    }
    27→
    28→    return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    29→  },

> AGENT

Event Subscriptions が Verified になったということは、Worker へのリクエストは届いています。

あとは：

1. **Install App** — 左サイドバー「Install App」でワークスペースにインストール済みか確認
2. **チャンネルにボット招待** — メンションしたいチャンネルで `/invite @Data Agent`

この2つを確認して、もう一度 `@Data Agent テスト` とメンションしてみてください。

> DEVELOPER

返信来た。

> AGENT

動きましたね！

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Slack アプリの3ステップ: **作成 → インストール → チャンネル招待**。マニフェストで作成しただけではイベントは届かない
- `ctx.waitUntil()` パターンにより、Slack の3秒タイムアウト制限内で200を返しつつ、AI処理はバックグラウンドで実行
- `generateText`（非ストリーミング）を Slack 用に、`streamText`（ストリーミング）を WebSocket チャット用に使い分けている
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

次のステップとして：
- **コミット＆PR更新** — agents のコードを現在のブランチにコミットするか、新しいブランチを作るか
- **テストを色々試す** — 「昨日の睡眠スコアは？」「最近1週間のアクティビティを見せて」など

どうしますか？

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
     4→
     5→export const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `あなたは Oura Ring データのアシスタントです。
     6→ユーザーの質問に対して、D1データベース (SQLite) に対するSQLを生成・実行して回答します。
     7→
     8→## 利用可能なデータ
     9→Oura Ring から取得した健康データ（睡眠、活動、コンディション、心拍数）
    10→
    11→## スキーマ
    12→${D1_SCHEMA}
    13→
    14→## ルール
    15→- SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE は拒否してください。
    16→- 日本語で回答してください。
    17→- v_oura_daily_summary ビューが使える場合はそちらを優先してください。
    18→- 大量データの取得は LIMIT を付けてください。
    19→- 結果を簡潔にまとめて回答してください。
    20→`;
    21→
    22→function isSafeQuery(sql: string): boolean {
    23→  const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();
    24→  return normalized.startsWith("SELECT") || normalized.startsWith("WITH");
    25→}
    26→
    27→export function createTools(db: D1Database) {
    28→  return {
    29→    queryD1: tool({
    30→      description: "D1データベースに対してSELECTクエリを実行する。読み取り専用。",
    31→      inputSchema: z.object({
    32→        sql: z.string().describe("実行するSELECTクエリ"),
    33→        params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("バインドパラメータ"),
    34→      }),
    35→      execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {
    36→        if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
    37→          return { success: false, error: "SELECT/WITH クエリのみ実行可能です。" };
    38→        }
    39→        try {
    40→          const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
    41→          const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;
    42→          const queryResult = await bound.all();
    43→
    44→          return {
    45→            success: true,
    46→            rowCount: queryResult.results.length,
    47→            results: queryResult.results.slice(0, 50),
    48→          };
    49→        } catch (error) {
    50→          return {
    51→            success: false,
    52→            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    53→          };
    54→        }
    55→      },
    56→    }),
    57→    listTables: tool({
    58→      description: "D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する",
    59→      inputSchema: z.object({}),
    60→      execute: async () => {
    61→        const queryResult = await db
    62→          .prepare(
    63→            "SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name"
    64→          )
    65→          .all();
    66→        return queryResult.results;
    67→      },
    68→    }),
    69→  };
    70→}
    71→
```

- Llama などの OSS モデルは、System Prompt が日本語だとツール呼び出しの指示を無視しやすい
- System Prompt を英語にし、具体例（Example）を含めることでツール使用率が大幅に向上する
- 「Respond in Japanese」でユーザーへの回答のみ日本語にする

     1→import { generateText, stepCountIs } from "ai";
     2→import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
     3→import { createTools, SYSTEM_PROMPT } from "./tools";
     4→import type { Env } from "./types";
     5→
     6→const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
     7→
     8→type SlackEvent =
     9→  | { type: "url_verification"; challenge: string }
    10→  | {
    11→      type: "event_callback";
    12→      event: {
    13→        type: string;
    14→        text?: string;
    15→        user?: string;
    16→        channel?: string;
    17→        bot_id?: string;
    18→        ts?: string;
    19→        thread_ts?: string;
    20→      };
    21→    };
    22→
    23→async function verifySlackSignature(
    24→  request: Request,
    25→  body: string,
    26→  signingSecret: string
    27→): Promise<boolean> {
    28→  const timestamp = request.headers.get("x-slack-request-timestamp");
    29→  const signature = request.headers.get("x-slack-signature");
    30→  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;
    31→
    32→  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes
    33→  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;
    34→
    35→  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;
    36→  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    37→    "raw",
    38→    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),
    39→    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    40→    false,
    41→    ["sign"]
    42→  );
    43→  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));
    44→  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    45→  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;
    46→}
    47→
    48→async function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {
    49→  await fetch("https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage", {
    50→    method: "POST",
    51→    headers: {
    52→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    53→      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    54→    },
    55→    body: JSON.stringify({
    56→      channel,
    57→      text,
    58→      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),
    59→    }),
    60→  });
    61→}
    62→
    63→export async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    64→  const body = await request.text();
    65→
    66→  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {
    67→    return new Response("Invalid signature", { status: 401 });
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);
    71→
    72→  // Slack URL verification challenge
    73→  if (event.type === "url_verification") {
    74→    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" } });
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  if (event.type !== "event_callback") {
    78→    return new Response("OK");
    79→  }
    80→
    81→  const { event: slackEvent } = event;
    82→
    83→  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops
    84→  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== "app_mention") {
    85→    return new Response("OK");
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, "").trim();
    89→  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {
    90→    return new Response("OK");
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  // Respond asynchronously — Slack expects 200 within 3 seconds
    94→  const channel = slackEvent.channel;
    95→  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;
    96→
    97→  // Use waitUntil pattern via global ExecutionContext
    98→  const promise = (async () => {
    99→    try {
   100→      const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
   101→      const { text } = await generateText({
   102→        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
   103→        system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
   104→        prompt: userText,
   105→        stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
   106→        tools: createTools(env.DB),
   107→        toolChoice: "auto",
   108→        maxRetries: 0,
   109→      });
   110→
   111→      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, channel, text, threadTs);
   112→    } catch (error) {
   113→      console.error("Slack handler error:", error);
   114→      await postSlackMessage(
   115→        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
   116→        channel,
   117→        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
   118→        threadTs
   119→      );
   120→    }
   121→  })();
   122→
   123→  // Return immediately, process in background
   124→  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);
   125→
   126→  return new Response("OK");
   127→}
   128→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/slack.ts",
  "content": "import { generateText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { D1_SCHEMA } from \"./schema\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\nconst SQL_GEN_PROMPT = `You are a SQL generator for a SQLite (D1) database containing Oura Ring health data.\nGiven a user question, output ONLY a valid SELECT SQL query. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks. Just the raw SQL.\n\nDatabase schema:\n${D1_SCHEMA}\n\nSQL hints:\n- \"yesterday\" = date('now', '-1 day')\n- \"this week\" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')\n- \"this month\" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')\n- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view\n- Always add LIMIT 30\n- Date columns are TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD format)\n\nOutput ONLY the SQL query. Nothing else.`;\n\nconst SUMMARIZE_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant. Given the user's question and query results from an Oura Ring database, provide a concise answer in Japanese.\nIf no data is returned, say \"データが見つかりませんでした。\"\nKeep the answer short and helpful.`;\n\ntype SlackEvent =\n  | { type: \"url_verification\"; challenge: string }\n  | {\n      type: \"event_callback\";\n      event: {\n        type: string;\n        text?: string;\n        user?: string;\n        channel?: string;\n        bot_id?: string;\n        ts?: string;\n        thread_ts?: string;\n      };\n    };\n\nasync function verifySlackSignature(\n  request: Request,\n  body: string,\n  signingSecret: string\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const timestamp = request.headers.get(\"x-slack-request-timestamp\");\n  const signature = request.headers.get(\"x-slack-signature\");\n  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;\n\n  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes\n  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;\n\n  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n    \"raw\",\n    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),\n    { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    false,\n    [\"sign\"]\n  );\n  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));\n  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;\n}\n\nasync function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {\n  await fetch(\"https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      channel,\n      text,\n      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),\n    }),\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isSafeQuery(sql: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = sql.trim().toUpperCase();\n  return normalized.startsWith(\"SELECT\") || normalized.startsWith(\"WITH\");\n}\n\nfunction extractSql(raw: string): string {\n  // Strip markdown code blocks if present\n  let sql = raw.replace(/```sql\\n?/gi, \"\").replace(/```\\n?/g, \"\").trim();\n  // Take only first statement\n  const semicolonIdx = sql.indexOf(\";\");\n  if (semicolonIdx > 0) {\n    sql = sql.substring(0, semicolonIdx + 1);\n  }\n  return sql;\n}\n\nexport async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n  const body = await request.text();\n\n  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {\n    return new Response(\"Invalid signature\", { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);\n\n  // Slack URL verification challenge\n  if (event.type === \"url_verification\") {\n    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"text/plain\" } });\n  }\n\n  if (event.type !== \"event_callback\") {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  const { event: slackEvent } = event;\n\n  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops\n  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== \"app_mention\") {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, \"\").trim();\n  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {\n    return new Response(\"OK\");\n  }\n\n  const channel = slackEvent.channel;\n  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;\n\n  const promise = (async () => {\n    try {\n      const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });\n\n      // Step 1: Generate SQL from user question\n      const { text: rawSql } = await generateText({\n        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n        system: SQL_GEN_PROMPT,\n        prompt: userText,\n      });\n\n      const sql = extractSql(rawSql);\n\n      if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {\n        await postSlackMessage(\n          env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,\n          channel,\n          \"SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。\",\n          threadTs\n        );\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: Execute SQL\n      const queryResult = await env.DB.prepare(sql).all();\n      const rows = queryResult.results.slice(0, 50);\n\n      // Step 3: Summarize results\n      const { text: answer } = await generateText({\n        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n        system: SUMMARIZE_PROMPT,\n        prompt: `ユーザーの質問: ${userText}\\n\\n実行したSQL: ${sql}\\n\\nクエリ結果 (${queryResult.results.length}件):\\n${JSON.stringify(rows, null, 2)}`,\n      });\n\n      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, channel, answer, threadTs);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"Slack handler error:\", error);\n      await postSlackMessage(\n        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,\n        channel,\n        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,\n        threadTs\n      );\n    }\n  })();\n\n  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);\n\n  return new Response(\"OK\");\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **2段階プロンプト方式**: ツール呼び出しに依存せず、Step 1 で SQL 生成 → Step 2 で結果要約
- OSS モデル（Llama等）はツール呼び出しの実装が不安定なことがある。プロンプトベースの方が確実
- SQL 生成プロンプトで「Output ONLY the SQL query. Nothing else.」と厳密に指示し、`extractSql` で余計な出力を除去
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

デプロイ完了。`@Data Agent 昨日の睡眠スコアを教えて` を再度お試しください。

package main

import (
	"github.com/pulumi/pulumi/sdk/v3/go/pulumi"
	"github.com/pulumi/pulumi/sdk/v3/go/pulumi/config"
)

func main() {
	pulumi.Run(func(ctx *pulumi.Context) error {
		cfg := config.New(ctx, "")
		accountId := cfg.Require("accountId")

		cf, err := createCloudflareResources(ctx, accountId)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}

		sf, err := createSnowflakeResources(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}

		// ===========================================
		// Outputs
		// ===========================================
		ctx.Export("d1DatabaseId", cf.D1DatabaseId)
		ctx.Export("d1DatabaseName", cf.D1DatabaseName)
		ctx.Export("r2BucketName", cf.R2BucketName)
		ctx.Export("kvNamespaceId", cf.KvNamespaceId)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeDatabaseName", sf.DatabaseName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeSchemaName", sf.SchemaName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeWarehouseName", sf.WarehouseName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeAdminDatabaseName", sf.AdminDatabaseName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeCoreDatabaseName", sf.CoreDatabaseName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeDbtRoleName", sf.DbtRoleName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeDbtServiceUserName", sf.DbtServiceUserName)
		ctx.Export("snowflakeGitRepositoryName", sf.GitRepositoryName)

		return nil
	})
}
```

```
-- ============================================
-- OAuth2 Token Storage
-- ============================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oauth_tokens (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    access_token TEXT NOT NULL,
    refresh_token TEXT NOT NULL,
    token_type TEXT DEFAULT 'Bearer',
    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    scope TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================
-- Oura Ring Tables
-- ============================================

-- Daily Sleep
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_sleep (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    score INTEGER,
    timestamp TEXT,
    -- contributors
    deep_sleep INTEGER,
    efficiency INTEGER,
    latency INTEGER,
    rem_sleep INTEGER,
    restfulness INTEGER,
    timing INTEGER,
    total_sleep INTEGER,
    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- Daily Activity
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_activity (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    score INTEGER,
    active_calories INTEGER,
    total_calories INTEGER,
    steps INTEGER,
    equivalent_walking_distance REAL,
    high_activity_time INTEGER,
    medium_activity_time INTEGER,
    low_activity_time INTEGER,
    sedentary_time INTEGER,
    resting_time INTEGER,
    met_average REAL,
    -- contributors
    meet_daily_targets INTEGER,
    move_every_hour INTEGER,
    recovery_time INTEGER,
    stay_active INTEGER,
    training_frequency INTEGER,
    training_volume INTEGER,
    timestamp TEXT,
    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- Daily Readiness
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_readiness (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    score INTEGER,
    temperature_deviation REAL,
    temperature_trend_deviation REAL,
    timestamp TEXT,
    -- contributors
    activity_balance INTEGER,
    body_temperature INTEGER,
    hrv_balance INTEGER,
    previous_day_activity INTEGER,
    previous_night INTEGER,
    recovery_index INTEGER,
    resting_heart_rate INTEGER,
    sleep_balance INTEGER,
    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- Heart Rate (high volume: ~288 records/day at 5min intervals)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_heart_rate (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    bpm INTEGER NOT NULL,
    source TEXT,
    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
    day TEXT NOT NULL,
    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================
-- Oura Indexes
-- ============================================

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_sleep_day ON oura_daily_sleep(day DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_activity_day ON oura_daily_activity(day DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_readiness_day ON oura_daily_readiness(day DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_day ON oura_heart_rate(day DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_timestamp ON oura_heart_rate(timestamp DESC);

-- ============================================
-- Oura Views
-- ============================================

CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_oura_daily_summary AS
SELECT
    s.day,
    s.score AS sleep_score,
    a.score AS activity_score,
    r.score AS readiness_score,
    a.steps,
    a.total_calories,
    a.active_calories,
    r.temperature_deviation,
    s.deep_sleep AS sleep_deep_sleep,
    s.efficiency AS sleep_efficiency,
    s.total_sleep AS sleep_total_sleep
FROM oura_daily_sleep s
LEFT JOIN oura_daily_activity a ON s.day = a.day
LEFT JOIN oura_daily_readiness r ON s.day = r.day
ORDER BY s.day DESC;

-- ============================================
-- Initial Data
-- ============================================

**D1 → R2 (Iceberg) のデータエクスポートについて、最初の質問です：**

対象データはどの範囲ですか？

1. **Oura Ring データのみ** — 睡眠・活動・コンディション・心拍数の4テーブル
2. **D1 全テーブル** — Oura + Linear + Withings + メタデータテーブル

**D1 → R2 のデータパイプラインをどう動かしますか？**

1. **Cron Trigger (定期実行)** — Workers の Cron で毎日/毎時自動エクスポート
2. **手動トリガー** — API エンドポイントを叩いて実行
3. **両方** — Cron で定期実行 + 手動でもキック可能

**Iceberg テーブルのデータファイル形式について：**

Iceberg のデータファイルは通常 Parquet ですが、Workers の CPU/メモリ制限（128MB / CPU 30秒）を考慮すると：

1. **Parquet** — 標準的だが、Workers 上で生成するには parquet-wasm 等が必要。サイズ・性能面で最適
2. **JSON (newline-delimited)** — Workers で簡単に生成可能。Iceberg は JSON データファイルもサポート。Parquet より非効率だが実装がシンプル
3. **最初は JSON で始めて、後で Parquet に移行** — 段階的アプローチ

どれがいいですか？

> DEVELOPER

1

> AGENT

**Iceberg メタデータの管理について：**

Iceberg は metadata JSON + manifest + manifest list を管理する必要があります。これを Workers 上でどこまで実装するかの方針です：

1. **最小限の Iceberg 実装** — metadata.json, manifest-list, manifest を自前で生成。読み取りは外部ツール（Spark, DuckDB, Snowflake等）に任せる
2. **REST Catalog 付き** — Iceberg REST Catalog API も Workers で実装し、Spark/Trino 等から直接カタログ経由でアクセス可能にする

1 で始めるのを推奨します。R2 上に正しい Iceberg フォーマットのファイルを置くことに集中し、カタログは後から追加できます。

> DEVELOPER

r2 data catalog 使いたい。

R2 Data Catalog は Cloudflare が提供する Iceberg REST Catalog 機能ですね。これなら Iceberg メタデータ管理を自前で実装する必要がなくなります。

まず最新の情報を確認させてください。

# Changelog

New updates and improvements at Cloudflare.

[ Subscribe to RSS ](/changelog/rss/index.xml) [ View RSS feeds ](/fundamentals/new-features/available-rss-feeds/) 

![hero image](/_astro/hero.CVYJHPAd_ZEA2nF.svg) 

[ ← Back to all posts ](/changelog/) 

## R2 Data Catalog is a managed Apache Iceberg data catalog built directly into R2 buckets

Apr 10, 2025 

[ R2 ](/r2/) 

Today, we're launching [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/) in open beta, a managed Apache Iceberg catalog built directly into your [Cloudflare R2](/r2/) bucket.

If you're not already familiar with it, [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) is an open table format designed to handle large-scale analytics datasets stored in object storage, offering ACID transactions and schema evolution. R2 Data Catalog exposes a standard Iceberg REST catalog interface, so you can connect engines like [Spark](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/spark-scala/), [Snowflake](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/snowflake/), and [PyIceberg](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/pyiceberg/) to start querying your tables using the tools you already know.

To enable a data catalog on your R2 bucket, find **R2 Data Catalog** in your buckets settings in the dashboard, or run:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable my-bucket


```

And that's it. You'll get a catalog URI and warehouse you can plug into your favorite Iceberg engines.

Visit our [getting started guide](/r2/data-catalog/get-started/) for step-by-step instructions on enabling R2 Data Catalog, creating tables, and running your first queries.

---
title: Manage catalogs
description: Understand how to manage Iceberg REST catalogs associated with R2 buckets
image: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dev-products-preview.png
---

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

  
## Was this helpful?

  
[ Edit](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/edit/production/src/content/docs/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs.mdx) [ Issue](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/issues/new/choose) 

Copy page

# Manage catalogs

Learn how to:

* Enable and disable [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/) on your buckets.
* Enable and disable [table maintenance](/r2/data-catalog/table-maintenance/) features like compaction and snapshot expiration.
* Authenticate Iceberg engines using API tokens.

## Enable R2 Data Catalog on a bucket

Enabling the catalog on a bucket turns on the REST catalog interface and provides a **Catalog URI** and **Warehouse name** required by Iceberg clients. Once enabled, you can create and manage Iceberg tables in that bucket.

* [ Dashboard ](#tab-panel-3254)
* [ Wrangler CLI ](#tab-panel-3255)

1. In the Cloudflare dashboard, go to the **R2 object storage** page.  
[ Go to **Overview** ](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/r2/overview)
2. Select the bucket you want to enable as a data catalog.
3. Switch to the **Settings** tab, scroll down to **R2 Data Catalog**, and select **Enable**.
4. Once enabled, note the **Catalog URI** and **Warehouse name**.

To enable the catalog on your bucket, run the [r2 bucket catalog enable command](/workers/wrangler/commands/#r2-bucket-catalog-enable):

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable <BUCKET_NAME>


```

After enabling, Wrangler will return your catalog URI and warehouse name.

## Disable R2 Data Catalog on a bucket

When you disable the catalog on a bucket, it immediately stops serving requests from the catalog interface. Any Iceberg table references stored in that catalog become inaccessible until you re-enable it.

* [ Dashboard ](#tab-panel-3256)
* [ Wrangler CLI ](#tab-panel-3257



# Disable snapshot expiration for a specific table

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration disable <BUCKET_NAME> <NAMESPACE> <TABLE>


```

## Authenticate your Iceberg engine

To connect your Iceberg engine to R2 Data Catalog, you must provide a Cloudflare API token with **both** R2 Data Catalog permissions and R2 storage permissions. Iceberg engines interact with R2 Data Catalog to perform table operations. The catalog also provides engines with SigV4 credentials, which are required to access the underlying data files stored in R2.

### Create API token in the dashboard

Create an [R2 API token](/r2/api/tokens/#permissions) with **Admin Read & Write** or **Admin Read only** permissions. These permissions include both:

* Access to R2 Data Catalog (read-only or read/write, depending on chosen permission)
* Access to R2 storage (read-only or read/write, depending on chosen permission)

Providing the resulting token value to your Iceberg engine gives it the ability to manage catalog metadata and handle data operations (reads or writes to R2).

### Create API token via API

To create an API token programmatically for use with R2 Data Catalog, you'll need to specify both R2 Data Catalog and R2 storage permission groups in your [Access Policy](/r2/api/tokens/#access-policy).

#### Example Access Policy

```

[

  {

    "id": "f267e341f3dd4697bd3b9f71dd96247f",

    "effect": "allow",

    "resources": {

      "com.cloudflare.edge.r2.bucket.4793d734c0b8e484dfc37ec392b5fa8a_default_my-bucket": "*",

      "com.cloudflare.edge.r2.bucket.4793d734c0b8e484dfc37ec392b5fa8a_eu_my-eu-bucket": "*"

    },

    "permission_groups": [

      {

        "id": "d229766a2f7f4d299f20eaa8c9b1fde9",

        "name": "Workers R2 Data Catalog Write"

      },

      {

        "id": "2efd5506f9c8494dacb1fa10a3e7d5b6",

        "name": "Workers R2 Storage Bucket Item Write"

      }

    ]

  }

]


```

To learn more about how to create API tokens for R2 Data Catalog using the API, including required permission groups a



# Disable compaction for a specific table

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction disable <BUCKET_NAME> <NAMESPACE> <TABLE>


```

## Enable snapshot expiration

Snapshot expiration automatically removes old table snapshots to reduce metadata bloat and storage costs. For more information about snapshot expiration and why it is valuable, refer to [Table maintenance](/r2/data-catalog/table-maintenance/).

Note

Snapshot expiration commands are available as of Wrangler version 4.56.0.

To enable snapshot expiration on your catalog, run the [r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable command](/workers/wrangler/commands/#r2-bucket-catalog-snapshot-expiration-enable):

Terminal window

```

# Enable catalog-level snapshot expiration (all tables)

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable <BUCKET_NAME> \

  --token <API_TOKEN> \

  --older-than-days 7 \

  --retain-last 10


# Enable snapshot expiration for a specific table

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable <BUCKET_NAME> <NAMESPACE> <TABLE> \

  --older-than-days 2 \

  --retain-last 5


```

## Disable snapshot expiration

Disabling snapshot expiration prevents the process from running for all tables (catalog level) or a specific table (table level). You can re-enable snapshot expiration at any time.

Terminal window

```

# Disable catalog-level snapshot expiration (all tables)

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration disable <BUCKET_NAME>


# Disable snapshot expiration for a specific table

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration disable <BUCKET_NAME> <NAMESPACE> <TABLE>


```

# R2 Data Catalog

Note

R2 Data Catalog is in **public beta**, and any developer with an [R2 subscription](/r2/pricing/) can start using it. Currently, outside of standard R2 storage and operations, you will not be billed for your use of R2 Data Catalog.

R2 Data Catalog is a managed [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) data catalog built directly into your R2 bucket. It exposes a standard Iceberg REST catalog interface, so you can connect the engines you already use, like [Spark](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/spark-scala/), [Snowflake](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/snowflake/), and [PyIceberg](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/pyiceberg/).

R2 Data Catalog makes it easy to turn an R2 bucket into a data warehouse or lakehouse for a variety of analytical workloads including log analytics, business intelligence, and data pipelines. R2's zero-egress fee model means that data users and consumers can access and analyze data from different clouds, data platforms, or regions without incurring transfer costs.

To get started with R2 Data Catalog, refer to the [R2 Data Catalog: Getting started](/r2/data-catalog/get-started/).

## What is Apache Iceberg?

[Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) is an open table format designed to handle large-scale analytics datasets stored in object storage. Key features include:

* ACID transactions - Ensures reliable, concurrent reads and writes with full data integrity.
* Optimized metadata - Avoids costly full table scans by using indexed metadata for faster queries.
* Full schema evolution - Allows adding, renaming, and dele
</text>

Copy page

# R2 Data Catalog

R2 Data Catalog sinks write processed data from pipelines as [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables to [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). Iceberg tables provide ACID transactions, schema evolution, and time travel capabilities for analytics workloads.

To create an R2 Data Catalog sink, run the [pipelines sinks create](/workers/wrangler/commands/#pipelines-sinks-create) command and specify the sink type, target bucket, namespace, and table name:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler pipelines sinks create my-sink \

  --type r2-data-catalog \

  --bucket my-bucket \

  --namespace my_namespace \

  --table my_table \

  --catalog-token YOUR_CATALOG_TOKEN


```

The sink will create the specified namespace and table if they do not exist. Sinks cannot be created for existing Iceberg tables.

## Format

R2 Data Catalog sinks only support Parquet format. JSON format is not supported for Iceberg tables.

### Compression options

Configure Parquet compression for optimal storage and query performance:

Terminal window

```

--compression zstd


```

**Available compression options:**

* `zstd` (default) - Best compression ratio
* `snappy` \- Fastest compression
* `gzip` \- Good compression, widely supported
* `lz4` \- Fast compression with reasonable ratio
* `uncompressed` \- No compression

### Row group size

[Row groups ↗](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/configurations/) are sets of rows in a Parquet file that are stored together, affecting memory usage and query performance. Configure the target row group size in MB:

Term

# Getting started

This guide will instruct you through:

* Creating your first [R2 bucket](/r2/buckets/) and enabling its [data catalog](/r2/data-catalog/).
* Creating an [API token](/r2/api/tokens/) needed for query engines to authenticate with your data catalog.
* Using [PyIceberg ↗](https://py.iceberg.apache.org/) to create your first Iceberg table in a [marimo ↗](https://marimo.io/) Python notebook.
* Using [PyIceberg ↗](https://py.iceberg.apache.org/) to load sample data into your table and query it.

## Prerequisites

1. Sign up for a [Cloudflare account ↗](https://dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up/workers-and-pages).
2. Install [Node.js ↗](https://docs.npmjs.com/downloading-and-installing-node-js-and-npm).

Node.js version manager

Use a Node version manager like [Volta ↗](https://volta.sh/) or [nvm ↗](https://github.com/nvm-sh/nvm) to avoid permission issues and change Node.js versions. [Wrangler](/workers/wrangler/install-and-update/), discussed later in this guide, requires a Node version of `16.17.0` or later.

## 1\. Create an R2 bucket

* [ Wrangler CLI ](#tab-panel-3246)
* [ Dashboard ](#tab-panel-3247)

1. If not already logged in, run:  
```  
npx wrangler login  
```
2. Create an R2 bucket:  
```  
npx wrangler r2 bucket create r2-data-catalog-tutorial  
```

1. In the Cloudflare dashboard, go to the **R2 object storage** page.  
[ Go to **Overview** ](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/r2/overview)
2. Select **Create bucket**.
3. Enter the bucket name: r2-data-catalog-tutorial
4. Select **Create bucket**.

## 2\.

# Sinks

Sinks define destinations for your data in Cloudflare Pipelines. They support writing to [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/) as Apache Iceberg tables or to [R2](/r2/) as raw JSON or Parquet files.

# R2 Data Catalog

R2 Data Catalog sinks write processed data from pipelines as [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables to [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). Iceberg tables provide ACID transactions, schema evolution, and time travel capabilities for analytics workloads.

To create an R2 Data Catalog sink, run the [pipelines sinks create](/workers/wrangler/commands/#pipelines-sinks-create) command and specify the sink type, target bucket, namespace, and table name:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler pipelines sinks create my-sink \

  --type r2-data-catalog \

  --bucket my-bucket \

  --namespace my_namespace \

  --table my_table \

  --catalog-token YOUR_CATALOG_TOKEN


```

The sink will create the specified namespace and table if they do not exist. Sinks cannot be created for existing Iceberg tables.

## Format

R2 Data Catalog sinks only support Parquet format. JSON format is not supported for Iceberg tables.

### Compression options

Configure Parquet compression for optimal storage and query performance:

Terminal window

```

--compression zstd


```

**Available compression options:**

* `zstd` (default) - Best compression ratio
* `snappy` \- Fastest compression
* `gzip` \- Good compression, widely supported
* `lz4` \- Fast compression with reasonable ratio
* `uncompressed` \- No compression

### Row group size

[Row groups ↗](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/configurations/) are sets of rows in a Parquet file that are stored together, affecting memory usage and query performance. Configure the target row group size in MB:

<title></title>
<text>
Ingest, transform, and load streaming data into Apache Iceberg or Parquet in R2.

 Available on Paid plans 

Cloudflare Pipelines ingests events, transforms them with SQL, and delivers them to R2 as [Iceberg tables](/r2/data-catalog/) or as Parquet and JSON files.

Whether you're processing server logs, mobile application events, IoT telemetry, or clickstream data, Pipelines provides durable ingestion via HTTP endpoints or Worker bindings, SQL-based transformations, and exactly-once delivery to R2\. This makes it easy to build analytics-ready data warehouses and lakehouses without managing streaming infrastructure.

Create your first pipeline by following the [getting started guide](/pipelines/getting-started) or running this [Wrangler](/workers/wrangler/) command:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler pipelines setup


```

---

## Features

### Create your first pipeline

Build your first pipeline to ingest data via HTTP or Workers, apply SQL transformations, and deliver to R2 as Iceberg tables or Parquet files.

[ Get started ](/pipelines/getting-started/) 

### Streams

Durable, buffered queues that receive events via HTTP endpoints or Worker bindings.

[ Learn about Streams ](/pipelines/streams/) 

### Pipelines

Connect streams to sinks with SQL transformations that validate, filter, transform, and enrich your data at ingestion time.

[ Learn about Pipelines ](/pipelines/pipelines/) 

### Sinks

Configure destinations for your data. Write Apache Iceberg tables to R2 Data Catalog or export as Parquet and JSON files.

[ Learn about Sinks ](/pipelines/sinks/) 

---

## Related products

**[R2](/r2/)** 

Cloudflare R2 Object Storage allows developers to store large amounts of unstructured data without the costly egress bandwidth fees associated with typical cloud storage services.

title: R2

image: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dev-products-preview.png

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

  
## Was this helpful?

  
[ Edit](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/edit/production/src/content/docs/pipelines/sinks/available-sinks/r2.mdx) [ Issue](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/issues/new/choose) 

Copy page

# R2

R2 sinks write processed data from pipelines as raw files to [R2 object storage](/r2/). They currently support writing to JSON and Parquet formats.

To create an R2 sink, run the [pipelines sinks create](/workers/wrangler/commands/#pipelines-sinks-create) command and specify the sink type and target [bucket](/r2/buckets/):

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler pipelines sinks create my-sink \

  --type r2 \

  --bucket my-bucket


```

## Format options

R2 sinks support two output formats:

### JSON format

Write data as newline-delimited JSON files:

Terminal window

```

--format json


```

### Parquet format

Write data as Parquet files for better query performance and compression:

Terminal window

```

--format parquet --compression zstd


```

**Compression options for Parquet:**

* `zstd` (default) - Best compression ratio
* `snappy` \- Fastest compression
* `gzip` \- Good compression, widely supported
* `lz4` \- Fast compression with reasonable ratio
* `uncompressed` \- No compression

**Row group size:** [Row groups ↗](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/configurations/) are sets of rows in a Parquet file that are stored together, affecting memory usage and query performance. Configure the target row group size in MB:

Terminal window

```

--target-row-group-size 256


```

## File organization

Files are written with UUID names within the partitioned directory structure. For example, with path `analytics` and default partitioning:

```

analytics/year=2025/month=09/day=18/002507a5-d449-48e8-a484-b1bea916102f.parquet


```

### Path

Set a base directory in your bucket

<text>
## Cloudflare Pipelines now available in beta

Apr 10, 2025 

[ Pipelines ](/pipelines/)[ R2 ](/r2/)[ Workers ](/workers/) 

[Cloudflare Pipelines](/pipelines) is now available in beta, to all users with a [Workers Paid](/workers/platform/pricing) plan.

Pipelines let you ingest high volumes of real time data, without managing the underlying infrastructure. A single pipeline can ingest up to 100 MB of data per second, via HTTP or from a [Worker](/workers). Ingested data is automatically batched, written to output files, and delivered to an [R2 bucket](/r2) in your account. You can use Pipelines to build a data lake of clickstream data, or to store events from a Worker.

Create your first pipeline with a single command:

Create a pipeline

```

$ npx wrangler@latest pipelines create my-clickstream-pipeline --r2-bucket my-bucket


🌀 Authorizing R2 bucket "my-bucket"

🌀 Creating pipeline named "my-clickstream-pipeline"

✅ Successfully created pipeline my-clickstream-pipeline


Id:    0e00c5ff09b34d018152af98d06f5a1xvc

Name:  my-clickstream-pipeline

Sources:

  HTTP:

    Endpoint:        https://0e00c5ff09b34d018152af98d06f5a1xvc.pipelines.cloudflare.com/

    Authentication:  off

    Format:          JSON

  Worker:

    Format:  JSON

Destination:

  Type:         R2

  Bucket:       my-bucket

  Format:       newline-delimited JSON

  Compression:  GZIP

Batch hints:

  Max bytes:     100 MB

  Max duration:  300 seconds

  Max records:   100,000


🎉 You can now send data to your pipeline!


Send data to your pipeline's HTTP endpoint:

curl "https://0e00c5ff09b34d018152af98d06f5a1xvc.pipelines.cloudflare.com/" -d '[{ ...JSON_DATA... }]'


To send data to your pipeline from a Worker, add the following configuration to your config file:

{

  "pipelines": [

    {

      "pipeline": "my-clickstream-pipeline",

      "binding": "PIPELINE"

    }

  ]

}


```

良い情報が揃いました。**Cloudflare Pipelines** がまさに D1 → R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) のパイプを繋ぐのに最適です。

設計を提案する前に、もう1つ質問です。

**R2 Data Catalog に書き出したデータは、何で読みたいですか？**

1. **DuckDB** — ローカル分析、軽量
2. **Snowflake** — 既にプロジェクトで使っている（Pulumi で管理済み）
3. **両方** — DuckDB でアドホック、Snowflake で本格分析
4. **まだ決めていない** — まずは書き出しだけ構築したい

> DEVELOPER

1

> AGENT

了解です。では設計をまとめます。

---

## 設計提案: D1 → R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) パイプライン

### アプローチ比較

| アプローチ | 概要 | メリット | デメリット |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. Pipelines (推奨)** | Worker が D1 を読んで Pipelines binding に送信 → Pipelines が Parquet 変換 + Iceberg メタデータ管理を自動処理 | Parquet 生成不要、Iceberg 管理不要、Cloudflare ネイティブ | Pipelines は beta、Workers Paid プラン必要 |
| **B. Workers + PyIceberg 外部スクリプト** | Python スクリプトで D1 API → PyIceberg → R2 | PyIceberg の豊富な機能 | Workers 外の実行環境が必要 |
| **C. Workers + 自前 Parquet/Iceberg** | Worker 内で parquet-wasm + Iceberg メタデータ自前管理 | 完全サーバーレス | 実装が複雑、Workers のメモリ制限 |

**推奨: アプローチ A (Pipelines)**

### アーキテクチャ

```
D1 (raw)
  ↓  Cron Trigger (毎日)
Worker (d1-to-iceberg)
  ↓  Pipelines binding で JSON イベント送信
Cloudflare Pipelines
  ↓  Parquet 変換 + Iceberg テーブル書き込み
R2 Data Catalog (data-lake バケット)
  ↓  Iceberg REST Catalog
DuckDB (ローカル分析)
```

### コンポーネント

**1. Worker (`d1-to-iceberg`)**
- Cron Trigger で毎日実行（例: `0 1 * * *` = 毎日 AM 1:00 UTC）
- D1 から前日分のデータを SELECT
- 各テーブル（sleep, activity, readiness, heart_rate）のデータを Pipelines に送信
- `sync_state` テーブルで最終エクスポート日を管理し、差分のみ送信

**2. R2 Data Catalog**
- 既存の `data-lake` バケットで Data Catalog を有効化
- namespace: `oura`
- テーブル: `daily_sleep`, `daily_activity`, `daily_readiness`, `heart_rate`

**3. Pipelines**
- テーブルごとに Pipeline を作成（4本）
- Worker binding 経由でデータ投入
- R2 Data Catalog sink で Iceberg テーブルに書き込み
- Parquet + zstd 圧縮

**4. DuckDB でクエリ**
```sql
ATTACH 'r2://data-lake' AS catalog (TYPE iceberg_rest, ...);
SELECT * FROM catalog.oura.daily_sleep;
```

### この設計で進めていいですか？

{

  "content": "# D1 → R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) パイプライン設計\n\n## 概要\n\nD1 の Oura Ring データを Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとしてエクスポートする。Cron Trigger で毎日差分を自動送信し、DuckDB からクエリ可能にする。\n\n## アーキテクチャ\n\n```\nD1 (raw)\n  ↓  Cron Trigger (毎日 01:00 UTC)\nWorker (d1-to-iceberg)\n  ↓  Pipelines binding で JSON イベント送信\nCloudflare Pipelines (4本)\n  ↓  Parquet 変換 + Iceberg テーブル書き込み\nR2 Data Catalog (data-lake バケット)\n  ↓  Iceberg REST Catalog\nDuckDB (ローカル分析)\n```\n\n## コンポーネント\n\n### 1. Worker (`d1-to-iceberg`)\n\n新しい Worker プロジェクト。D1 から Oura データを読み取り、Pipelines に送信する。\n\n**ディレクトリ**: `d1-to-iceberg/`\n\n**環境変数・バインディング**:\n- `DB`: D1 Database (raw)\n- `PIPELINE_SLEEP`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-sleep)\n- `PIPELINE_ACTIVITY`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-activity)\n- `PIPELINE_READINESS`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-readiness)\n- `PIPELINE_HEART_RATE`: Pipeline binding (oura-heart-rate)\n\n**トリガー**: Cron `0 1 * * *` (毎日 UTC 01:00 = JST 10:00)\n\n**処理フロー**:\n1. D1 の `sync_state` テーブルから最終エクスポート日を取得\n2. 最終エクスポート日以降のレコードを各テーブルから SELECT\n3. 各レコードを対応する Pipeline binding に送信\n4. `sync_state` テーブルを更新\n\n**差分管理**:\n- 既存の `sync_state` テーブルに `last_export_at` カラムを追加（D1 マイグレーション）\n- データソース ID: `oura-export`\n- 初回実行時は全データをエクスポート\n\n**心拍数テーブルのバッチ処理**:\n- `oura_heart_rate` は1日約288レコード（5分間隔）\n- 1回のエクスポートで数千レコードになる可能性\n- Pipeline の `send()` で配列として一括送信（Pipelines 側でバッチ処理される）\n\n### 2. R2 Data Catalog\n\n既存の `data-lake` バケットで Data Catalog を有効化する。\n\n**セットアップ手順**:\n```bash\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n```\n\n**namespace**: `oura`\n\n**テーブル**: Pipelines の sink 作成時に自動作成される\n- `oura.daily_sleep`\n- `oura.daily_activity`\n- `oura.daily_readiness`\n- `oura.heart_rate`\n\n**メンテナンス**: compaction と snapshot expiration を有効化\n```bash\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_sleep\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake --older-than-days 30 --retain-last 10\n```\n\n### 3. Pipelines (4本)\n\nテーブルごとに1本の Pipeline を作成。Worker binding 経由でデータを受け取り、R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとして書き込む。\n\n**Pipeline 一覧**:\n\n| Pipeline 名 | Stream | Sink (R2 Data Catalog) | 圧縮 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `oura-daily-sleep` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_sleep` | zstd |\n| `oura-daily-activity` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_activity` | zstd |\n| `oura-daily-readiness` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_readiness` | zstd |\n| `oura-heart-rate` | Worker binding | `oura.heart_rate` | zstd |\n\n**入力スキーマ例 (daily_sleep)**:\n```json\n{\n  \"fields\": [\n    { \"name\": \"id\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"day\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"score\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"deep_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"efficiency\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"latency\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"rem_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"restfulness\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"timing\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"total_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"synced_at\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": false }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### 4. DuckDB クエリ\n\nR2 Data Catalog の Iceberg REST Catalog エンドポイントに接続してクエリ。\n\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg;\nLOAD iceberg;\n\nCREATE SECRET (\n  TYPE r2,\n  KEY_ID 'your-access-key-id',\n  SECRET 'your-secret-access-key',\n  ACCOUNT_ID 'your-account-id'\n);\n\nATTACH '' AS my_catalog (\n  TYPE iceberg,\n  CATALOG_TYPE rest,\n  CATALOG_URI 'https://<ACCOUNT_ID>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com/data-lake',\n  WAREHOUSE 'data-lake'\n);\n\nSELECT * FROM my_catalog.oura.daily_sleep WHERE day >= '2026-03-01';\n```\n\n### 5. D1 マイグレーション\n\n```sql\n-- 0005_export_sync_state.sql\nINSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)\nVALUES ('oura-export', 'oura', NULL);\n```\n\n`sync_state.last_sync_at` を最終エクスポート日として再利用する。\n\n## インフラ変更\n\n### Pulumi\n\n変更なし。`data-lake` バケットは既に Pulumi で管理されている。R2 Data Catalog の有効化と Pipelines の作成は Wrangler CLI で行う（Pulumi の Cloudflare provider にはまだ Pipelines/Data Catalog リソースがない可能性が高い）。\n\n## ファイル構成\n\n```\nd1-to-iceberg/\n├── src/\n│   ├── index.ts          # Cron handler + エクスポートロジック\n│   └── types.ts          # Env 型定義\n├── biome.json\n├── package.json\n├── tsconfig.json\n└── wrangler.jsonc\ninfrastructure/d1/migrations/\n└── 0005_export_sync_state.sql\n```\n\n## 制限事項・考慮事項\n\n- **Pipelines は beta**: 本番ワークロードには注意が必要\n- **Pipelines は Workers Paid プラン必須**: 既にプロジェクトで使用中なので問題なし\n- **心拍数データの量**: 1日288レコード × 365日 ≈ 105,000レコード/年。初回エクスポートでも問題ない規模\n- **冪等性**: 同じ日のデータが二重送信されても Iceberg の append になる。重複排除が必要な場合は後でクエリ側で対応\n- **エラーハンドリング**: Pipeline 送信失敗時は `sync_state` を更新しない → 次回のCronで再送信\n\n## 対象外\n\n- Snowflake 連携（将来のフェーズ）\n- Linear / Withings データのエクスポート（将来拡張）\n- 重複排除ロジック（まずは append-only）\n"
}
```

I now have everything needed for a complete review. Here is my analysis.

---

## Spec Review: D1 to R2 Iceberg Pipeline

### Issues Found

**Issue 1: DuckDB ATTACH syntax is wrong (Blocking)**

The spec shows this at line 124-129:
```sql
CREATE SECRET (
  TYPE r2,
  KEY_ID 'your-access-key-id',
  SECRET 'your-secret-access-key',
  ACCOUNT_ID 'your-account-id'
);

ATTACH '' AS my_catalog (
  TYPE iceberg,
  CATALOG_TYPE rest,
  CATALOG_URI 'https://<ACCOUNT_ID>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com/data-lake',
  WAREHOUSE 'data-lake'
);
```

The actual R2 Data Catalog DuckDB syntax (confirmed from docs) is different. The SECRET type must be `ICEBERG` (not `r2`), it takes a `TOKEN` (an R2 API token with both R2 and Data Catalog permissions), not `KEY_ID`/`SECRET`/`ACCOUNT_ID`. The ATTACH form uses `ENDPOINT` for the catalog URI, not `CATALOG_URI`, and the warehouse is positional (first arg), not a keyword. The correct form is:

```sql
CREATE SECRET r2_secret (TYPE ICEBERG, TOKEN '<r2-api-token>');
ATTACH 'data-lake' AS my_catalog (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT '<catalog_uri>');
```

The catalog URI value itself comes from `wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake` output - it is not the R2 storage endpoint. These are two different URLs. The spec conflates R2 object storage URL with the Iceberg REST catalog endpoint.

**Issue 2: Compaction command syntax is wrong (Minor but will cause CLI failures)**

Spec line 72-74:
```bash
npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_sleep
npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake --older-than-days 30 --retain-last 10
```

The actual compaction syntax requires `--token` and puts namespace/table as flags or after the bucket. The working form from the docs is:
```bash
npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_sleep --target-size 128
npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake --token <API_TOKEN> --older-than-days 30 --retain-last 10
```

The `--token` is required for snapshot-expiration but is missing from the spec.

**Issue 3: sync_state migration approach is ambiguous (Design clarity)**

The spec says "再利用する" (reuse) `sync_state.last_sync_at` with a new row `'oura-export'`. But there is already a row with `id = 'oura'` in `sync_state` (inserted in `0002_oura_tables.sql`, line 132-133). The export timestamp semantics differ from the ingestion sync timestamp - `last_sync_at` for `id='oura'` tracks when data was last pulled from the Oura API, while `last_export_at` for `id='oura-export'` tracks when data was last pushed to Iceberg. These serve different purposes and could diverge (e.g., a D1 sync runs but the Iceberg export fails). Reusing the same column name `last_sync_at` for export state is semantically misleading. The spec should either rename the column intent clearly, or add a dedicated `last_export_at` column. Adding a separate column via migration is the cleaner path.

**Issue 4: oura_heart_rate has no stable `id` field - schema mismatch (Blocking)**

In the D1 schema (`0002_oura_tables.sql` line 84-91), `oura_heart_rate` uses `id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT` - this is an internal autoincrement key, not a business key. The Pipeline schema spec defines `{ "name": "id", "type": "string", "required": true }` for `daily_sleep`. For heart rate, the spec does not define a schema at all, but the Worker logic needs to handle that the `id` from the D1 row is an autoincrement integer, not a meaningful deduplication key. The spec should define the heart rate Pipeline schema explicitly (omit `id`, use `timestamp`+`bpm`+`source` as the natural key) and note the difference from the other three tables.

**Issue 5: Missing wrangler.jsonc binding configuration detail (Completeness)**

The spec lists the binding names (`PIPELINE_SLEEP`, `PIPELINE_ACTIVITY`, etc.) and mentions wrangler.jsonc as a file to create, but never shows the actual binding snippet. Based on the official API, the binding format is:
```jsonc
"pipelines": [
  { "pipeline": "<stream-id>", "binding": "PIPELINE_SLEEP" },
  ...
]
```
The stream IDs are only known after running `wrangler pipelines setup`, but the spec should at least show the binding structure as a placeholder. Without this, implementers will need to guess the config.

**Issue 6: Workers CPU time concern for initial export not fully addressed (Risk)**

The spec notes "初回実行時は全データをエクスポート" (full export on first run) but does not quantify the CPU time risk. The Workers Paid plan has a 30-second CPU time limit. Heart rate alone could be 100,000+ records. Sending them as a single array to `pipeline.send([...all_records])` is fine since Pipelines handles the buffering, but the D1 SELECT of 100,000 rows has real cost. The spec acknowledges the volume but does not prescribe paging (e.g., chunked D1 queries with `LIMIT`/`OFFSET` or cursor-based pagination). This is worth explicitly calling out as a design decision.

**Issue 7: Pipeline schema for daily_sleep is incomplete relative to D1 schema**

The spec's `daily_sleep` schema omits the `timestamp` field (the ISO datetime string from Oura, distinct from `day`). The D1 `oura_daily_sleep` table has a `timestamp TEXT` column (line 26 of `0002_oura_tables.sql`). This field should appear in the Pipeline schema.

---

### What is Correct and Solid

- Architecture diagram is accurate for the Cloudflare data plane
- 4 Pipeline topology (one per table) is correct - Pipelines bindings are per-stream
- Cron trigger `0 1 * * *` is reasonable
- `sync_state` reuse for tracking export state is a valid pattern
- `INSERT OR IGNORE` migration for the new `oura-export` sync_state row is correct
- R2 Data Catalog namespace/table naming convention (`oura.daily_sleep` etc.) is valid
- Idempotency / append-only noted as a known trade-off
- Pulumi exclusion rationale (no provider support for Pipelines/Data Catalog) is accurate
- Pipelines beta limitations are appropriately flagged
- `zstd` compression selection is reasonable

---

### Verdict: NOT APPROVED

Fix these items before implementation:

1. Correct the DuckDB ATTACH syntax: `TYPE ICEBERG` secret with `TOKEN`, use `ENDPOINT` not `CATALOG_URI`, and source the actual catalog URI from `wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable` output.
2. Add `--token` to the snapshot-expiration command.
3. Explicitly define the heart rate Pipeline schema (without the autoincrement `id`).
4. Add the `"pipelines"` binding snippet to the wrangler.jsonc section.
5. Clarify the `sync_state` column semantics - consider renaming the reused purpose or adding a note that `last_sync_at` in the `oura-export` row means "last export timestamp".
6. Add a note on initial export pagination strategy for heart rate given the 30s CPU limit.
7. Add `timestamp` field to the `daily_sleep` schema definition.

Sources:
- [R2 Data Catalog - Cloudflare R2 docs](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/data-catalog/)
- [DuckDB - Cloudflare R2 docs](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/duckdb/)
- [Manage catalogs - Cloudflare R2 docs](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/)
- [Getting started - Cloudflare Pipelines Docs](https://developers.cloudflare.com/pipelines/getting-started/)
- [Cloudflare Pipelines full reference](https://developers.cloudflare.com/pipelines/llms-full.txt)
- [R2 Data Catalog public beta announcement](https://blog.cloudflare.com/r2-data-catalog-public-beta/)

   145→
   146→### Pulumi
   147→
   148→変更なし。`data-lake` バケットは既に Pulumi で管理されている。R2 Data Catalog の有効化と Pipelines の作成は Wrangler CLI で行う（Pulumi の Cloudflare provider にはまだ Pipelines/Data Catalog リソースがない可能性が高い）。
   149→
   150→## ファイル構成
   151→
   152→```
   153→d1-to-iceberg/
   154→├── src/
   155→│   ├── index.ts          # Cron handler + エクスポートロジック
   156→│   └── types.ts          # Env 型定義
   157→├── biome.json
   158→├── package.json
   159→├── tsconfig.json
   160→└── wrangler.jsonc
   161→infrastructure/d1/migrations/
   162→└── 0005_export_sync_state.sql
   163→```
   164→
   165→## 制限事項・考慮事項
   166→
   167→- **Pipelines は beta**: 本番ワークロードには注意が必要
   168→- **Pipelines は Workers Paid プラン必須**: 既にプロジェクトで使用中なので問題なし
   169→- **心拍数データの量**: 1日288レコード × 365日 ≈ 105,000レコード/年。初回エクスポートでも問題ない規模
   170→- **冪等性**: 同じ日のデータが二重送信されても Iceberg の append になる。重複排除が必要な場合は後でクエリ側で対応
   171→- **エラーハンドリング**: Pipeline 送信失敗時は `sync_state` を更新しない → 次回のCronで再送信
   172→
   173→## 対象外
   174→
   175→- Snowflake 連携（将来のフェーズ）
   176→- Linear / Withings データのエクスポート（将来拡張）
   177→- 重複排除ロジック（まずは append-only）
   178→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-11-d1-to-r2-iceberg-design.md",
  "content": "# D1 → R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) パイプライン設計\n\n## 概要\n\nD1 の Oura Ring データを Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとしてエクスポートする。Cron Trigger で毎日差分を自動送信し、DuckDB からクエリ可能にする。\n\n## アーキテクチャ\n\n```\nD1 (raw)\n  ↓  Cron Trigger (毎日 01:00 UTC)\nWorker (d1-to-iceberg)\n  ↓  Pipelines binding で JSON イベント送信\nCloudflare Pipelines (4本)\n  ↓  Parquet 変換 + Iceberg テーブル書き込み\nR2 Data Catalog (data-lake バケット)\n  ↓  Iceberg REST Catalog\nDuckDB (ローカル分析)\n```\n\n## コンポーネント\n\n### 1. Worker (`d1-to-iceberg`)\n\n新しい Worker プロジェクト。D1 から Oura データを読み取り、Pipelines に送信する。\n\n**ディレクトリ**: `d1-to-iceberg/`\n\n**環境変数・バインディング**:\n- `DB`: D1 Database (raw)\n- `PIPELINE_SLEEP`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-sleep)\n- `PIPELINE_ACTIVITY`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-activity)\n- `PIPELINE_READINESS`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-readiness)\n- `PIPELINE_HEART_RATE`: Pipeline binding (oura-heart-rate)\n\n**wrangler.jsonc の Pipeline バインディング設定**:\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"pipelines\": [\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-sleep\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_SLEEP\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-activity\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_ACTIVITY\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-readiness\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_READINESS\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-heart-rate\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_HEART_RATE\" }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n**トリガー**: Cron `0 1 * * *` (毎日 UTC 01:00 = JST 10:00)\n\n**処理フロー**:\n1. D1 の `sync_state` テーブルから最終エクスポート日を取得（`id = 'oura-export'`）\n2. 最終エクスポート日以降のレコードを各テーブルから SELECT\n3. 各レコードを対応する Pipeline binding に送信\n4. `sync_state` テーブルの `last_sync_at` を更新\n\n**差分管理**:\n- 既存の `sync_state` テーブルに `oura-export` 行を追加（D1 マイグレーション）\n- `last_sync_at` カラムを「最終エクスポート日」として使用（既存の `id='oura'` 行は「最終 API 取り込み日」であり、用途が異なる）\n- 初回実行時（`last_sync_at = NULL`）は全データをエクスポート\n\n**心拍数テーブルのバッチ処理とページング**:\n- `oura_heart_rate` は1日約288レコード（5分間隔）\n- 初回エクスポート時は全履歴で 100,000+ レコードの可能性がある\n- Workers Paid の CPU 制限（30秒）に対応するため、1回のクエリにつき `LIMIT 10000` でページングする\n- 各ページを Pipeline の `send()` で送信し、次のページを取得\n- `day` + `timestamp` でカーソルベースのページネーションを行う\n\n### 2. R2 Data Catalog\n\n既存の `data-lake` バケットで Data Catalog を有効化する。\n\n**セットアップ手順**:\n```bash\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n```\n実行後に表示される **Catalog URI** と **Warehouse name** を控えておく（DuckDB 接続時に使用）。\n\n**namespace**: `oura`\n\n**テーブル**: Pipelines の sink 作成時に自動作成される\n- `oura.daily_sleep`\n- `oura.daily_activity`\n- `oura.daily_readiness`\n- `oura.heart_rate`\n\n**メンテナンス**: compaction と snapshot expiration を有効化\n```bash\n# compaction（テーブルごとに有効化）\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_sleep --target-size 128\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_activity --target-size 128\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_readiness --target-size 128\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura heart_rate --target-size 128\n\n# snapshot expiration（カタログレベル）\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake \\\n  --token <CATALOG_API_TOKEN> \\\n  --older-than-days 30 \\\n  --retain-last 10\n```\n\n### 3. Pipelines (4本)\n\nテーブルごとに1本の Pipeline を作成。Worker binding 経由でデータを受け取り、R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとして書き込む。\n\n**Pipeline 一覧**:\n\n| Pipeline 名 | Stream | Sink (R2 Data Catalog) | 圧縮 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `oura-daily-sleep` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_sleep` | zstd |\n| `oura-daily-activity` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_activity` | zstd |\n| `oura-daily-readiness` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_readiness` | zstd |\n| `oura-heart-rate` | Worker binding | `oura.heart_rate` | zstd |\n\n**入力スキーマ (daily_sleep)**:\n```json\n{\n  \"fields\": [\n    { \"name\": \"id\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"day\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"score\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"timestamp\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"deep_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"efficiency\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"latency\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"rem_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"restfulness\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"timing\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"total_sleep\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"synced_at\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": false }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n**入力スキーマ (heart_rate)**:\n\n`oura_heart_rate` の D1 上の `id` は `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT`（内部キー）であり、ビジネスキーではない。Iceberg テーブルでは `id` を除外し、`timestamp` + `bpm` + `source` を自然キーとして扱う。\n\n```json\n{\n  \"fields\": [\n    { \"name\": \"bpm\", \"type\": \"int32\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"source\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"timestamp\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"day\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"synced_at\", \"type\": \"string\", \"required\": false }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### 4. DuckDB クエリ\n\nR2 Data Catalog の Iceberg REST Catalog エンドポイントに接続してクエリ。\n\nAPI トークンは R2 Admin Read & Write 権限で作成（R2 Data Catalog + R2 Storage の両方の権限を含む）。\n\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg;\nLOAD iceberg;\n\n-- R2 Data Catalog 用の SECRET を作成（TYPE は ICEBERG、TOKEN は R2 API トークン）\nCREATE SECRET r2_catalog (\n  TYPE ICEBERG,\n  TOKEN '<R2_API_TOKEN>'\n);\n\n-- Catalog URI は `wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable` の出力値を使用\n-- （R2 ストレージ URL とは異なる）\nATTACH 'data-lake' AS oura_lake (\n  TYPE ICEBERG,\n  ENDPOINT '<CATALOG_URI>'\n);\n\nSELECT * FROM oura_lake.oura.daily_sleep WHERE day >= '2026-03-01';\n```\n\n### 5. D1 マイグレーション\n\n```sql\n-- 0005_export_sync_state.sql\n-- oura-export: D1 → R2 Iceberg エクスポートの最終実行日を管理\n-- 既存の id='oura' (API 取り込み日) とは別の用途\nINSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)\nVALUES ('oura-export', 'oura', NULL);\n```\n\n`sync_state` テーブルの `last_sync_at` カラムを再利用する。`id='oura'` は「Oura API からの最終取り込み日」、`id='oura-export'` は「R2 への最終エクスポート日」を表す。\n\n## インフラ変更\n\n### Pulumi\n\n変更なし。`data-lake` バケットは既に Pulumi で管理されている。R2 Data Catalog の有効化と Pipelines の作成は Wrangler CLI で行う（Pulumi の Cloudflare provider にはまだ Pipelines/Data Catalog リソースがない）。\n\n## ファイル構成\n\n```\nd1-to-iceberg/\n├── src/\n│   ├── index.ts          # Cron handler + エクスポートロジック\n│   └── types.ts          # Env 型定義\n├── biome.json\n├── package.json\n├── tsconfig.json\n└── wrangler.jsonc\ninfrastructure/d1/migrations/\n└── 0005_export_sync_state.sql\n```\n\n## 制限事項・考慮事項\n\n- **Pipelines は beta**: 本番ワークロードには注意が必要\n- **Pipelines は Workers Paid プラン必須**: 既にプロジェクトで使用中なので問題なし\n- **心拍数データの量**: 1日288レコード × 365日 ≈ 105,000レコード/年。初回エクスポートは `LIMIT 10000` でページングして CPU 制限に対応\n- **冪等性**: 同じ日のデータが二重送信されても Iceberg の append になる。重複排除が必要な場合は後でクエリ側で対応\n- **エラーハンドリング**: Pipeline 送信失敗時は `sync_state` を更新しない → 次回のCronで再送信\n\n## 対象外\n\n- Snowflake 連携（将来のフェーズ）\n- Linear / Withings データのエクスポート（将来拡張）\n- 重複排除ロジック（まずは append-only）\n"
}

Now I'll verify each of the 7 previously reported issues against the current spec.

**Issue 1: DuckDB ATTACH syntax — TYPE ICEBERG secret with TOKEN, ENDPOINT not CATALOG_URI**

Lines 160-173 show:
- `CREATE SECRET r2_catalog (TYPE ICEBERG, TOKEN '<R2_API_TOKEN>')` — TYPE ICEBERG and TOKEN are correct.
- `ATTACH 'data-lake' AS oura_lake (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT '<CATALOG_URI>')` — TYPE ICEBERG and ENDPOINT are present.
- A comment clarifies the CATALOG_URI comes from `wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable` output, not the R2 storage URL.

FIXED.

**Issue 2: snapshot-expiration --token flag**

Lines 95-98 show:
```
npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake \
  --token <CATALOG_API_TOKEN> \
  --older-than-days 30 \
  --retain-last 10
```
The `--token` flag is present.

FIXED.

**Issue 3: heart_rate Pipeline schema without autoincrement id**

Lines 134-148 explicitly state: "Iceberg テーブルでは `id` を除外し、`timestamp` + `bpm` + `source` を自然キーとして扱う。" The schema fields list `bpm`, `source`, `timestamp`, `day`, `synced_at` — no `id` field.

FIXED.

**Issue 4: wrangler.jsonc pipelines binding snippet**

Lines 37-46 show a complete `pipelines` array snippet with all four bindings (`PIPELINE_SLEEP`, `PIPELINE_ACTIVITY`, `PIPELINE_READINESS`, `PIPELINE_HEART_RATE`).

FIXED.

**Issue 5: sync_state column semantics clarification**

Lines 57-58 explicitly state: "`last_sync_at` カラムを「最終エクスポート日」として使用（既存の `id='oura'` 行は「最終 API 取り込み日」であり、用途が異なる）". Lines 185-186 repeat this distinction in the migration section.

FIXED.

**Issue 6: Initial export pagination for heart_rate**

Lines 62-66 describe the paging strategy: `LIMIT 10000` per query, cursor-based pagination on `day` + `timestamp`, and the rationale (100,000+ records on first export, 30-second CPU limit). Line 213 reiterates this in the constraints section.

FIXED.

**Issue 7: timestamp field in daily_sleep schema**

Line 122 shows `{ "name": "timestamp", "type": "string", "required": false }` present in the `daily_sleep` fields list.

FIXED.

> AGENT

`timestamp` は Oura API が返す **睡眠データの記録日時**（ISO 8601）です。実データを見ると `day` と同じ日の `T00:00:00.000+00:00` で、`day` と実質重複しています。

sleep/activity/readiness では `day`（日付）が実用的なキーで、`timestamp` は Oura API の仕様上存在するだけで情報量が少ないです。Iceberg スキーマから外しても問題ありません。

**含める/外す、どちらがいいですか？**

1. **外す** — `day` があれば十分、スキーマをシンプルに保つ
2. **含める** — Oura API の元データを忠実に再現

> DEVELOPER

７の追加するとはどういうこと

> AGENT

レビューの指摘7は「D1 の `oura_daily_sleep` テーブルに `timestamp` カラムがあるのに、Pipeline スキーマ定義に含まれていなかった」ので追加した、という意味です。

ただし今確認した通り、`timestamp` は `day` + `T00:00:00` で情報量ゼロなので、**外してスキーマをシンプルにする**のが良さそうです。
