     1→# D1 → R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) パイプライン設計
     2→
     3→## 概要
     4→
     5→D1 の Oura Ring データを Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとしてエクスポートする。Cron Trigger で毎日差分を自動送信し、DuckDB からクエリ可能にする。
     6→
     7→## アーキテクチャ
     8→
     9→```
    10→D1 (raw)
    11→  ↓  Cron Trigger (毎日 01:00 UTC)
    12→Worker (d1-to-iceberg)
    13→  ↓  Pipelines binding で JSON イベント送信
    14→Cloudflare Pipelines (4本)
    15→  ↓  Parquet 変換 + Iceberg テーブル書き込み
    16→R2 Data Catalog (data-lake バケット)
    17→  ↓  Iceberg REST Catalog
    18→DuckDB (ローカル分析)
    19→```
    20→
    21→## コンポーネント
    22→
    23→### 1. Worker (`d1-to-iceberg`)
    24→
    25→新しい Worker プロジェクト。D1 から Oura データを読み取り、Pipelines に送信する。
    26→
    27→**ディレクトリ**: `d1-to-iceberg/`
    28→
    29→**環境変数・バインディング**:
    30→- `DB`: D1 Database (raw)
    31→- `PIPELINE_SLEEP`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-sleep)
    32→- `PIPELINE_ACTIVITY`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-activity)
    33→- `PIPELINE_READINESS`: Pipeline binding (oura-daily-readiness)
    34→- `PIPELINE_HEART_RATE`: Pipeline binding (oura-heart-rate)
    35→
    36→**wrangler.jsonc の Pipeline バインディング設定**:
    37→```jsonc
    38→{
    39→  "pipelines": [
    40→    { "pipeline": "oura-daily-sleep", "binding": "PIPELINE_SLEEP" },
    41→    { "pipeline": "oura-daily-activity", "binding": "PIPELINE_ACTIVITY" },
    42→    { "pipeline": "oura-daily-readiness", "binding": "PIPELINE_READINESS" },
    43→    { "pipeline": "oura-heart-rate", "binding": "PIPELINE_HEART_RATE" }
    44→  ]
    45→}
    46→```
    47→
    48→**トリガー**: Cron `0 1 * * *` (毎日 UTC 01:00 = JST 10:00)
    49→
    50→**処理フロー**:
    51→1. D1 の `sync_state` テーブルから最終エクスポート日を取得（`id = 'oura-export'`）
    52→2. 最終エクスポート日以降のレコードを各テーブルから SELECT
    53→3. 各レコードを対応する Pipeline binding に送信
    54→4. `sync_state` テーブルの `last_sync_at` を更新
    55→
    56→**差分管理**:
    57→- 既存の `sync_state` テーブルに `oura-export` 行を追加（D1 マイグレーション）
    58→- `last_sync_at` カラムを「最終エクスポート日」として使用（既存の `id='oura'` 行は「最終 API 取り込み日」であり、用途が異なる）
    59→- 初回実行時（`last_sync_at = NULL`）は全データをエクスポート
    60→
    61→**心拍数テーブルのバッチ処理とページング**:
    62→- `oura_heart_rate` は1日約288レコード（5分間隔）
    63→- 初回エクスポート時は全履歴で 100,000+ レコードの可能性がある
    64→- Workers Paid の CPU 制限（30秒）に対応するため、1回のクエリにつき `LIMIT 10000` でページングする
    65→- 各ページを Pipeline の `send()` で送信し、次のページを取得
    66→- `day` + `timestamp` でカーソルベースのページネーションを行う
    67→
    68→### 2. R2 Data Catalog
    69→
    70→既存の `data-lake` バケットで Data Catalog を有効化する。
    71→
    72→**セットアップ手順**:
    73→```bash
    74→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake
    75→```
    76→実行後に表示される **Catalog URI** と **Warehouse name** を控えておく（DuckDB 接続時に使用）。
    77→
    78→**namespace**: `oura`
    79→
    80→**テーブル**: Pipelines の sink 作成時に自動作成される
    81→- `oura.daily_sleep`
    82→- `oura.daily_activity`
    83→- `oura.daily_readiness`
    84→- `oura.heart_rate`
    85→
    86→**メンテナンス**: compaction と snapshot expiration を有効化
    87→```bash
    88→# compaction（テーブルごとに有効化）
    89→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_sleep --target-size 128
    90→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_activity --target-size 128
    91→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura daily_readiness --target-size 128
    92→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog compaction enable data-lake oura heart_rate --target-size 128
    93→
    94→# snapshot expiration（カタログレベル）
    95→npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog snapshot-expiration enable data-lake \
    96→  --token <CATALOG_API_TOKEN> \
    97→  --older-than-days 30 \
    98→  --retain-last 10
    99→```
   100→
   101→### 3. Pipelines (4本)
   102→
   103→テーブルごとに1本の Pipeline を作成。Worker binding 経由でデータを受け取り、R2 Data Catalog に Iceberg テーブルとして書き込む。
   104→
   105→**Pipeline 一覧**:
   106→
   107→| Pipeline 名 | Stream | Sink (R2 Data Catalog) | 圧縮 |
   108→|---|---|---|---|
   109→| `oura-daily-sleep` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_sleep` | zstd |
   110→| `oura-daily-activity` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_activity` | zstd |
   111→| `oura-daily-readiness` | Worker binding | `oura.daily_readiness` | zstd |
   112→| `oura-heart-rate` | Worker binding | `oura.heart_rate` | zstd |
   113→
   114→**入力スキーマ (daily_sleep)**:
   115→```json
   116→{
   117→  "fields": [
   118→    { "name": "id", "type": "string", "required": true },
   119→    { "name": "day", "type": "string", "required": true },
   120→    { "name": "score", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   121→    { "name": "timestamp", "type": "string", "required": false },
   122→    { "name": "deep_sleep", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   123→    { "name": "efficiency", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   124→    { "name": "latency", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   125→    { "name": "rem_sleep", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   126→    { "name": "restfulness", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   127→    { "name": "timing", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   128→    { "name": "total_sleep", "type": "int32", "required": false },
   129→    { "name": "synced_at", "type": "string", "required": false }
   130→  ]
   131→}
   132→```
   133→
   134→**入力スキーマ (heart_rate)**:
   135→
   136→D1 の全カラムをそのまま保存する。`id` は `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT`（内部キー）だが、元データの忠実な再現のために含める。
   137→
   138→```json
   139→{
   140→  "fields": [
   141→    { "name": "id", "type": "int32", "required": true },
   142→    { "name": "bpm", "type": "int32", "required": true },
   143→    { "name": "source", "type": "string", "required": false },
   144→    { "name": "timestamp", "type": "string", "required": true },
   145→    { "name": "day", "type": "string", "required": true },
   146→    { "name": "synced_at", "type": "string", "required": false }
   147→  ]
   148→}
   149→```
   150→
   151→### 4. DuckDB クエリ
   152→
   153→R2 Data Catalog の Iceberg REST Catalog エンドポイントに接続してクエリ。
   154→
   155→API トークンは R2 Admin Read & Write 権限で作成（R2 Data Catalog + R2 Storage の両方の権限を含む）。
   156→
   157→```sql
   158→INSTALL iceberg;
   159→LOAD iceberg;
   160→
   161→-- R2 Data Catalog 用の SECRET を作成（TYPE は ICEBERG、TOKEN は R2 API トークン）
   162→CREATE SECRET r2_catalog (
   163→  TYPE ICEBERG,
   164→  TOKEN '<R2_API_TOKEN>'
   165→);
   166→
   167→-- Catalog URI は `wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable` の出力値を使用
   168→-- （R2 ストレージ URL とは異なる）
   169→ATTACH 'data-lake' AS oura_lake (
   170→  TYPE ICEBERG,
   171→  ENDPOINT '<CATALOG_URI>'
   172→);
   173→
   174→SELECT * FROM oura_lake.oura.daily_sleep WHERE day >= '2026-03-01';
   175→```
   176→
   177→### 5. D1 マイグレーション
   178→
   179→```sql
   180→-- 0005_export_sync_state.sql
   181→-- oura-export: D1 → R2 Iceberg エクスポートの最終実行日を管理
   182→-- 既存の id='oura' (API 取り込み日) とは別の用途
   183→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)
   184→VALUES ('oura-export', 'oura', NULL);
   185→```
   186→
   187→`sync_state` テーブルの `last_sync_at` カラムを再利用する。`id='oura'` は「Oura API からの最終取り込み日」、`id='oura-export'` は「R2 への最終エクスポート日」を表す。
   188→
   189→## インフラ変更
   190→
   191→### Pulumi
   192→
   193→変更なし。`data-lake` バケットは既に Pulumi で管理されている。R2 Data Catalog の有効化と Pipelines の作成は Wrangler CLI で行う（Pulumi の Cloudflare provider にはまだ Pipelines/Data Catalog リソースがない）。
   194→
   195→## ファイル構成
   196→
   197→```
   198→d1-to-iceberg/
   199→├── src/
   200→│   ├── index.ts          # Cron handler + エクスポートロジック
   201→│   └── types.ts          # Env 型定義
   202→├── biome.json
   203→├── package.json
   204→├── tsconfig.json
   205→└── wrangler.jsonc
   206→infrastructure/d1/migrations/
   207→└── 0005_export_sync_state.sql
   208→```
   209→
   210→## 制限事項・考慮事項
   211→
   212→- **Pipelines は beta**: 本番ワークロードには注意が必要
   213→- **Pipelines は Workers Paid プラン必須**: 既にプロジェクトで使用中なので問題なし
   214→- **心拍数データの量**: 1日288レコード × 365日 ≈ 105,000レコード/年。初回エクスポートは `LIMIT 10000` でページングして CPU 制限に対応
   215→- **冪等性**: 同じ日のデータが二重送信されても Iceberg の append になる。重複排除が必要な場合は後でクエリ側で対応
   216→- **エラーハンドリング**: Pipeline 送信失敗時は `sync_state` を更新しない → 次回のCronで再送信
   217→
   218→## 対象外
   219→
   220→- Snowflake 連携（将来のフェーズ）
   221→- Linear / Withings データのエクスポート（将来拡張）
   222→- 重複排除ロジック（まずは append-only）
   223→
```

```
     1→-- ============================================
     2→-- OAuth2 Token Storage
     3→-- ============================================
     4→
     5→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oauth_tokens (
     6→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
     7→    access_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     8→    refresh_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     9→    token_type TEXT DEFAULT 'Bearer',
    10→    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    11→    scope TEXT,
    12→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    13→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    14→);
    15→
    16→-- ============================================
    17→-- Oura Ring Tables
    18→-- ============================================
    19→
    20→-- Daily Sleep
    21→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_sleep (
    22→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    23→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    24→    score INTEGER,
    25→    timestamp TEXT,
    26→    -- contributors
    27→    deep_sleep INTEGER,
    28→    efficiency INTEGER,
    29→    latency INTEGER,
    30→    rem_sleep INTEGER,
    31→    restfulness INTEGER,
    32→    timing INTEGER,
    33→    total_sleep INTEGER,
    34→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    35→);
    36→
    37→-- Daily Activity
    38→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_activity (
    39→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    40→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    41→    score INTEGER,
    42→    active_calories INTEGER,
    43→    total_calories INTEGER,
    44→    steps INTEGER,
    45→    equivalent_walking_distance REAL,
    46→    high_activity_time INTEGER,
    47→    medium_activity_time INTEGER,
    48→    low_activity_time INTEGER,
    49→    sedentary_time INTEGER,
    50→    resting_time INTEGER,
    51→    met_average REAL,
    52→    -- contributors
    53→    meet_daily_targets INTEGER,
    54→    move_every_hour INTEGER,
    55→    recovery_time INTEGER,
    56→    stay_active INTEGER,
    57→    training_frequency INTEGER,
    58→    training_volume INTEGER,
    59→    timestamp TEXT,
    60→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    61→);
    62→
    63→-- Daily Readiness
    64→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_readiness (
    65→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    66→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    67→    score INTEGER,
    68→    temperature_deviation REAL,
    69→    temperature_trend_deviation REAL,
    70→    timestamp TEXT,
    71→    -- contributors
    72→    activity_balance INTEGER,
    73→    body_temperature INTEGER,
    74→    hrv_balance INTEGER,
    75→    previous_day_activity INTEGER,
    76→    previous_night INTEGER,
    77→    recovery_index INTEGER,
    78→    resting_heart_rate INTEGER,
    79→    sleep_balance INTEGER,
    80→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    81→);
    82→
    83→-- Heart Rate (high volume: ~288 records/day at 5min intervals)
    84→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_heart_rate (
    85→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    86→    bpm INTEGER NOT NULL,
    87→    source TEXT,
    88→    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
    89→    day TEXT NOT NULL,
    90→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    91→);
    92→
    93→-- ============================================
    94→-- Oura Indexes
    95→-- ============================================
    96→
    97→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_sleep_day ON oura_daily_sleep(day DESC);
    98→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_activity_day ON oura_daily_activity(day DESC);
    99→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_readiness_day ON oura_daily_readiness(day DESC);
   100→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_day ON oura_heart_rate(day DESC);
   101→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_timestamp ON oura_heart_rate(timestamp DESC);
   102→
   103→-- ============================================
   104→-- Oura Views
   105→-- ============================================
   106→
   107→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_oura_daily_summary AS
   108→SELECT
   109→    s.day,
   110→    s.score AS sleep_score,
   111→    a.score AS activity_score,
   112→    r.score AS readiness_score,
   113→    a.steps,
   114→    a.total_calories,
   115→    a.active_calories,
   116→    r.temperature_deviation,
   117→    s.deep_sleep AS sleep_deep_sleep,
   118→    s.efficiency AS sleep_efficiency,
   119→    s.total_sleep AS sleep_total_sleep
   120→FROM oura_daily_sleep s
   121→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_activity a ON s.day = a.day
   122→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_readiness r ON s.day = r.day
   123→ORDER BY s.day DESC;
   124→
   125→-- ============================================
   126→-- Initial Data
   127→-- ============================================
   128→
   129→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron)
   130→VALUES ('oura', 'Oura Ring', 'api', 'https://api.ouraring.com', '0 0 * * *');
   131→
   132→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)
   133→VALUES ('oura', 'oura', NULL);
   134→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-03-11-d1-to-r2-iceberg.md",
  "content": "# D1 → R2 Iceberg Pipeline Implementation Plan\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (if subagents available) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** D1 の Oura Ring データを Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg) にエクスポートする Worker を構築する。\n\n**Architecture:** Cron Trigger で毎日動く Worker が D1 から差分データを SELECT し、4本の Pipelines binding に送信。Pipelines が Parquet 変換 + Iceberg メタデータ管理を担当。DuckDB から Iceberg REST Catalog 経由でクエリ。\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Cloudflare Workers, D1, Pipelines, R2 Data Catalog, pnpm, Biome\n\n**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-11-d1-to-r2-iceberg-design.md`\n\n---\n\n## File Map\n\n| File | Action | Responsibility |\n|---|---|---|\n| `d1-to-iceberg/package.json` | Create | 依存関係 (wrangler, biome, typescript) |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/tsconfig.json` | Create | TypeScript 設定 |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/biome.json` | Create | Lint & Format 設定 |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/wrangler.jsonc` | Create | Worker 設定 (D1, Pipelines, Cron) |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/src/types.ts` | Create | Env 型定義 |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/src/export.ts` | Create | テーブルごとのエクスポートロジック |\n| `d1-to-iceberg/src/index.ts` | Create | Cron handler エントリポイント |\n| `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0005_export_sync_state.sql` | Create | sync_state に oura-export 行を追加 |\n\n---\n\n## Chunk 1: インフラセットアップ\n\n### Task 1: R2 Data Catalog の有効化\n\n**Files:** なし (CLI 操作のみ)\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: R2 Data Catalog を有効化**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n```\n\n出力される **Catalog URI** と **Warehouse name** を控える。\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: 出力を確認**\n\nCatalog URI と Warehouse name が表示されることを確認。\n\n### Task 2: Pipelines の作成\n\n**Files:** なし (CLI 操作のみ)\n\n4本の Pipeline を作成する。各 Pipeline は R2 Data Catalog sink を持つ。\n\nまず R2 API トークンを Cloudflare ダッシュボードで作成する:\n- R2 > **Manage R2 API Tokens** > **Create API Token**\n- Permission: **Admin Read & Write** (R2 Data Catalog + R2 Storage の両方を含む)\n- トークン値を控える → `CATALOG_TOKEN` として使用\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: oura-daily-sleep Pipeline を作成**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines create oura-daily-sleep \\\n  --r2-data-catalog \\\n  --bucket data-lake \\\n  --namespace oura \\\n  --table daily_sleep \\\n  --catalog-token <CATALOG_TOKEN> \\\n  --compression zstd\n```\n\n入力スキーマの設定が対話的に求められる場合は、spec の daily_sleep スキーマに従って設定する。\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: oura-daily-activity Pipeline を作成**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines create oura-daily-activity \\\n  --r2-data-catalog \\\n  --bucket data-lake \\\n  --namespace oura \\\n  --table daily_activity \\\n  --catalog-token <CATALOG_TOKEN> \\\n  --compression zstd\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: oura-daily-readiness Pipeline を作成**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines create oura-daily-readiness \\\n  --r2-data-catalog \\\n  --bucket data-lake \\\n  --namespace oura \\\n  --table daily_readiness \\\n  --catalog-token <CATALOG_TOKEN> \\\n  --compression zstd\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: oura-heart-rate Pipeline を作成**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines create oura-heart-rate \\\n  --r2-data-catalog \\\n  --bucket data-lake \\\n  --namespace oura \\\n  --table heart_rate \\\n  --catalog-token <CATALOG_TOKEN> \\\n  --compression zstd\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Pipeline 一覧を確認**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines list\n```\n\n4本の Pipeline が表示されることを確認。\n\n### Task 3: D1 マイグレーション\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0005_export_sync_state.sql`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: マイグレーションファイルを作成**\n\n```sql\n-- oura-export: D1 → R2 Iceberg エクスポートの最終実行日を管理\n-- 既存の id='oura' (API 取り込み日) とは別の用途\nINSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)\nVALUES ('oura-export', 'oura', NULL);\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: リモート D1 にマイグレーションを適用**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion\nnpx wrangler d1 migrations apply raw --remote\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: マイグレーションを確認**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler d1 execute raw --remote --command \"SELECT * FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'\"\n```\n\n`id='oura-export'`, `last_sync_at=NULL` の行が存在することを確認。\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add infrastructure/d1/migrations/0005_export_sync_state.sql\ngit commit -m \"feat: Add D1 migration for oura-export sync state\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Chunk 2: Worker プロジェクトのスキャフォールディング\n\n### Task 4: プロジェクト初期化\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/package.json`\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/tsconfig.json`\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/biome.json`\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/wrangler.jsonc`\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/src/types.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: ディレクトリ作成**\n\n```bash\nmkdir -p d1-to-iceberg/src\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: package.json を作成**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"d1-to-iceberg\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"wrangler dev\",\n    \"deploy\": \"wrangler deploy --minify\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\",\n    \"lint\": \"biome lint .\",\n    \"lint:fix\": \"biome lint --write .\",\n    \"format\": \"biome format --write .\",\n    \"check\": \"biome check .\",\n    \"check:fix\": \"biome check --write .\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@biomejs/biome\": \"^2.3.13\",\n    \"@cloudflare/workers-types\": \"^4.20250124.0\",\n    \"typescript\": \"^5.7.0\",\n    \"wrangler\": \"^4.4.0\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: tsconfig.json を作成**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"compilerOptions\": {\n    \"target\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"module\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"moduleResolution\": \"Bundler\",\n    \"strict\": true,\n    \"skipLibCheck\": true,\n    \"lib\": [\"ESNext\"],\n    \"types\": [\"@cloudflare/workers-types/experimental\"]\n  },\n  \"include\": [\"src/**/*.ts\"],\n  \"exclude\": [\"node_modules\"]\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: biome.json を作成**\n\ningestion プロジェクトの biome.json をコピー（jsx 関連は不要なので同一で問題なし）:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"https://biomejs.dev/schemas/2.3.13/schema.json\",\n  \"vcs\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"clientKind\": \"git\",\n    \"useIgnoreFile\": true\n  },\n  \"files\": {\n    \"ignoreUnknown\": true,\n    \"includes\": [\"**\", \"!**/node_modules\", \"!**/dist\", \"!**/.wrangler\", \"!**/wrangler-env.d.ts\"]\n  },\n  \"formatter\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"indentStyle\": \"space\",\n    \"indentWidth\": 2,\n    \"lineWidth\": 100\n  },\n  \"linter\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"rules\": {\n      \"recommended\": true,\n      \"suspicious\": {\n        \"noExplicitAny\": \"warn\"\n      },\n      \"complexity\": {\n        \"noForEach\": \"off\"\n      }\n    }\n  },\n  \"javascript\": {\n    \"formatter\": {\n      \"quoteStyle\": \"double\",\n      \"semicolons\": \"always\",\n      \"trailingCommas\": \"es5\"\n    }\n  },\n  \"assist\": { \"actions\": { \"source\": { \"organizeImports\": \"on\" } } }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: wrangler.jsonc を作成**\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json\",\n  \"name\": \"d1-to-iceberg\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-03-01\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"observability\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"head_sampling_rate\": 1\n  },\n\n  // Cron: 毎日 UTC 01:00 (JST 10:00)\n  \"triggers\": {\n    \"crons\": [\"0 1 * * *\"]\n  },\n\n  // D1 — 既存の raw データベースに接続\n  \"d1_databases\": [\n    {\n      \"binding\": \"DB\",\n      \"database_name\": \"raw\",\n      \"database_id\": \"30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026\"\n    }\n  ],\n\n  // Pipelines — 4本の Pipeline binding\n  \"pipelines\": [\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-sleep\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_SLEEP\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-activity\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_ACTIVITY\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-daily-readiness\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_READINESS\" },\n    { \"pipeline\": \"oura-heart-rate\", \"binding\": \"PIPELINE_HEART_RATE\" }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: src/types.ts を作成**\n\n```typescript\nexport type Env = {\n  DB: D1Database;\n  PIPELINE_SLEEP: Pipeline;\n  PIPELINE_ACTIVITY: Pipeline;\n  PIPELINE_READINESS: Pipeline;\n  PIPELINE_HEART_RATE: Pipeline;\n};\n```\n\n注意: `Pipeline` 型は `@cloudflare/workers-types` に含まれている。もし型が見つからない場合は Pipelines のドキュメントを確認して調整する。\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: 依存関係をインストール**\n\n```bash\ncd d1-to-iceberg\npnpm install\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 8: 型チェック**\n\n```bash\npnpm typecheck\n```\n\n`types.ts` のみなのでエラーなしで通るはず。`Pipeline` 型がない場合は `any` で仮置きし、後で修正。\n\n- [ ] **Step 9: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add d1-to-iceberg/\ngit commit -m \"feat: Scaffold d1-to-iceberg Worker project\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Chunk 3: エクスポートロジックの実装\n\n### Task 5: エクスポート関数の実装\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/src/export.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: export.ts を作成**\n\nエクスポートロジック。各テーブルの差分データを D1 から取得し、Pipeline に送信する。\n\n```typescript\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 10000;\n\nasync function getLastExportDate(db: D1Database): Promise<string | null> {\n  const row = await db\n    .prepare(\"SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();\n  return row?.last_sync_at ?? null;\n}\n\nasync function updateLastExportDate(db: D1Database, date: string): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .prepare(\"UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ? WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .bind(date)\n    .run();\n}\n\nasync function exportTable(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  table: string,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  const whereClause = lastExportDate\n    ? `WHERE day > '${lastExportDate}'`\n    : \"\";\n  const sql = `SELECT * FROM ${table} ${whereClause} ORDER BY day ASC`;\n  const result = await db.prepare(sql).all();\n  const rows = result.results;\n\n  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  await pipeline.send(rows);\n  return rows.length;\n}\n\nasync function exportHeartRate(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  let totalSent = 0;\n  let lastDay = lastExportDate ?? \"\";\n  let lastTimestamp = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    let sql: string;\n    if (lastDay === \"\" && lastTimestamp === \"\") {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    } else if (lastTimestamp === \"\") {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > '${lastDay}' ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    } else {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > '${lastDay}') OR (day = '${lastDay}' AND timestamp > '${lastTimestamp}') ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    }\n\n    const result = await db.prepare(sql).all();\n    const rows = result.results;\n\n    if (rows.length === 0) break;\n\n    await pipeline.send(rows);\n    totalSent += rows.length;\n\n    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };\n    lastDay = lastRow.day;\n    lastTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;\n\n    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;\n  }\n\n  return totalSent;\n}\n\nexport async function runExport(env: Env): Promise<string> {\n  const lastExportDate = await getLastExportDate(env.DB);\n  const logs: string[] = [];\n\n  const sleepCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_SLEEP,\n    \"oura_daily_sleep\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_sleep: ${sleepCount} rows`);\n\n  const activityCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_ACTIVITY,\n    \"oura_daily_activity\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_activity: ${activityCount} rows`);\n\n  const readinessCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_READINESS,\n    \"oura_daily_readiness\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_readiness: ${readinessCount} rows`);\n\n  const heartRateCount = await exportHeartRate(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_HEART_RATE,\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`heart_rate: ${heartRateCount} rows`);\n\n  // 今日の日付でエクスポート日を更新\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n  await updateLastExportDate(env.DB, today);\n\n  const summary = `Export complete: ${logs.join(\", \")}`;\n  console.log(summary);\n  return summary;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: 型チェック**\n\n```bash\ncd d1-to-iceberg\npnpm typecheck\n```\n\n`Pipeline` 型の `send()` メソッドの引数型を確認。エラーがあれば修正。\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add d1-to-iceberg/src/export.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: Implement D1 to Pipelines export logic\"\n```\n\n### Task 6: Cron ハンドラーの実装\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `d1-to-iceberg/src/index.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: index.ts を作成**\n\n```typescript\nimport { runExport } from \"./export\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nexport default {\n  async scheduled(_event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {\n    ctx.waitUntil(runExport(env));\n  },\n\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n\n    if (url.pathname === \"/health\") {\n      return new Response(\"OK\");\n    }\n\n    // 手動トリガー用 (デバッグ)\n    if (url.pathname === \"/export\" && request.method === \"POST\") {\n      const result = await runExport(env);\n      return new Response(result);\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Biome チェック**\n\n```bash\ncd d1-to-iceberg\npnpm check\n```\n\nエラーがあれば `pnpm check:fix` で修正。\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: 型チェック**\n\n```bash\npnpm typecheck\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add d1-to-iceberg/src/index.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: Add Cron handler and manual trigger endpoint\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Chunk 4: デプロイとテスト\n\n### Task 7: デプロイ\n\n**Files:** なし\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: デプロイ**\n\n```bash\ncd d1-to-iceberg\nnpx wrangler deploy\n```\n\nデプロイ成功を確認。Pipeline binding のエラーが出る場合は、Pipeline 名が `wrangler.jsonc` と一致しているか確認。\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: ヘルスチェック**\n\n```bash\ncurl https://d1-to-iceberg.ta93abe.workers.dev/health\n```\n\n`OK` が返ることを確認。\n\n### Task 8: 手動エクスポートテスト\n\n**Files:** なし\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: 手動エクスポートを実行**\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST https://d1-to-iceberg.ta93abe.workers.dev/export\n```\n\nレスポンスに各テーブルの送信行数が含まれることを確認:\n```\nExport complete: daily_sleep: N rows, daily_activity: N rows, daily_readiness: N rows, heart_rate: N rows\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: sync_state が更新されたことを確認**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion\nnpx wrangler d1 execute raw --remote --command \"SELECT * FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'\"\n```\n\n`last_sync_at` に今日の日付が入っていることを確認。\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: R2 Data Catalog にデータが書き込まれたことを確認**\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler r2 object list data-lake --prefix oura/\n```\n\nParquet ファイルが生成されていることを確認。\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add d1-to-iceberg/\ngit commit -m \"feat: Complete d1-to-iceberg Worker with Pipelines export\"\n```\n\n### Task 9: DuckDB での確認\n\n**Files:** なし\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: DuckDB で Iceberg テーブルにクエリ**\n\nローカルの DuckDB で実行:\n\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg;\nLOAD iceberg;\n\nCREATE SECRET r2_catalog (\n  TYPE ICEBERG,\n  TOKEN '<R2_API_TOKEN>'\n);\n\nATTACH 'data-lake' AS oura_lake (\n  TYPE ICEBERG,\n  ENDPOINT '<CATALOG_URI>'\n);\n\nSELECT * FROM oura_lake.oura.daily_sleep ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT I now have enough information to do a thorough review. Here is the complete assessment:

---

## Review Results

The plan is well-structured and covers all spec components. However, there are **5 specific issues** that must be addressed before implementation.

---

### Issue 1: SQL Injection in `exportTable` (Critical)

**File:** `d1-to-iceberg/src/export.ts`, the `exportTable` function

The `WHERE` clause for the daily tables uses string interpolation instead of a bound parameter:

```typescript
// PLAN (unsafe)
const whereClause = lastExportDate
  ? `WHERE day > '${lastExportDate}'`
  : "";
const sql = `SELECT * FROM ${table} ${whereClause} ORDER BY day ASC`;
const result = await db.prepare(sql).all();
```

`lastExportDate` comes from the database (`sync_state.last_sync_at`) so in this specific flow it is not user-supplied input, which reduces practical risk. However, the `table` parameter is also interpolated directly with no validation, and `lastExportDate` still violates the project's own coding conventions (`CLAUDE.md` shows `.bind()` usage as the correct pattern). The fix is to use `.bind()`:

```typescript
const sql = lastExportDate
  ? `SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC`
  : `SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY day ASC`;
const result = lastExportDate
  ? await db.prepare(sql).bind(lastExportDate).all()
  : await db.prepare(sql).all();
```

The same string interpolation issue applies to `exportHeartRate` — all three SQL branches inline `lastDay` and `lastTimestamp` directly. All of these must use `.bind()`. The cursor-based pagination query with `(day > ?) OR (day = ? AND timestamp > ?)` requires multiple bind positions and is supported by D1's `.bind(a, b, c)` signature.

---

### Issue 2: `exportHeartRate` Pagination Uses Wrong Starting Cursor (Moderate)

The function resets the pagination cursor to `lastExportDate` on the first page, but for subsequent pages it uses `lastDay`/`lastTimestamp` from the last row. The problem is in the initial condition:

```typescript
// PLAN
let lastDay = lastExportDate ?? "";
let lastTimestamp = "";

// First-page condition:
if (lastDay === "" && lastTimestamp === "") {
  // full scan — correct only when lastExportDate is null
} else if (lastTimestamp === "") {
  // WHERE day > lastDay — this is correct for page 1
} else {
  // cursor condition — this applies from page 2 onward
}
```

On the very first iteration when `lastExportDate` is set, `lastTimestamp` is `""`, so it enters the second branch: `WHERE day > '${lastDay}'`. That is correct for day-level filtering (mirrors `exportTable`). From page 2 onward, `lastTimestamp` becomes non-empty, so it enters the cursor branch. This logic is actually sound, but the three-branch condition is fragile and hard to read. It also means the first page of a non-null `lastExportDate` run will miss records on the boundary day (same as `exportTable`, which also uses `>` not `>=`). This is consistent with spec intent (last export date was already exported), so the logic is acceptable but should be documented.

The real concern is: once `lastDay` and `lastTimestamp` are set from the last row of a page, the third branch correctly handles the cursor. No off-by-one in normal operation. **This is acceptable as written** provided the SQL injection fix is also applied.

---

### Issue 3: Spec File Structure vs. Plan File Structure Mismatch (Minor but Misleading)

The spec (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-11-d1-to-r2-iceberg-design.md`, line 199–208) shows this file layout:

```
d1-to-iceberg/
├── src/
│   ├── index.ts   # Cron handler + エクスポートロジック  ← single file
│   └── types.ts
```

The spec comment says "Cron handler + エクスポートロジック" in `index.ts` — implying a single source file. The plan correctly splits this into `export.ts` + `index.ts`, which is a better design and consistent with `ingestion/src/services/` separation patterns. This deviation from the spec is an improvement, not a defect. No change needed, but the implementer should be aware the spec diagram is simplified.

---

### Issue 4: D1 Migration Applied from Wrong Directory (Minor)

**Plan Task 3, Step 2:**

```bash
cd ingestion
npx wrangler d1 migrations apply raw --remote
```

This is correct — D1 migrations must be run from the `ingestion/` directory where `wrangler.jsonc` references the `raw` database. However, **Step 3's verification command** should also be run from the `ingestion/` directory:

```bash
# Step 3 is missing the cd ingestion prefix
npx wrangler d1 execute raw --remote --command "SELECT * FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'"
```

Task 8, Step 2 has the same issue — the command appears without a preceding `cd ingestion`, though the context from the previous step may carry over in a shell session. Making the directory explicit in each step is safer for agentic execution.

---

### Issue 5: `biome.json` Schema Version Inconsistency (Trivial)

The plan's `biome.json` references schema version `2.3.13`, which does not exist as of August 2025 (current Biome stable is `1.9.x`). The ingestion project's `biome.json` (which the plan instructs to copy) also says `2.3.13`. Since the plan says "copy ingestion's biome.json," and the ingestion file already has this version, the plan is internally consistent — but if `@biomejs/biome: "^2.3.13"` is not a real published version, `pnpm install` will fail. The implementer should verify the actual latest Biome version and use that in both `package.json` and `biome.json`.

---

### Items Confirmed Correct

- All 4 spec components (Worker, R2 Data Catalog, Pipelines, DuckDB) are covered with actionable steps
- `wrangler.jsonc` pipeline binding names exactly match the spec
- D1 `database_id` (`30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026`) matches the existing `ingestion/wrangler.jsonc`
- Migration file number `0005` is correct (0004 is the last existing migration)
- Cron expression `0 1 * * *` matches spec's UTC 01:00
- `sync_state` INSERT uses correct columns (`id`, `data_source_id`, `last_sync_at`) consistent with the schema in `0001_initial_schema.sql`
- `updateLastExportDate` correctly uses `.bind(date)` — safe
- `getLastExportDate` uses `.first<>()` pattern consistent with D1 API
- Compaction and snapshot expiration commands match the spec exactly
- DuckDB `ATTACH` syntax matches the spec
- Error handling strategy (do not update `sync_state` on failure) aligns with spec's stated approach

---

### Summary of Required Fixes Before Implementation

1. **Critical:** In `export.ts`, replace all string-interpolated SQL values in `exportTable` and `exportHeartRate` with `.bind()` parameters. The `table` parameter in `exportTable` must be validated against an allowlist (`['oura_daily_sleep', 'oura_daily_activity', 'oura_daily_readiness']`) since it cannot be bound as a parameter in SQLite.

2. **Minor:** Add explicit `cd ingestion` before the D1 verification command in Task 3 Step 3, and before Task 8 Step 2.

3. **Trivial:** Verify `@biomejs/biome` version `2.3.13` is a real published package before running `pnpm install`.

The plan is **not approved in its current form** due to Issue 1. Once the SQL in `exportTable` and `exportHeartRate` is rewritten to use `.bind()` with a table name allowlist, the plan is otherwise sound and complete.

WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .bind(date)\n    .run();\n}\n\nasync function exportTable(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  table: string,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  const whereClause = lastExportDate\n    ? `WHERE day > '${lastExportDate}'`\n    : \"\";\n  const sql = `SELECT * FROM ${table} ${whereClause} ORDER BY day ASC`;\n  const result = await db.prepare(sql).all();\n  const rows = result.results;\n\n  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  await pipeline.send(rows);\n  return rows.length;\n}\n\nasync function exportHeartRate(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  let totalSent = 0;\n  let lastDay = lastExportDate ?? \"\";\n  let lastTimestamp = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    let sql: string;\n    if (lastDay === \"\" && lastTimestamp === \"\") {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    } else if (lastTimestamp === \"\") {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > '${lastDay}' ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    } else {\n      sql = `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > '${lastDay}') OR (day = '${lastDay}' AND timestamp > '${lastTimestamp}') ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`;\n    }\n\n    const result = await db.prepare(sql).all();\n    const rows = result.results;\n\n    if (rows.length === 0) break;\n\n    await pipeline.send(rows);\n    totalSent += rows.length;\n\n    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };\n    lastDay = lastRow.day;\n    lastTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;\n\n    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;\n  }\n\n  return totalSent;\n}",
  "new_string": "```typescript\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 10000;\n\n// テーブル名の allowlist（SQL injection 防止）\nconst ALLOWED_TABLES = [\n  \"oura_daily_sleep\",\n  \"oura_daily_activity\",\n  \"oura_daily_readiness\",\n] as const;\ntype AllowedTable = (typeof ALLOWED_TABLES)[number];\n\nfunction isAllowedTable(table: string): table is AllowedTable {\n  return (ALLOWED_TABLES as readonly string[]).includes(table);\n}\n\nasync function getLastExportDate(db: D1Database): Promise<string | null> {\n  const row = await db\n    .prepare(\"SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();\n  return row?.last_sync_at ?? null;\n}\n\nasync function updateLastExportDate(db: D1Database, date: string): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .prepare(\"UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ? WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .bind(date)\n    .run();\n}\n\nasync function exportTable(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  table: string,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  if (!isAllowedTable(table)) {\n    throw new Error(`Table not allowed: ${table}`);\n  }\n\n  // table 名は allowlist で検証済み、day は .bind() でパラメータ化\n  const sql = lastExportDate\n    ? `SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC`\n    : `SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY day ASC`;\n  const result = lastExportDate\n    ? await db.prepare(sql).bind(lastExportDate).all()\n    : await db.prepare(sql).all();\n  const rows = result.results;\n\n  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  await pipeline.send(rows);\n  return rows.length;\n}\n\nasync function exportHeartRate(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  let totalSent = 0;\n  let cursorDay = lastExportDate ?? \"\";\n  let cursorTimestamp = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    let result: D1Result<Record<string, unknown>>;\n\n    if (cursorDay === \"\" && cursorTimestamp === \"\") {\n      // 初回エクスポート: 全データ\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .all();\n    } else if (cursorTimestamp === \"\") {\n      // 差分エクスポート: 最初のページ（day のみでフィルタ）\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .bind(cursorDay)\n        .all();\n    } else {\n      // 2ページ目以降: day + timestamp でカーソル\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > ?) OR (day = ? AND timestamp > ?) ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .bind(cursorDay, cursorDay, cursorTimestamp)\n        .all();\n    }\n\n    const rows = result.results;\n    if (rows.length === 0) break;\n\n    await pipeline.send(rows);\n    totalSent += rows.length;\n\n    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };\n    cursorDay = lastRow.day;\n    cursorTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;\n\n    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;\n  }\n\n  return totalSent;\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/d1-to-iceberg/src/export.ts",
  "content": "import type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 10000;\n\nconst ALLOWED_TABLES = [\n  \"oura_daily_sleep\",\n  \"oura_daily_activity\",\n  \"oura_daily_readiness\",\n] as const;\ntype AllowedTable = (typeof ALLOWED_TABLES)[number];\n\nfunction isAllowedTable(table: string): table is AllowedTable {\n  return (ALLOWED_TABLES as readonly string[]).includes(table);\n}\n\nasync function getLastExportDate(db: D1Database): Promise<string | null> {\n  const row = await db\n    .prepare(\"SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();\n  return row?.last_sync_at ?? null;\n}\n\nasync function updateLastExportDate(db: D1Database, date: string): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .prepare(\"UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ? WHERE id = 'oura-export'\")\n    .bind(date)\n    .run();\n}\n\nasync function exportTable(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  table: string,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  if (!isAllowedTable(table)) {\n    throw new Error(`Table not allowed: ${table}`);\n  }\n\n  const sql = lastExportDate\n    ? `SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC`\n    : `SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY day ASC`;\n  const result = lastExportDate\n    ? await db.prepare(sql).bind(lastExportDate).all()\n    : await db.prepare(sql).all();\n  const rows = result.results;\n\n  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  await pipeline.send(rows);\n  return rows.length;\n}\n\nasync function exportHeartRate(\n  db: D1Database,\n  pipeline: Pipeline,\n  lastExportDate: string | null\n): Promise<number> {\n  let totalSent = 0;\n  let cursorDay = lastExportDate ?? \"\";\n  let cursorTimestamp = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    let result: D1Result<Record<string, unknown>>;\n\n    if (cursorDay === \"\" && cursorTimestamp === \"\") {\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .all();\n    } else if (cursorTimestamp === \"\") {\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .bind(cursorDay)\n        .all();\n    } else {\n      result = await db\n        .prepare(\n          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > ?) OR (day = ? AND timestamp > ?) ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`\n        )\n        .bind(cursorDay, cursorDay, cursorTimestamp)\n        .all();\n    }\n\n    const rows = result.results;\n    if (rows.length === 0) break;\n\n    await pipeline.send(rows);\n    totalSent += rows.length;\n\n    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };\n    cursorDay = lastRow.day;\n    cursorTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;\n\n    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;\n  }\n\n  return totalSent;\n}\n\nexport async function runExport(env: Env): Promise<string> {\n  const lastExportDate = await getLastExportDate(env.DB);\n  const logs: string[] = [];\n\n  const sleepCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_SLEEP,\n    \"oura_daily_sleep\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_sleep: ${sleepCount} rows`);\n\n  const activityCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_ACTIVITY,\n    \"oura_daily_activity\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_activity: ${activityCount} rows`);\n\n  const readinessCount = await exportTable(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_READINESS,\n    \"oura_daily_readiness\",\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`daily_readiness: ${readinessCount} rows`);\n\n  const heartRateCount = await exportHeartRate(\n    env.DB,\n    env.PIPELINE_HEART_RATE,\n    lastExportDate\n  );\n  logs.push(`heart_rate: ${heartRateCount} rows`);\n\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n  await updateLastExportDate(env.DB, today);\n\n  const summary = `Export complete: ${logs.join(\", \")}`;\n  console.log(summary);\n  return summary;\n}\n"
}

}

declare module "cloudflare:pipelines" {
  export abstract class PipelineTransformationEntrypoint<
    Env = unknown,
    I extends PipelineRecord = PipelineRecord,
    O extends PipelineRecord = PipelineRecord,
  > {
    protected env: Env;
    protected ctx: ExecutionContext;
    constructor(ctx: ExecutionContext, env: Env);
    /**
     * run receives an array of PipelineRecord which can be
     * transformed and returned to the pipeline
     * @param records Incoming records from the pipeline to be transformed
     * @param metadata Information about the specific pipeline calling the transformation entrypoint
     * @returns A promise containing the transformed PipelineRecord array
     */
    public run(records: I[], metadata: PipelineBatchMetadata): Promise<O[]>;
  }
  export type PipelineRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
  export type PipelineBatchMetadata = {
    pipelineId: string;
    pipelineName: string;
  };
  export interface Pipeline<T extends PipelineRecord = PipelineRecord> {
    /**
     * The Pipeline interface represents the type of a binding to a Pipeline
     *
     * @param records The records to send to the pipeline
     */
    send(records: T[]): Promise<void>;
  }
}

先ほどの R2 Data Catalog の出力を確認しました：
- **Catalog URI**: `https://catalog.cloudflarestorage.com/b0047256d1afc1be1df08289ee3be552/data-lake`
- **Warehouse**: `b0047256d1afc1be1df08289ee3be552_data-lake`

次は **Pipelines の作成** です。Cloudflare ダッシュボードで R2 API トークンを作成する必要があります：

1. **R2** > **Manage R2 API Tokens** > **Create API Token**
2. Permission: **Admin Read & Write**
3. トークン値を控える

トークンを作成したら教えてください。それを使って4本の Pipeline を作成します。

```
     1→import type { Pipeline } from "cloudflare:pipelines";
     2→import type { Env } from "./types";
     3→
     4→const PAGE_SIZE = 10000;
     5→
     6→const ALLOWED_TABLES = ["oura_daily_sleep", "oura_daily_activity", "oura_daily_readiness"] as const;
     7→type AllowedTable = (typeof ALLOWED_TABLES)[number];
     8→
     9→function isAllowedTable(table: string): table is AllowedTable {
    10→  return (ALLOWED_TABLES as readonly string[]).includes(table);
    11→}
    12→
    13→async function getLastExportDate(db: D1Database): Promise<string | null> {
    14→  const row = await db
    15→    .prepare("SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'")
    16→    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();
    17→  return row?.last_sync_at ?? null;
    18→}
    19→
    20→async function updateLastExportDate(db: D1Database, date: string): Promise<void> {
    21→  await db
    22→    .prepare("UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ? WHERE id = 'oura-export'")
    23→    .bind(date)
    24→    .run();
    25→}
    26→
    27→async function exportTable(
    28→  db: D1Database,
    29→  pipeline: Pipeline,
    30→  table: string,
    31→  lastExportDate: string | null
    32→): Promise<number> {
    33→  if (!isAllowedTable(table)) {
    34→    throw new Error(`Table not allowed: ${table}`);
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  const sql = lastExportDate
    38→    ? `SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC`
    39→    : `SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY day ASC`;
    40→  const result = lastExportDate
    41→    ? await db.prepare(sql).bind(lastExportDate).all()
    42→    : await db.prepare(sql).all();
    43→  const rows = result.results;
    44→
    45→  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;
    46→
    47→  await pipeline.send(rows);
    48→  return rows.length;
    49→}
    50→
    51→async function exportHeartRate(
    52→  db: D1Database,
    53→  pipeline: Pipeline,
    54→  lastExportDate: string | null
    55→): Promise<number> {
    56→  let totalSent = 0;
    57→  let cursorDay = lastExportDate ?? "";
    58→  let cursorTimestamp = "";
    59→
    60→  while (true) {
    61→    let result: D1Result<Record<string, unknown>>;
    62→
    63→    if (cursorDay === "" && cursorTimestamp === "") {
    64→      result = await db
    65→        .prepare(`SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`)
    66→        .all();
    67→    } else if (cursorTimestamp === "") {
    68→      result = await db
    69→        .prepare(
    70→          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`
    71→        )
    72→        .bind(cursorDay)
    73→        .all();
    74→    } else {
    75→      result = await db
    76→        .prepare(
    77→          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > ?) OR (day = ? AND timestamp > ?) ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`
    78→        )
    79→        .bind(cursorDay, cursorDay, cursorTimestamp)
    80→        .all();
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    const rows = result.results;
    84→    if (rows.length === 0) break;
    85→
    86→    await pipeline.send(rows);
    87→    totalSent += rows.length;
    88→
    89→    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };
    90→    cursorDay = lastRow.day;
    91→    cursorTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;
    92→
    93→    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  return totalSent;
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runExport(env: Env): Promise<string> {
   100→  const lastExportDate = await getLastExportDate(env.DB);
   101→  const logs: string[] = [];
   102→
   103→  const sleepCount = await exportTable(
   104→    env.DB,
   105→    env.PIPELINE_SLEEP,
   106→    "oura_daily_sleep",
   107→    lastExportDate
   108→  );
   109→  logs.push(`daily_sleep: ${sleepCount} rows`);
   110→
   111→  const activityCount = await exportTable(
   112→    env.DB,
   113→    env.PIPELINE_ACTIVITY,
   114→    "oura_daily_activity",
   115→    lastExportDate
   116→  );
   117→  logs.push(`daily_activity: ${activityCount} rows`);
   118→
   119→  const readinessCount = await exportTable(
   120→    env.DB,
   121→    env.PIPELINE_READINESS,
   122→    "oura_daily_readiness",
   123→    lastExportDate
   124→  );
   125→  logs.push(`daily_readiness: ${readinessCount} rows`);
   126→
   127→  const heartRateCount = await exportHeartRate(env.DB, env.PIPELINE_HEART_RATE, lastExportDate);
   128→  logs.push(`heart_rate: ${heartRateCount} rows`);
   129→
   130→  const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
   131→  await updateLastExportDate(env.DB, today);
   132→
   133→  const summary = `Export complete: ${logs.join(", ")}`;
   134→  console.log(summary);
   135→  return summary;
   136→}
   137→
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`loaded_at`をPipeline送信前に付与することで、「いつD1からIcebergにエクスポートされたか」を記録します。これはデータリネージの基本で、後からデータの鮮度を判断するのに役立ちます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

  "new_string": "  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  const loadedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n  const enriched = rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, loaded_at: loadedAt }));\n  await pipeline.send(enriched);\n  return enriched.length;\n}"
}

     1→import { runExport } from "./export";
     2→import type { Env } from "./types";
     3→
     4→export default {
     5→  async scheduled(_event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
     6→    ctx.waitUntil(runExport(env));
     7→  },
     8→
     9→  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    10→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    11→
    12→    if (url.pathname === "/health") {
    13→      return new Response("OK");
    14→    }
    15→
    16→    // 手動トリガー用 (デバッグ)
    17→    if (url.pathname === "/export" && request.method === "POST") {
    18→      const result = await runExport(env);
    19→      return new Response(result);
    20→    }
    21→
    22→    return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    23→  },
    24→};
    25→
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`fetch` ハンドラでは `await runExport(env)` で直接待っています。heart_rate テーブルが数万行あるとCPU時間制限（有料プラン30秒）を超える可能性があります。`scheduled` ハンドラは `ctx.waitUntil()` でバックグラウンド実行するため制限が緩和されます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

エクスポート成功です！

- **daily_sleep**: 1,720行
- **daily_activity**: 1,778行
- **daily_readiness**: 1,720行
- **heart_rate**: 821,530行

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
82万行のheart_rateデータもページネーション（10,000行/バッチ）で問題なく処理できました。Cloudflare Pipelines の `send()` がバッファリングしてくれるため、大量データでもWorker CPU時間内に収まります。初回エクスポートは全データなので時間がかかりましたが、次回以降は差分のみなので高速になります。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

全4テーブルのデータがR2 Data Catalogに送られました。次のステップとして：

1. **R2にデータが到着しているか確認** — Pipelineはバッファリングしてからバッチで書き込むため、数分後にR2バケットにParquetファイルが現れるはずです
2. **コミット＆PR作成** — `loaded_at`追加とデプロイ済みの変更をコミット

# Cloudflare Pipelines Observability Metrics

## Operator Metrics
Dataset: `pipelinesOperatorAdaptiveGroups`

| Metric | Field Name | Description |
|--------|-----------|-------------|
| Bytes In | bytesIn | "Total number of bytes read by the pipeline" |
| Records In | recordsIn | "Total number of records read by the pipeline" |
| Decode Errors | decodeErrors | "Number of messages that could not be deserialized in the stream schema" |

**Dimensions:** pipelineId, streamId, datetime, date, datetimeHour

---

## Sink Metrics
Dataset: `pipelinesSinkAdaptiveGroups`

| Metric | Field Name | Description |
|--------|-----------|-------------|
| Bytes Written | bytesWritten | "Total number of bytes written to the sink, after compression" |
| Records Written | recordsWritten | "Total number of records written to the sink" |
| Files Written | filesWritten | "Number of files written to the sink" |
| Row Groups Written | rowGroupsWritten | "Number of row groups written (for Parquet files)" |
| Uncompressed Bytes Written | uncompressedBytesWritten | Bytes before compression is applied |

**Dimensions:** pipelineId, sinkId, datetime, date, datetimeHour

---

## User Error Metrics
Dataset: `pipelinesUserErrorsAdaptiveGroups`

| Metric | Field Name | Description |
|--------|-----------|-------------|
| Count | count | "Number of events that failed validation" |

**Error Types:** missing_field, type_mismatch, parse_failure, null_value

**Dimensions:** pipelineId, errorFamily, errorType, date, datetime, datetimeHour, datetimeMinute
```

> AGENT

[TA-395](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-395/feat-cloudflare-pipelines-observability-メトリクスを-r2-に保存) を Backlog に作成しました。

3つのデータセットを網羅しています：
- **Operator Metrics** — 読み取りバイト数・レコード数・デコードエラー
- **Sink Metrics** — 書き込みバイト数・レコード数・ファイル数・Row Group数
- **User Error Metrics** — バリデーション失敗数（型不一致・null値など）

GraphQL Analytics API で取得して R2 に保存する形になります。

R2 APIトークンはS3の Access Key ID ではありません。R2のS3互換APIには専用のAccess Key IDとSecret Access Keyのペアが必要です。

```
# Querying R2 Data Catalog via Iceberg REST API

Based on the provided documentation, specific details about REST API endpoint formats and direct query methods are **not included** in this getting started guide.

## What the Guide Covers

The documentation demonstrates accessing the catalog through **PyIceberg**, a Python client library. The setup requires:

**Authentication:**
- "Iceberg clients (including PyIceberg) must authenticate to the catalog with an R2 API token that has both R2 and catalog permissions."

**Connection Parameters:**
The code example shows connecting via:
```python
catalog = RestCatalog(
    name="my_catalog",
    warehouse=WAREHOUSE,
    uri=CATALOG_URI,
    token=TOKEN,
)
```

Where you obtain the `CATALOG_URI` and `WAREHOUSE` name from your bucket's Data Catalog settings.

## What's Missing

This guide focuses on setup and basic operations (creating tables, appending data, scanning tables) but doesn't specify:
- Direct REST endpoint URL patterns
- Raw API calls for listing tables/namespaces
- Query syntax beyond PyIceberg methods

For detailed API specifications and alternative query engines, the guide references "Connect to Iceberg engines" documentation, which likely contains engine-specific configuration examples.
```

> AGENT

データは到着していますが、問題があります。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Cloudflare Pipelines のデフォルト動作では、送信したJSONオブジェクトがそのまま**1つの `value` カラム（JSON文字列）**にフラット化されずに格納されています。カラムは `__ingest_ts`（Pipeline側のタイムスタンプ）と `value`（元データのJSON文字列）の2つだけです。

各フィールド（`day`, `score`, `loaded_at`等）を個別カラムとして保存するには、**Pipelineの SQL でカラムを展開する**必要があります。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

現状、データは `value` カラムにJSON文字列として格納されています。個別カラムとして保存するにはPipelineのSQLを修正する必要がありますが、まずは現状の到着を確認できました。

**確認結果：**
| テーブル | ステータス |
|---------|-----------|
| `oura.daily_sleep` | 到着済み |
| `oura.daily_activity` | 到着済み |
| `oura.daily_readiness` | 到着済み |
| `oura.heart_rate` | 到着済み |

PipelineのSQLを修正して個別カラム展開するか、それともJSON形式のまま進めますか？

> DEVELOPER

dbt duckdb でパースすればいいかな＞

> DEVELOPER

dbt duckdb でパースすればいいかな？

> AGENT

はい、それで良いと思います。Iceberg層はraw dataとしてそのまま保持し、dbt + DuckDB で `json_extract` してカラム展開する方がレイヤー分離として綺麗です。

```
D1 (raw) → Pipelines → R2 Iceberg (JSON as-is) → dbt/DuckDB (parse & model)
```

これもLinearに起票しておきますか？

> DEVELOPER

# R2 SQL

Note

R2 SQL is in **open beta**, and any developer with an [R2 subscription](/r2/pricing/) can start using it. Currently, outside of standard R2 storage and operations, you will not be billed for your use of R2 SQL. We will update [the pricing page](/r2-sql/platform/pricing) and provide at least 30 days notice before enabling billing.

Query Apache Iceberg tables managed by R2 Data Catalog using SQL.

R2 SQL is Cloudflare's serverless, distributed, analytics query engine for querying [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables stored in [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). R2 SQL is designed to efficiently query large amounts of data by automatically utilizing file pruning, Cloudflare's distributed compute, and R2 object storage.

Terminal window

```

❯ npx wrangler r2 sql query "3373912de3f5202317188ae01300bd6_data-catalog" \

"SELECT * FROM default.transactions LIMIT 10"


 ⛅️ wrangler 4.38.0

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

▲ [WARNING] 🚧 `wrangler r2 sql query` is an open-beta command. Please report any issues to https://github.com/cloudflare/workers-sdk/issues/new/choose


┌─────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┬─────────┬──────────┬─────────────────

title: Query data

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

  
## Was this helpful?

  
[ Edit](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/edit/production/src/content/docs/r2-sql/query-data.mdx) [ Issue](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/issues/new/choose) 

Copy page

# Query data

Query [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables managed by [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). R2 SQL queries can be made via [Wrangler](/workers/wrangler/) or HTTP API.

## Get your warehouse name

To query data with R2 SQL, you'll need your warehouse name associated with your [catalog](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/). To retrieve it, you can run the [r2 bucket catalog get command](/workers/wrangler/commands/#r2-bucket-catalog-get):

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog get <BUCKET_NAME>


```

Alternatively, you can find it in the dashboard by going to the **R2 object storage** page, selecting the bucket, switching to the **Settings** tab, scrolling to **R2 Data Catalog**, and finding **Warehouse name**.

## Query via Wrangler

To begin, install [npm ↗](https://docs.npmjs.com/getting-started). Then [install Wrangler, the Developer Platform CLI](/workers/wrangler/install-and-update/).

Wrangler needs an API token with permissions to access R2 Data Catalog, R2 storage, and R2 SQL to execute queries. The `r2 sql query` command looks for the token in the `WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN` environment variable.

Set up your environment:

Terminal window

```

export WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Or create a `.env` file with:

```

WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Where `YOUR_API_TOKEN` is the token you created with the [required permissions](#authentication). For more information on setting environment variables, refer to [Wrangler system environment variables](/workers/wrangler/system-environment-variables/).

To run a SQL query, run the [r2 
with the [required permissions](#authentication). For more information on setting environment variables, refer to [Wrangler system environment variables](/workers/wrangler/system-environment-variables/).

To run a SQL query, run the [r2 sql query command](/workers/wrangler/commands/#r2-sql-query):

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 sql query <WAREHOUSE> "SELECT * FROM namespace.table_name limit 10;"


```

For a full list of supported sql commands, refer to the [R2 SQL reference page](/r2-sql/sql-reference).

## Query via API

Below is an example of using R2 SQL via the REST endpoint:

Terminal window

```

curl -X POST \

  "https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/{ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/{BUCKET_NAME}" \

  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN}" \

  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \

  -d '{

    "query": "SELECT * FROM namespace.table_name limit 10;"

  }'


```

The API requires an API token with the appropriate permissions in the Authorization header. Refer to [Authentication](#authentication) for details on creating a token.

For a full list of supported sql commands, refer to the [R2 SQL reference page](/r2-sql/sql-reference).

## Authentication

To query data with R2 SQL, you must provide a Cloudflare API token with R2 SQL, R2 Data Catalog, and R2 storage permissions. R2 SQL requires these permissions to access catalog metadata and read the underlying data files stored in R2.

<title></title>
<text>

Replace `{stream-id}` with your actual stream endpoint from the pipeline setup.

## 6\. Validate data in your bucket

1. In the Cloudflare dashboard, go to the **R2 object storage** page.
2. Select your bucket: `pipelines-tutorial`.
3. You should see Iceberg metadata files and data files created by your pipeline. Note: If you aren't seeing any files in your bucket, try waiting a couple of minutes and trying again.
4. The data is organized in the Apache Iceberg format with metadata tracking table versions.

## 7\. Query your data using R2 SQL

Set up your environment to use R2 SQL:

Terminal window

```

export WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Or create a `.env` file with:

```

WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Where `YOUR_API_TOKEN` is the token you created in step 3\. For more information on setting environment variables, refer to [Wrangler system environment variables](/workers/wrangler/system-environment-variables/).

Query your data:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 sql query "YOUR_WAREHOUSE_NAME" "

SELECT

    user_id,

    event_type,

    product_id,

    amount

FROM default.ecommerce

WHERE event_type = 'purchase'

LIMIT 10"


```

Replace `YOUR_WAREHOUSE_NAME` with the warehouse name from step 2.

You can also query this table with any engine that supports Apache Iceberg. To learn more about connecting other engines to R2 Data Catalog, refer to [Connect to Iceberg engines](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/).

## Learn more

[ Managing R2 Data Catalogs ](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/) Enable or disable R2 Data Catalog on your bucket, retrieve configuration details, and authenticate your Iceberg engine. 


</text>

# Changelog

New updates and improvements at Cloudflare.

[ Subscribe to RSS ](/changelog/rss/index.xml) [ View RSS feeds ](/fundamentals/new-features/available-rss-feeds/) 

![hero image](/_astro/hero.CVYJHPAd_ZEA2nF.svg) 

[ ← Back to all posts ](/changelog/) 

## Announcing R2 SQL

Sep 25, 2025 

[ R2 SQL ](/r2-sql/) 

Today, we're launching the **open beta** for [R2 SQL](/r2-sql/): A serverless, distributed query engine that can efficiently analyze petabytes of data in [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables managed by [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog).

R2 SQL is ideal for exploring analytical and time-series data stored in R2, such as logs, events from [Pipelines](/pipelines/), or clickstream and user behavior data.

If you already have a table in R2 Data Catalog, running queries is as simple as:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 sql query YOUR_WAREHOUSE "

SELECT

    user_id,

    event_type,

    value

FROM events.user_events

WHERE event_type = 'CHANGELOG' or event_type = 'BLOG'

  AND __ingest_ts > '2025-09-24T00:00:00Z'

ORDER BY __ingest_ts DESC

LIMIT 100"


```

To get started with R2 SQL, check out our [getting started guide](/r2-sql/get-started/) or learn more about supported features in the [SQL reference](/r2-sql/sql-reference/). For a technical deep dive into how we built R2 SQL, read our [blog post ↗](https://blog.cloudflare.com/r2-sql-deep-dive/).
</text>

## R2 Data Catalog is a managed Apache Iceberg data catalog built directly into R2 buckets

Apr 10, 2025 

[ R2 ](/r2/) 

Today, we're launching [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/) in open beta, a managed Apache Iceberg catalog built directly into your [Cloudflare R2](/r2/) bucket.

If you're not already familiar with it, [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) is an open table format designed to handle large-scale analytics datasets stored in object storage, offering ACID transactions and schema evolution. R2 Data Catalog exposes a standard Iceberg REST catalog interface, so you can connect engines like [Spark](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/spark-scala/), [Snowflake](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/snowflake/), and [PyIceberg](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/pyiceberg/) to start querying your tables using the tools you already know.

To enable a data catalog on your R2 bucket, find **R2 Data Catalog** in your buckets settings in the dashboard, or run:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable my-bucket


```

And that's it. You'll get a catalog URI and warehouse you can plug into your favorite Iceberg engines.

Visit our [getting started guide](/r2/data-catalog/get-started/) for step-by-step instructions on enabling R2 Data Catalog, creating tables, and running your first queries.
</text>

R2 SQL is ideal for exploring analytical and time-series data stored in R2, such as logs, events from [Pipelines](/pipelines/), or clickstream and user behavior data.

## Was this helpful?

  
[ Edit](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/edit/production/src/content/docs/r2/data-catalog/index.mdx) [ Issue](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflare-docs/issues/new/choose)

# R2 Data Catalog

Note

R2 Data Catalog is in **public beta**, and any developer with an [R2 subscription](/r2/pricing/) can start using it. Currently, outside of standard R2 storage and operations, you will not be billed for your use of R2 Data Catalog.

R2 Data Catalog is a managed [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) data catalog built directly into your R2 bucket. It exposes a standard Iceberg REST catalog interface, so you can connect the engines you already use, like [Spark](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/spark-scala/), [Snowflake](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/snowflake/), and [PyIceberg](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/pyiceberg/).

R2 Data Catalog makes it easy to turn an R2 bucket into a data warehouse or lakehouse for a variety of analytical workloads including log analytics, business intelligence, and data pipelines. R2's zero-egress fee model means that data users and consumers can access and analyze data from different clouds, data platforms, or regions without incurring transfer costs.

To get started with R2 Data Catalog, refer to the [R2 Data Catalog: Getting started](/r2/data-catalog/get-started/).

## What is Apache Iceberg?

[Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) is an open table format designed to handle large-scale analytics datasets stored in object storage. Key features include:

* ACID transactions - Ensures reliable, concurrent reads and writes with full data integrity.
* Optimized metadata - Avoids costly full table scans by using indexed metadata for faster queries.
* Full schema evolution - Allows adding, renaming, and dele
rites with full data integrity.
* Optimized metadata - Avoids costly full table scans by using indexed metadata for faster queries.
* Full schema evolution - Allows adding, renaming, and deleting columns without rewriting data.

Iceberg is already [widely supported ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/vendors/) by engines like Apache Spark, Trino, Snowflake, DuckDB, and ClickHouse, with a fast-growing community behind it.

## Why do you need a data catalog?

Although the Iceberg data and metadata files themselves live directly in object storage (like [R2 ↗](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/)), the list of tables and pointers to the current metadata need to be tracked centrally by a data catalog.

Think of a data catalog as a library's index system. While books (your data) are physically distributed across shelves (object storage), the index provides a single source of truth about what books exist, their locations, and their latest editions. Without this index, readers (query engines) would waste time searching for books, might access outdated versions, or could accidentally shelve new books in ways that make them unfindable.

Similarly, data catalogs ensure consistent, coordinated access, which allows multiple query engines to safely read from and write to the same tables without conflicts or data corruption.

## Learn more

[ Get started ](/r2/data-catalog/get-started/) Learn how to enable the R2 Data Catalog on your bucket, load sample data, and run your first query. 

[ Managing catalogs ](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/) Enable or disable R2 Data Catalog on your bucket, retrieve configuration details, and authenticate your Iceberg engine. 

[ Connect to Iceberg engines ](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/) Find detailed setup instructions for Apache Spark and other common query engines.

> AGENT

R2 SQL の結果です：

| テーブル | D1 行数 | R2 Iceberg 行数 | 比率 |
|---------|---------|----------------|------|
| `oura.daily_sleep` | 1,720 | 3,440 | 2x |
| `oura.daily_activity` | 1,778 | 3,556 | 2x |
| `oura.daily_readiness` | 1,720 | 3,440 | 2x |
| `oura.heart_rate` | 821,530 | 1,643,060 | 2x |

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
全テーブルがちょうど2倍になっています。これはエクスポートを2回実行した（最初のタイムアウトしたcurlと2回目のcurl）ためです。`sync_state` の `last_sync_at` が1回目で更新される前に2回目が走った、もしくは両方とも全件エクスポートになったと考えられます。Iceberg は append-only なので重複が蓄積されます。dbt で `DISTINCT` や deduplication が必要です。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

---

| json(json)                                                   | Validates the provided string is JSON and returns a minified version of that JSON object.                                                      | json('{"hello":\["world" ,"there"\] }') returns {"hello":\["world","there"\]}              |
| json\_array(value1, value2, value3, ...)                     | Return a JSON array from the values.                                                                                                           | json\_array(1, 2, 3) returns \[1, 2, 3\]                                                   |
| json\_array\_length(json) \- json\_array\_length(json, path) | Return the length of the JSON array                                                                                                            | json\_array\_length('{"data":\["x", "y", "z"\]}', '$.data') returns 3                      |
| json\_extract(json, path)                                    | Extract the value(s) at the given path using $.path.to.value syntax.                                                                           | json\_extract('{"temp":"78.3", "sunset":"20:44"}', '$.temp') returns "78.3"                |
| json -> path                                                 | Extract the value(s) at the given path using path syntax and return it as JSON.                                                                |

| json\_set(json, path, value)                                 | Insert a value at the given path. Overwrites an existing value.                                                                                |                                                                                            |

Returns one of null, true, false, integer, real, text, array, or object. | json\_type('{"temperatures":\[73.6, 77.8, 80.2\]}', '$.temperatures') returns array        |
| json\_valid(json)                                            | Returns 0 (false) for invalid JSON, and 1 (true) for valid JSON.                                                                               | json\_valid({invalid:json})returns0\\                                                      |
| json\_quote(value)                                           | Converts the provided SQL value into its JSON representation.                                                                                  | json\_quote('\[1, 2, 3\]') returns \[1,2,3\]                                               |
| json\_group\_array(value)                                    | Returns the provided value(s) as a JSON array.                                                                                                 |                                                                                            |
| json\_each(value) \- json\_each(value, path)                 | Returns each element within the object as an individual row. It will only traverse the top-level object.                                       |                                                                                            |
| json\_tree(value) \- json\_tree(value, path)                 | Returns each element within the object as an individu
                                                               |                                                                                            |
| json ->> path                                                | Extract the value(s) at the given path using path syntax and return it as a SQL type.                                                          |                                                                                            |
| json\_insert(json, path, value)                              | Insert a value at the given path. Does not overwrite an existing value.                                                                        |                                                                                            |
| json\_object(label1, value1, ...)                            | Accepts pairs of (keys, values) and returns a JSON object.                                                                                     | json\_object('temp', 45, 'wind\_speed\_mph', 13) returns {"temp":45,"wind\_speed\_mph":13} |
| json\_patch(target, patch)                                   | Uses a JSON [MergePatch ↗](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc7396) approach to merge the provided patch into the target JSON object.              |                                                                                            |
| json\_remove(json, path, ...)                                | Remove the key and value at the specified path.                                                                                                | json\_remove('\[60,70,80,90\]', '$\[0\]') returns 70,80,90\]                               |
| json\_replace(json, path, value)                             | Insert a value at the given path. Overwrites an existing value, but does not create a new key if it doesn't exist.                             |                                                                                            |

<title></title>
<text>


### json\_get\_json

Retrieves a nested JSON string from a JSON string by the specified path. The value is returned as raw JSON.

```

SELECT json_get_json('{"a": {"b": {"c": 1}}}', 'a', 'b') FROM source;

'{"c": 1}'


```

### json\_as\_text

Retrieves any value from a JSON string by the specified path and returns it as a string, regardless of the original type.

```

SELECT json_as_text('{"a": {"b": 42}}', 'a', 'b') FROM source;

"42"


```

Also available via the `->>` operator:

```

SELECT '{"a": {"b": 42}}'->>'a'->>'b' FROM source;

"42"


```

### json\_length

Returns the length of a JSON object or array at the specified path. Returns `0`if the path does not exist or is not an object/array.

```

SELECT json_length('{"a": [1, 2, 3]}', 'a') FROM source;

3


```

## Json path functions

JSON functions provide basic json parsing functions using[JsonPath ↗](https://goessner.net/articles/JsonPath/), an evolving standard for querying JSON objects.

### extract\_json

Returns the JSON elements in the first argument that match the JsonPath in the second argument. The returned value is an array of json strings.

```


SELECT extract_json('{"a": 1, "b": 2, "c": 3}', '$.a') FROM source;

['1']


```

### extract\_json\_string

Returns an unescaped String for the first item matching the JsonPath, if it is a string.

```

SELECT extract_json_string('{"a": "a", "b": 2, "c": 3}', '$.a') FROM source;

'a'


```

# JSON functions

Cloudflare Pipelines provides two set of JSON functions, the first based on PostgreSQL's SQL functions and syntax, and the second based on the[JSONPath ↗](https://jsonpath.com/) standard.

## SQL functions

The SQL functions provide basic JSON parsing functions similar to those found in PostgreSQL.

### json\_contains

Returns `true` if the JSON string contains the specified key(s).

## Types of generated columns

There are two types of generated columns:

* `VIRTUAL` (default): the column is generated when read. This has the benefit of not consuming storage, but can increase compute time (and thus reduce query performance), especially for larger queries.
* `STORED`: the column is generated when the row is written. The column takes up storage space just as a regular column would, but the column does not need to be generated on every read, which can improve read query performance.

When omitted from a generated column expression, generated columns default to the `VIRTUAL` type. The `STORED` type is recommended when the generated column is compute intensive. For example, when parsing large JSON structures.

## Define a generated column

Generated columns can be defined during table creation in a `CREATE TABLE` statement or afterwards via the `ALTER TABLE` statement.

To create a table that defines a generated column, you use the `AS` keyword:

```

CREATE TABLE some_table (

    -- other columns omitted

    some_generated_column AS <function_that_generates_the_column_data>

)


```

As a concrete example, to automatically extract the `location` value from the following JSON sensor data, you can define a generated column called `location` (of type `TEXT`), based on a `raw_data` column that stores the raw representation of our JSON data.

```

{

    "measurement": {

        "temp_f": "77.4",

        "aqi": [21, 42, 58],

        "o3": [18, 500],

        "wind_mph": "13",

        "location": "US-NY"

    }

}


```

To define a generated column with the value of `$.measurement.location`, you can use the [json\_extract](/d1/sql-api/query-json/#extract-values) function to extract the value from the `raw_data`
